<a id="open-group"></a>
## The Open Group

原文：The Open Group。PDF 物理页：[9]。状态：`source-compared-revised`。

The Open Group 是全球联盟，通过技术标准与开源举措帮助实现业务目标，并在其 900 多个成员组成的多元群体中培育协作、包容与相互尊重的文化。成员包括多个行业的客户、系统和解决方案供应商、工具厂商、集成商、学界人士与顾问。

The Open Group 的使命是通过以下方式推动无边界信息流（Boundaryless Information Flow™）的实现：

- 与客户合作，捕获、理解并处理当前和新出现的需求，制定政策并分享最佳实践。
- 与供应商、联盟和标准组织合作，形成共识、促进互操作，演进并整合规范与开源技术。
- 提供全面服务，提高联盟的运营效率。
- 开发并运营业界领先的认证服务，鼓励采购经过认证的产品。

更多信息见 www.opengroup.org。

The Open Group 出版广泛的技术文档，其中多数聚焦标准与指南的开发，也包括白皮书、技术研究、认证与测试文档以及商业类出版物。详情见 www.opengroup.org/library。


### 原文内容单元

<details><summary>展开英文原文及文本块定位</summary>

**G186:p0009:c001 · PDF 9**

The Open Group

**G186:p0009:c002 · PDF 9**

The Open Group is a global consortium that enables the achievement of business objectives through technology standards and open source initiatives by fostering a culture of collaboration, inclusivity, and mutual respect among our diverse group of 900+ memberships. Our membership includes customers, systems and solutions suppliers, tool vendors, integrators, academics, and consultants across multiple industries.

**G186:p0009:c003 · PDF 9**

The mission of The Open Group is to drive the creation of Boundaryless Information Flow™ achieved by:

**G186:p0009:c004 · PDF 9**

• Working with customers to capture, understand, and address current and emerging requirements, establish policies, and share best practices

**G186:p0009:c005 · PDF 9**

• Working with suppliers, consortia, and standards bodies to develop consensus and facilitate interoperability, to evolve and integrate specifications and open source technologies

**G186:p0009:c006 · PDF 9**

• Offering a comprehensive set of services to enhance the operational efficiency of consortia

**G186:p0009:c007 · PDF 9**

• Developing and operating the industry’s premier certification service and encouraging procurement of certified products

**G186:p0009:c008 · PDF 9**

Further information on The Open Group is available at www.opengroup.org.

**G186:p0009:c009 · PDF 9**

The Open Group publishes a wide range of technical documentation, most of which is focused on development of standards and guides, but which also includes white papers, technical studies, certification and testing documentation, and business titles. Full details are available at www.opengroup.org/library.

</details>

1. Introduction

<a id="1.1"></a>
## 1.1 概述

原文：1.1. Overview。PDF 物理页：[17, 18, 19]。状态：`source-compared-revised`。

本文档为使用 TOGAF 框架开发、维护并使用企业架构（EA）提供指导，是 TOGAF 框架的伴侣，旨在把框架中的概念与通用构造“落地”。文档提出一种开发、维护并使用 EA 的方法，使 EA 与利益相关者的需求与期望对齐，并实现可预测的价值创造。

文档意在展示：每位实践者如何用**同一概念**（而非同一技术、模板或流程）（a）为企业交付有用的 EA，（b）改进 EA 能力。这一点很重要：同一概念。例如，实践证据表明，没有一个 EA 团队不使用存储库——无论存储库是一个文件文件夹，还是完整的建模分析软件。若你在这一点上感到困扰，请停下来想一想你带进这场讨论的先入之见。例如，阅读时若你产生类似“但真正的存储库应包括……”的反应，请自问这是否普遍成立。**存储库的概念是普遍的，实现方式各异。**

TOGAF 框架的基本脚手架是**概念**；框架中的其他内容要么是示例，要么是帮助你起步的初始集。若不喜欢某个示例，可利用框架的模块化结构替换它——领先的实践者与用户常这样做。本文档就是建议实践者让 TOGAF 框架的通用结构发挥作用。

本文档面向**实践者**——被委派开发、维护并使用 EA 的人。“实践者”一词有意反映角色，而非企业中众多职位头衔之一。

文档结构提供实践者可随时查阅的语境、内容与决策理由。若有效使用，经过深思熟虑开发的 EA 将基于开放标准与全球互操作，优化企业内外部的**无边界信息流**。

本文档明确关于开发、维护，最重要的是**使用** EA。潜在企业与目的的范围之广，需要这样长度的指南来指明方向。遵循《世界级企业架构》白皮书 [W102] 的方法，TOGAF 标准通常用于开发支持战略制定、组合管理、项目规划与执行、解决方案开发的架构。集体经验表明：不存在唯一正确的 EA 交付物、模型、视图、工作产品或技术；正确方法取决于架构开发举措的目的。任何声称存在单一正确方法的人，都不能为你提供成功所需的正确建议。本文档帮助实践者识别适合特定目的的方法。

开发、维护并使用 EA 需要与战略制定、预算、收益实现、组合管理、项目群与项目管理、运营单元等专业职能深度互动。本文档将：

- 介绍关键关注主题；
- 描述与该主题相关的 TOGAF 标准概念；
- 展示其与开发、维护并使用 EA 的关系；
- 讨论实践者需要知道什么；
- 描述实践者应如何运用这些知识。

文档虽有逻辑结构，但并非简单任务清单；步骤深度与细节取决于目的并且是迭代的，唯一变量是每一步投入的时间。与所有变革工作一样，列出需要知道什么，不等于规定文档详细程度。

企业的关键决策按**业务周期**做出；架构应为决策提供信息并使能决策。只要把架构交付与企业的业务周期、以及架构开发举措的目的对齐即可。价值是在架构被使用时才交付的，道理就这么简单。

本文档分为六个部分：

- **第一部分 引言与概念**：包含本篇引言与一组定义。
- **第二部分 企业架构的指导**：涉及企业架构是什么、用于什么；跨 EA 全景协调 EA 开发；使 EA 开发与业务周期协调。
- **第三部分 开发企业架构的指导**：涉及使用 ADM；开发支持战略的架构；开发支持组合的架构；开发支持项目的架构；开发支持解决方案交付的架构；特殊情况。
- **第四部分 使用企业架构的指导**：涉及深陷解决方案交付时该做什么；架构在行动（敏捷企业、应对事件等）。
- **第五部分 维护企业架构的指导**：涉及管理多个同时进行的路线图；深陷解决方案交付时该做什么。
- **第六部分 附录**：给出与框架、参考模型等相关的有用表格清单。


### 语义规则与适用边界

- 复用的是 TOGAF 概念；技术、模板和流程可以不同。
  - 适用边界：存储库是通用概念，文件夹或建模软件都可实现；不能把某一工具强设为所有项目的前提。
  - PDF 17：Not the same technique, not the same template, not the same process.
- 架构工作产品与方法选择取决于本次架构目的。
  - 适用边界：四种常见目的是战略、组合、项目和解决方案开发；不存在对所有目的唯一正确的交付物或模型。
  - PDF 17：the correct approach is specific to the purpose
- 架构必须在业务周期中的决策时点可用；其价值在被使用时才交付。
  - 适用边界：输出完成度不能替代决策有用性。
  - PDF 18：The value is delivered when the architecture is used.

### 原文内容单元

<details><summary>展开英文原文及文本块定位</summary>

**G186:p0017:c001 · PDF 17**

1.1. Overview

**G186:p0017:c002 · PDF 17**

This document provides guidance on using the TOGAF framework to develop, maintain, and use an Enterprise Architecture (EA). This document is a companion to the TOGAF framework and is intended to bring the concepts and generic constructs in the TOGAF framework to life. This document puts forward an approach to develop, maintain, and use an EA that aligns to a set of requirements and expectations of the stakeholders and enables predictable value creation.

**G186:p0017:c003 · PDF 17**

It is intended to take the TOGAF concepts and show how each Practitioner can use the same concept to (a) deliver useful EA for their Enterprise and (b) deliver improvements to EA Capability. This point is important: use the same concept. Not the same technique, not the same template, not the same process. The same concept. For example, evidence from prevalent practice shows that there is not a single EA team that didn’t use a repository, whether the repository is a file folder or a fully-fledged installation of modeling and analytic software. If you are struggling with this point, stop and think about any preconceptions you are carrying into the conversation. For example, while reading, if you have a reaction similar to “but a real repository includes …”, ask yourself if this is universally true. The concept of a repository is universal; the implementation varies.

**G186:p0017:c004 · PDF 17**

The essential scaffolding of the TOGAF framework is the concepts. Everything else in the TOGAF framework is either an example or a starter set to get you moving. If you do not like the example, then you can take advantage of the modular structure of the TOGAF framework and substitute it. Leading Practitioners and users often take this approach. This document is about advising the Practitioner in making the universal structure of the TOGAF framework work.

**G186:p0017:c005 · PDF 17**

This document is written for the Practitioner, the person who is tasked to develop, maintain, and use an EA. Choice of the term Practitioner is deliberate, reflecting the role, rather than one of the myriad job titles in an Enterprise the Practitioner may have.

**G186:p0017:c006 · PDF 17**

This document is structured to provide the context, content, and rationale behind choices and steps that an EA Practitioner can consult at any point. When effectively used, a thoughtfully developed EA optimizes Boundaryless Information Flow™ within and between Enterprises based on open standards and global interoperability.

**G186:p0017:c007 · PDF 17**

This document is explicitly about developing, maintaining, and, most importantly, using an EA. The

**G186:p0017:c008 · PDF 17**

[1]

**G186:p0017:c009 · PDF 17**

range of potential Enterprises and purposes require a guide of this length to define the direction. Following the approach suggested in the World-Class Enterprise Architecture White Paper [W102], the TOGAF Standard is routinely applied to develop architectures supporting strategy development, portfolio management, project planning and execution, and solution development. Collective experiences reflect that there is no one right EA deliverable, model, view, work product, or technique. Rather, the correct approach is specific to the purpose of the architecture development initiative. Anyone who suggests there is a single correct approach, model, view, work product, or technique is not providing the right advice for you to succeed. This document will help you, the Practitioner, to identify the approach that is appropriate to any particular purpose.

**G186:p0018:c000 · PDF 18**

Developing, maintaining, and using an EA requires deep interaction with several specialized functions such as strategy development, budgeting, benefits realization, portfolio management, program & project management, and operational units. This document will:

**G186:p0018:c001 · PDF 18**

• Introduce key topics of concern

**G186:p0018:c002 · PDF 18**

• Describe the TOGAF Standard concepts related to the topic

**G186:p0018:c003 · PDF 18**

• Show how it is related to developing, maintaining, and using an EA

**G186:p0018:c004 · PDF 18**

• Discuss what the Practitioner needs to know

**G186:p0018:c005 · PDF 18**

• Describe what the Practitioner should do with this knowledge

**G186:p0018:c006 · PDF 18**

Even though this document has a logical structure, it is not simple task list. The depth and detail of the steps needed to be taken by the Practitioner are specific to the purpose and are iterative. The only variable is time spent for every step. As with all change work, listing what you need to know is not the same as defining the level of detail in the documentation.

**G186:p0018:c007 · PDF 18**

Key decisions are made in an Enterprise following a business cycle. An architecture should inform and enable decision-making. Just align the delivery of architecture to the Enterprise’s business cycle and the purpose of the architecture development initiative. The value is delivered when the architecture is used. It is plain and simple. This document is divided into six parts, as follows:

<a id="1.2"></a>
## 1.2 如何结合 TOGAF 框架使用本文档

原文：1.2. How to Use this Document with the TOGAF Framework。PDF 物理页：[19, 20]。状态：`source-compared-revised`。

TOGAF 框架提供了一套基本的、通用的脚手架，适用于多种组织、行业与架构风格。本文档旨在填补 TOGAF 框架未明确涉及的部分，并提供一种解读该标准的方法。这并不意味着 TOGAF 框架有缺陷。TOGAF 框架的设计本就需要解读或定制，它必须提供通用的脚手架。在“企业”定义所给出的种种不同示例之间，共同且普遍的东西是什么？是以**概念**形式表达的必备脚手架。

看待 TOGAF 框架的一种方式是：它是为**专家型理论家**写的——即为思考 EA 的结构与实践的人而写。而《TOGAF® 系列指南：TOGAF® 领导者指南——建立并演进 EA 能力》[G184] 则是为受命建立或演进 EA 能力的人而写。

本文档直接面向**做实际工作的人**：开发、维护并使用 EA 的人；不担心理论、也不担心如何构建或维护 EA 能力的人；开发、使用并维护良好 EA 的人。

本文档虽不假定读者具备 TOGAF 框架的详细知识，但会探讨 TOGAF 标准的核心概念，并把这些概念放在“用它们来开发、维护并使用 EA”的语境中。内容包括：关于迭代的指导、EA 存储库、为支持战略/组合/项目/解决方案交付四种目的而执行 ADM，以及对 EA 实践的开发与使用进行有效治理。

本文档采取的方法是：在“使用”的语境中探讨概念结构。本文档假定你已建立 EA 能力，并已针对本企业定制了 TOGAF 框架。

本文档是 TOGAF 库的组成部分。TOGAF 库中的其他文档包括《TOGAF® 领导者指南——建立并演进 EA 能力》。TOGAF 库提供了对 TOGAF 标准的完整解读，用以建立 EA 能力、发展 EA 能力团队，并交付有用的架构来指导变革、治理企业变革举措。


### 语义规则与适用边界

- 本指南以已建立 EA 能力并已为本企业定制 TOGAF 为前提。
  - 适用边界：缺少该前提时，应引用 G184 的能力建立工作；不能把实践者工作流当作自动完成了组织授权与能力建设。
  - PDF 20：assumes that you have established an EA Capability

1.2. How to Use this Document with the TOGAF Framework

**G186:p0019:c012 · PDF 19**

The TOGAF framework provides essential universal scaffolding useful in a range of organizations, industries, and architectural styles. This document is designed to fill in what is not explicitly addressed by the TOGAF framework and provides an approach to interpret the standard. This does not suggest that the TOGAF framework is flawed. The TOGAF framework is designed to require interpretation or customization. It has to provide universal scaffolding. What is common and universal between all of the different examples provided in the definition of Enterprise? Essential scaffolding expressed as concepts.

**G186:p0019:c013 · PDF 19**

One way to look at the TOGAF framework is that it is written for the expert theoretician – the person who thinks about the structure and practice of EA. TOGAF® Series Guide: The TOGAF Leader’s Guide to Establishing and Evolving an EA Capability [G184] is for the person tasked with establishing or evolving an EA Capability.

**G186:p0019:c014 · PDF 19**

This document is written directly for the person who does the work: develops, maintains, and uses an EA. The person who is not worried about the theory, and who is not worried about how to structure or maintain an EA Capability. The person who develops, uses, and maintains a good EA.

**G186:p0019:c015 · PDF 19**

While this document assumes no detailed knowledge of the TOGAF framework, it explores the core concepts of the TOGAF Standard. It places these concepts together in the context of using them to develop, maintain, and use an EA. This includes guidance on iteration, an EA Repository, executing the ADM for the purpose of supporting Strategy, Portfolio, Project, and Solution Delivery, and performing effective governance of the development and use of the EA practice.

the TOGAF framework for your Enterprise.

## 2.1 企业（Enterprise）

原文：2.1. Enterprise。PDF 物理页：[21]。状态：`source-compared-revised`。

**企业：用于识别 EA 与 EA 能力所涵盖边界的、对组织的最高描述层级。**

> 注：该定义有意保持灵活，不与组织的法律边界或职能边界绑定。它必须同时覆盖单一制组织，以及由使命或供应链连接起来的多个独立组织所构成的扩展型组织，也必须覆盖组织内部的各个运营实体。设想一家把制造、物流与支持外包给合作伙伴的组织；一支多国维和部队；一家《财富》50 强公司中规模数十亿美元的事业部——它们都是企业。


### 语义规则与适用边界

- Enterprise 是 EA 与 EA 能力的分析边界，不必等同法定法人或职能组织边界。
  - 适用边界：可包含供应链或使命连接的多个组织，也可指一个组织内的运营实体。
  - PDF 21：Note: This definition is deliberately flexible and not associated with an organization’s legal or functional boundaries. It must cover monolithic organizations and extended organizations that include separate organizations connected by a mission or supply chain, as well as operating entities within an organization. Consider an organization that uses outsourced partners to provide manufacturing, logistics, and support; a multi-national peacekeeping force; and a multi-billion-dollar division of a Fortune 50 firm. All are Enterprises.

## 2.2 企业架构（EA）

原文：2.2. Enterprise Architecture (EA)。PDF 物理页：[21]。状态：`source-compared-revised`。

由于本文档的重点是解释 TOGAF 框架与企业架构概念，最好对这个概念作较为详细的界定；过分简洁的定义往往需要专门知识才能体会其中的细微差别。关于 EA 的讨论见第 3 章。

可以采用的简洁定义有两个，分别来自 Gartner 与 DoDAF。Gartner 把企业架构定义为：“把业务愿景与战略转化为有效企业变革的过程，其方式是创建、沟通并改进描述企业未来状态、并使企业得以演进的关键原则与模型。”DoDAF 把架构定义为：“一组抽象与模型，用于简化和沟通复杂的结构、流程、规则与约束，以改进理解、实施、预测与资源安排。”

EA 业界许多人认为区分“架构”（architecture）与“架构描述”（architecture description）这两个术语很有用，但**本文档不作这种区分**。


### 语义规则与适用边界

- 本指南不区分 architecture 与 architecture description。
  - 适用边界：这是本文约定，不是推翻其他标准对两者的区分。
  - PDF 21：While many in the EA profession find distinguishing the terms “architecture” and “architecture description” useful, this document does not make any such distinction.

### 原文内容单元

<details><summary>展开英文原文及文本块定位</summary>

**G186:p0021:c005 · PDF 21**

2.2. Enterprise Architecture (EA)

**G186:p0021:c006 · PDF 21**

As the focus of this document is to explain the TOGAF framework and the concept of Enterprise Architecture, it is better to define this concept in some detail. Succinct definitions tend to require specialized knowledge to understand the nuance. See Chapter 3 for a discussion of EA.

**G186:p0021:c007 · PDF 21**

Two concise definitions that can be used are from Gartner and DoDAF. Gartner[1] defines Enterprise Architecture as: “the process of translating business vision and strategy into effective Enterprise change by creating, communicating, and improving the key principles and models that describe the Enterprise’s future state and enable its evolution”. DoDAF defines architecture as: “a set of abstractions and models that simplify and communicate complex structures, processes, rules, and constraints to improve understanding, implementation, forecasting, and resourcing”.

**G186:p0021:c008 · PDF 21**

While many in the EA profession find distinguishing the terms “architecture” and “architecture description” useful, this document does not make any such distinction.

</details>

## 3. 企业架构的目的

原文：3. The Purpose of Enterprise Architecture。PDF 物理页：[25]。状态：`source-compared-revised`。

快速浏览相关文献就会很快发现：对于企业架构（EA）是什么样子、如何使用 EA，人们并没有一致的理解。那些试图简洁定义 EA 的说法，往往谈论系统的基本概念、要素、关系与属性；这类尝试通常带有很高的专业门槛，对非专业人士常常没有意义。进一步说，可以认为这正是许多评论者只聚焦于自己所开发的架构、并隐含假定人人都该照做所导致的结果。**理解源自目的。**

EA 是一种战略工具：它提供一种方法，用来识别并处理“愿景与现实”之间的差距——无论差距由什么驱动。它加速企业实现既定目标的能力。这一工具自带使用它的方法、支撑方向分类的**分类法**（taxonomy），以及从使用该工具中获益所需的资源。

本章将回答以下问题：

- 为什么开发企业架构很重要？
- 什么是企业架构？
- 如何使用企业架构？


### 语义规则与适用边界

- EA 用于识别和处理期望与现实的差距，以支持既定目标。
  - 适用边界：方法、分类法和资源共同支撑使用；单一图或模型不足以代替这一用途。
  - PDF 25：EA is a strategic tool that presents an approach to identify and address gaps between aspirations and reality, whatever drives the gaps. It accelerates the ability of an Enterprise to achieve its stated objectives. The tool comes with its method to use, taxonomy to support the directions, and resources needed to benefit from using the tool.

### 原文内容单元

<details><summary>展开英文原文及文本块定位</summary>

**G186:p0025:c000 · PDF 25**

3. The Purpose of Enterprise Architecture

**G186:p0025:c001 · PDF 25**

A quick perusal of the literature will rapidly highlight that there is no consistent understanding of what an Enterprise Architecture (EA) looks like, or how one uses an EA. Attempts to succinctly define EA speak of fundamental concepts, elements, relationships, and properties of a system. These attempts tend to carry a high level of specialized knowledge and often make little sense to non-specialists. Further, it can be argued that this is the result of many commentators focusing on the architecture they develop, with the implicit assumption that everyone should do the same. Understanding comes from purpose.

**G186:p0025:c002 · PDF 25**

EA is a strategic tool that presents an approach to identify and address gaps between aspirations and reality, whatever drives the gaps. It accelerates the ability of an Enterprise to achieve its stated objectives. The tool comes with its method to use, taxonomy to support the directions, and resources needed to benefit from using the tool.

**G186:p0025:c003 · PDF 25**

This chapter will address the following questions:

**G186:p0025:c004 · PDF 25**

• Why is it important to develop an Enterprise Architecture?

**G186:p0025:c005 · PDF 25**

• What is an Enterprise Architecture?

**G186:p0025:c006 · PDF 25**

• How to use an Enterprise Architecture?

</details>

<a id="3.1"></a>
## 3.1 为什么开发企业架构很重要？

原文：3.1. Why is it Important to Develop an Enterprise Architecture?。PDF 物理页：[25, 26]。状态：`source-compared-revised`。

开发 EA 的原因非常简单：**引导有效变革**。

所有企业都在寻求改进。无论是公共企业、私营企业还是社会企业，都需要有意识、有效的变革来实现改进。对私营企业，改进可能是股东价值或敏捷性；对公共企业，可能是基于法定授权的价值主张或效率；对社会企业，可能只是使命的改进。

关于有效变革的指导，将在实现已批准 EA 的活动中发挥。在实施过程中，EA 被利益相关者用来**治理变革**。治理的第一部分是**引导**变革活动——使变革与实现预期价值的最优路径对齐；治理的第二部分是**控制**变革活动——确保变革始终停留在最优路径上。

改进的范围驱动着所做的一切。一套方法用于同时验证目标与变革，确保二者可行、能交付期望价值，并且成本有效。**架构化的方法**提供了一套严谨的规划与变革治理方法。

用最简单的话说，EA 必须描述企业的**未来状态**与**现状**。对未来的描述使合适的人理解：在企业所处的语境中，为达成企业的目标、目的、使命与愿景必须做什么。现状与未来状态之间的差距，凸显出**必须改变什么**。好的 EA 促进有效的治理、管理、风险管理，并促成机会的利用。一份差距清单让“必须改变什么、这种改变意味着什么”变得显而易见：拟议项目是否与所需一致？是否与优先级一致？是否与完整的目标与目的集一致？

上文的论述凸显了 EA 的概念范围。这一范围常导致一种假定：EA 只用于回答**大问题**。事实绝非如此。同样的概念、方法、技术与框架，完全可以用于处理大大小小问题的终态、偏好权衡与价值实现。本质差别不在于你做什么，而在于**记录下来的架构长什么样**。系统的范围会变，对要素与属性的详细描述会变，但所有概念保持不变。


### 语义规则与适用边界

- 实施期间的治理既指导变革与预期价值对齐，也控制其保持在相应路径上。
  - 适用边界：目标必须经过批准，指导与控制不是架构师个人偏好。
  - PDF 25：Guidance on effective change will take place during the activity to realize the approved EA. During implementation,[1] EA is used by the stakeholders to govern change. The first part of governance is to direct change activity – align the change with the optimal path to realizing the expected value. The second part of governance is to control the change activity – ensuring the change stays on the optimal path.
- 当前状态与未来状态的差距必须能够说明要改变什么及其影响。
  - 适用边界：项目对齐检查还需优先级与完整目标集合，不能仅比较技术组件。
  - PDF 26：In its simplest terms, EA must describe the future state and the current state of the Enterprise. The description of the future state enables the right people to understand what must be done to meet the Enterprise’s goals, objective, mission, and vision in the context within which the Enterprise operates. The gap between the Enterprise’s current state and future state highlights what must change. A good EA facilitates effective governance, management, risk management, and exploitation opportunities. A list of gaps makes obvious what must change and the implications of that change: is the proposed project in alignment with what is needed? In alignment with priority? In alignment with the complete set of goals and objectives?

**G186:p0025:c007 · PDF 25**

3.1. Why is it Important to Develop an Enterprise

**G186:p0025:c008 · PDF 25**

Architecture?

**G186:p0025:c009 · PDF 25**

An EA is developed for one very simple reason: to guide effective change.

**G186:p0025:c010 · PDF 25**

All Enterprises are seeking to improve. Regardless of whether it is a public, private, or social Enterprise, there is a need for deliberate, effective change to improve. Improvement can be shareholder value or agility for a private Enterprise, mandate-based value proposition or efficiency for a public Enterprise, or simply an improvement of mission for a social Enterprise.

**G186:p0025:c011 · PDF 25**

Guidance on effective change will take place during the activity to realize the approved EA. During implementation,[1] EA is used by the stakeholders to govern change. The first part of governance is to direct change activity – align the change with the optimal path to realizing the expected value. The second part of governance is to control the change activity – ensuring the change stays on the optimal path.

**G186:p0025:c012 · PDF 25**

The scope of the improvement drives everything that is done. A methodology that serves to validate both the objective and the change, ensuring that both are feasible, delivers the desired value, and in a cost-effective manner. An architected approach provides a rigorous planning and change governance methodology.

**G186:p0026:c000 · PDF 26**

In its simplest terms, EA must describe the future state and the current state of the Enterprise. The description of the future state enables the right people to understand what must be done to meet the Enterprise’s goals, objective, mission, and vision in the context within which the Enterprise operates. The gap between the Enterprise’s current state and future state highlights what must change. A good EA facilitates effective governance, management, risk management, and exploitation opportunities. A list of gaps makes obvious what must change and the implications of that change: is the proposed project in alignment with what is needed? In alignment with priority? In alignment with the complete set of goals and objectives?

**G186:p0026:c001 · PDF 26**

The preceding paragraphs highlight the conceptual scope of EA. This scope often leads to the assumption that EA is only used to answer the big questions. Nothing can be further from the truth. The same concepts, methods, techniques, and frameworks can readily be used to address the end state, preference trade-off, and value realization for big and little questions. The essential difference is not what you do; it is what the documented architecture looks like. The scope of the system varies; the detailed description of elements and properties vary. All of the concepts remain the same.

</details>

<a id="3.2"></a>
## 3.2 什么是企业架构？

原文：3.2. What is an Enterprise Architecture?。PDF 物理页：[26]。状态：`source-compared-revised`。

简言之，EA 提供了实现企业战略的**最有效路径**。好的 EA 采用整体化方法，运用适当的分析、规划、设计与实施方法，把战略转化为定义良好的执行路径。

EA 的目的在于：通过规划、设计、部署与交付变革的循环，使企业能够最有效地达成使命、业务战略与目标。架构化的方法提供一套严谨的规划方法，验证业务目标，确保目标可行、能交付期望的业务价值，并且其达成是成本有效的。

实现这一目的，来自对**企业、语境、变革范围以及将要实现的价值**的理解。使用 EA 有助于这种理解：企业以一致的术语被描述，凸显出基本组成部分及其相互作用方式。一致的术语使同类比较成为可能；针对期望终态与偏好，探讨对基本组成部分的潜在变更。这种理解与分析，使企业能够在相互竞争的偏好之间、在不同成本与不同收益的潜在变更之间进行权衡。

简言之，好的 EA 使利益相关者能够**有意识地**在一组相互竞争的偏好之间取得恰当平衡。它让各业务单元在追求业务价值交付时可以安全地创新，同时确保组织对整合战略的需求得到满足，使扩展型企业获得尽可能紧密的协同。


### 语义规则与适用边界

- EA 帮助利益相关者在竞争性偏好之间知情权衡。
  - 适用边界：局部创新仍须满足整合战略和扩展企业的协同需要。
  - PDF 26：In short, a good EA enables stakeholders to knowingly strike the right balance between any competing set of preferences. It allows individual business units to innovate safely in their pursuit of business value delivery. At the same time, it ensures the needs of the organization for an integrated strategy are met, permitting the closest possible synergy across the extended Enterprise.

### 原文内容单元

**G186:p0026:c002 · PDF 26**

3.2. What is an Enterprise Architecture?

**G186:p0026:c003 · PDF 26**

In short, EA provides the most effective path to realizing an Enterprise’s strategy.[2] A good EA uses a holistic approach to translate strategy into a well-defined execution path, using appropriate analysis, planning, design, and implementation methods.

**G186:p0026:c004 · PDF 26**

The purpose of EA is to enable the Enterprise to most effectively achieve the mission, business strategy, and goals through cycles of planning, design, deployment, and delivery of change. An architected approach provides a rigorous planning methodology that validates the business objectives, ensuring that they are feasible, deliver the desired business value, and their achievement is cost-effective.

**G186:p0026:c005 · PDF 26**

Achieving this purpose comes from understanding the Enterprise, the context, the scope of change, and the value that will be realized. Using EA facilitates understanding. The Enterprise is described in consistent terms, highlighting fundamental parts and how they interact. Consistent terms enable like- with-like comparison. Potential changes to the fundamental parts are explored regarding the desired end-state and preferences. This understanding and analysis enable trade-off between competing preferences and potential changes that carry different costs and different benefits.

**G186:p0026:c006 · PDF 26**

In short, a good EA enables stakeholders to knowingly strike the right balance between any competing set of preferences. It allows individual business units to innovate safely in their pursuit of business value delivery. At the same time, it ensures the needs of the organization for an integrated strategy are met, permitting the closest possible synergy across the extended Enterprise.

<a id="3.2.1"></a>
## 3.2.1 EA 全景简介

原文：3.2.1. Introduction to the EA Landscape。PDF 物理页：[26, 27, 28, 29]。状态：`source-compared-revised`。

TOGAF 框架用 **EA 全景**（EA Landscape）指代 EA 的完整描述集合。本文档区分 EA 全景与 EA，因为一个完整 EA 全景中不会只有单一描述：任一时刻，典型企业可能有许多架构描述——有的针对非常具体的需求，有的更通用；有的关注细节，有的提供大局；有的针对同一主题的不同状态（现状、目标、过渡）或不同时期。

TOGAF 标准提供组织 EA 全景的框架；EA 全景识别所有潜在架构的边界及相关约束与指导。

许多特征可用于组织 EA 全景。必须认识的关键概念是：**任何开发与维护 EA 的举措都只填充 EA 全景的一部分**；随着时间推移、经过多次行动，EA 全景被填充与刷新。TOGAF 中关于迭代的许多论述正是为此。

与其把迭代理解为 ADM 的重排与循环，不如把 TOGAF 的**架构项目**概念与 **EA 全景**概念结合：每个架构项目都有意识地只开发“刚好足够”的 EA 全景，以服务当前需要；开发在先前架构的语境中进行，先前架构指导或约束当前工作；每个架构项目都会创建、细化并可能改变 EA 全景中的组件。

当 EA 全景被填充后，其中包含可用的描述、约束或指导。若不进行重复的信息收集、分析、评审与批准，实践者无法有信心地推进；现有决策、指导与约束为当前架构开发提供信息。最佳实践把信息收集与分析限制在解决当前问题所需的最低限度。EA 的努力只有在 EA 被使用时才回报最高；而架构师“完成”之前，EA 无法被使用。所有架构开发都必须以**上市时间（TTM）**评估。只填充 EA 全景所需部分，并遵循已有约束与指导，可加速 TTM。

四个常见的独立特征界定 EA 全景：

- **广度**（Breadth）：架构项目覆盖的主题范围。广度容易混淆，因为它可指广泛主题；以领域、组织、举措为例。广度可以是具体主题领域的层级，例如组织可按组织层级分解；主题是灵活的：处理特定举措会包含所有受影响组织，而组织会涉及所有受影响举措。广度是最重要的范围维度之一，为实践者提供分析语境。
- **详细程度**（Level of Detail）：不言自明。很容易在领域、组织或举措范围内不断探索与细化；随着架构开发，细化到回答当前问题所需的程度即可。足以支持决策或方向性指导的“足够好”答案即可推进。始终开发到满足架构项目目的所需的最低详细程度；始终牢记，更详细的架构工作受更不详细或更高层架构的指导与约束。最后，所需细节越多，TTM 越长——细节需要时间收集、分析、描述与获批。
- **时间**（Time）：每个架构开发项目都有规划期，即期望达到目标架构的时间点。时间带来挑战，因为未来在变动；通常规划期越长，架构越不详细（这常成立但非普遍规律）。最后，在达到规划期之前存在一个或多个过渡架构时需谨慎：更详细的架构必须仔细遵循该时点有效的指导与约束，而指导与约束会随不同过渡状态变化，这可能是挑战。
- **时效性**（Recency）：每份架构描述、规格与视图都在某一时点创建；它们总是为特定目的而建，并以最小的信息收集与分析解决当前问题。所有 EA 都会老化，或优雅或突然。时效性提示：先前 EA 可能需要评审，并重申或替换。良好的存储库能区分“开发中的架构、已批准的架构、已实现的架构、已重申的架构”；开发期间架构可能非常新，但可能尚不适合治理；未经批准应仅视为提示。时效性也可作为历史架构的组织因素。

关键点是：EA 全景内容只在需要时开发；一旦获批，它约束所有后续 EA 开发与 EA 使用。关于时间、顺序与业务周期的更广泛讨论，参见 5.3 节。

EA 全景的维度帮助我们思考 EA；但多数情况下，容易构建出“无效的简化”。架构项目不是图 3-2 所示那样的整齐立方体；真实表示更像海胆——有整合的中心，但尖刺伸向四面八方。

〔图示：图 3-1：EA 全景的特征；参阅本节结构化引用。〕

〔图示：图 3-2：带一个架构项目的 EA 全景；参阅本节结构化引用。〕

从图 3-2 看，要点在于：架构项目覆盖 EA 全景的特定部分——由广度、规划期与细节界定的部分；范围内可能已有先前工作。示例不覆盖最粗或最细的层，也不覆盖所有时期或主题；它处理全景的特定部分，将填充或刷新 EA 全景的一部分。由于存在更高层工作，架构项目中的所有工作都受更高层架构约束；示例停在某一详细程度，实践者需约束细节水平；示例处于企业总规划期内，受规划期内可与必须完成的事项约束。

更复杂的是，更高层架构可能以“未实现的目标、未实现的过渡、已实现的现状”存在；必须牢记：若更高层架构没有显式变更，现状很可能仍然有效。最后，该架构项目只是 EA 全景潜在广度的一个子集。**TTM 是有用架构的关键特征**；实践者必须坚持被要求的范围（广度、时间、细节）。范围之外的工作可能有趣、甚至未来需要，但不属于本次架构举措的范围。

当 EA 团队试图参与每场对话、做太多事时，其能量与效能会被稀释。TOGAF 的**架构工作请求**作为阶段 A 入口，正是为了界定当前架构项目：它告诉 EA 团队，在现有 EA 全景语境中，企业寻求一个目标架构，处理特定主题集合、达到必要详细程度，并能在特定规划期内完成。架构项目的实质产出是填充、替换或重申 EA 全景内容；当利益相关者接受目标后，所有后续 EA 工作、变更规划与变更执行都受已批准架构治理。

相关结构化资料：[figure-3-1](../figures/G186/fig3-1.json)。本节核对图注及正文语义；图内标签、拓扑和甘特任务的完整审校状态以独立图记录为准，本轮正文覆盖不会自动升级。
相关结构化资料：[figure-3-2](../figures/G186/fig3-2.json)。本节核对图注及正文语义；图内标签、拓扑和甘特任务的完整审校状态以独立图记录为准，本轮正文覆盖不会自动升级。

### 语义规则与适用边界

- 每个架构项目仅开发当前需要的 EA 全景部分，并在既有架构约束下创建、细化或修改内容。
  - 适用边界：迭代不只是 ADM 重排；上层架构与先前批准结果必须带入。
  - PDF 27：Instead of considering iteration regarding re-sequencing and looping the ADM, combine the TOGAF concept of an Architecture Project with the concept of the EA Landscape. Every Architecture Project knowingly develops just enough of the EA Landscape to serve the need at hand. The development is done in the context of prior architecture that guides or constrains the current work. Each Architecture Project will create, refine, and potentially change components in the EA Landscape.
- 详细程度只增加到回答当前问题所需；更详细架构受较少详细或上层架构约束。
  - 适用边界：不是所有战略架构都粗略，也不是所有图都应最细。
  - PDF 27：It is easy to get carried away to explore and elaborate continually within the scope of a domain, organization, or initiative. As the architectures are developed, elaborate to the extent needed to answer the question at hand. A good enough answer to support a decision or directionally guide is sufficient to make progress. Always develop to the least detail required to address the purpose of the Architecture Project. Always keep in mind that working on more detailed architecture is guided and constrained by less detailed or superior architecture. Lastly, the more detail required, the longer the TTM. Detail takes time to gather, analyze, describe, and get approved.
- 存储库须区分开发中、已批准、已实现和已重申；未经批准的新架构只能作为提示。
  - 适用边界：新近不等于治理有效，旧内容也不自动失效。
  - PDF 28：They are always built for a purpose, with an eye to the minimum information gathering and analysis to address the question at hand. All EAs age, often gracefully or suddenly. Recency is a hint that prior EA may need to be reviewed and either reaffirmed or replaced. A good repository can distinguish between architecture that is under development, architecture that has been approved, architecture that has been realized, and architecture that has been reaffirmed. During the development period, the architecture may be very current, but may not be valid for governance. Without approval, it should be considered as hints only. Recency may be used as an organizing factor for historical architectures.
- 上层架构可为尚未实现的目标或过渡，也可为已实现现状。
  - 适用边界：没有显式上层变更时，现状很可能仍有效；不能以新项目自动推翻。
  - PDF 29：Complicating our lives, the superior architecture may exist either as an unrealized target, unrealized transition, or a realized current state. It must always be kept in mind that where there is not an explicit change in superior architecture, the current state probably remains valid. Lastly, this Architecture Project is a subset of the potential breadth of the scope of the EA Landscape. TTM is a key feature of useful architecture; Practitioners must stick to the scope (breadth, time, detail) of what they have been asked. Work outside the scope may be interesting, potentially even needed in the future, but is not within the scope of this architecture initiative.
- 架构工作请求界定主题、必要细节和规划期；批准后的目标治理后续架构和实施。
  - 适用边界：范围外工作即使可能有用也不属于当前请求。
  - PDF 29：The energy and efficacy of an EA team is diluted when it tries to be in every conversation by trying to do too much. The construct of a TOGAF Request for Architecture Work as the entry to Phase A exists to bound the current Architecture Project. The Request for Architecture Work tells the EA team that, within the context of the existing EA Landscape, its Enterprise is looking for a Target Architecture addressing a specific set of subjects at a necessary level of detail that can be accomplished within a particular planning horizon. A substantive output of the Architecture Project is to populate, replace, or reaffirm the contents of the EA Landscape. When stakeholders accept the target, all further EA work, change planning, and change execution are governed by the approved architecture.

### 原文内容单元

<details><summary>展开英文原文及文本块定位</summary>

**G186:p0026:c007 · PDF 26**

3.2.1. Introduction to the EA Landscape

**G186:p0026:c008 · PDF 26**

The TOGAF framework uses a concept of the EA Landscape to refer to the complete set of descriptions for the EA. This document distinguishes EA Landscape from EA, because there will not be a single description in a comprehensive EA Landscape. At any point in time, a typical Enterprise may have many architectures described. Some architectures will address very specific needs; others will be more general. Some will address detail; some will provide a big picture. Some will address the same topics in

**G186:p0027:c000 · PDF 27**

different states (current, target, and transition), or different periods of time. To address this complexity, the TOGAF Standard provides a framework for organizing the EA Landscape. The EA Landscape identifies the boundary of all potential architecture, and associated constraints and guidance.

**G186:p0027:c001 · PDF 27**

Many characteristics can be used to organize an EA Landscape. An essential concept to recognize is that any initiative to develop and maintain EA populates part of the EA Landscape. Over time, over multiple actions, the EA Landscape is filled and refreshed. Much of the commentary on iteration in the TOGAF framework is designed to address this point.

**G186:p0027:c002 · PDF 27**

Instead of considering iteration regarding re-sequencing and looping the ADM, combine the TOGAF concept of an Architecture Project with the concept of the EA Landscape. Every Architecture Project knowingly develops just enough of the EA Landscape to serve the need at hand. The development is done in the context of prior architecture that guides or constrains the current work. Each Architecture Project will create, refine, and potentially change components in the EA Landscape.

**G186:p0027:c003 · PDF 27**

When populated, the EA Landscape contains a description, constraints, or guidance that can be used. Without performing repeated information gathering, analysis, review, and approval, the Practitioner cannot proceed with confidence. Existing decisions, guidance, and constraints inform current architecture development. Best practice limits information gathering and analysis to the minimum necessary to address the question at hand. Effort spent on EA returns the highest value when the EA is used. The EA cannot be used until the architect is “done”. All architecture development must be assessed against Time-To-Market (TTM). Filling in only the required parts of the EA Landscape, and following the constraints and guidance already in place, speeds TTM.

**G186:p0027:c004 · PDF 27**

Four common independent characteristics frame the EA Landscape:

**G186:p0027:c005 · PDF 27**

• Breadth: The subject matter covered by an Architecture Project

**G186:p0027:c006 · PDF 27**

Breadth is easy to find confusing since it can refer to a wide range of subjects. Consider domain, organization, and initiative as examples. Breadth can be a hierarchy of specific subject areas. For example, an organization can be broken down through the organizational hierarchy. Subjects are supple. For example, addressing a specific initiative will include all impacted organizations, and an organization will address all impacted initiatives. Breadth is one of the most important scoping dimensions. It provides the Practitioner the context of their analysis.

**G186:p0027:c007 · PDF 27**

• Level of Detail: The level of detail should be self-explanatory

**G186:p0027:c008 · PDF 27**

It is easy to get carried away to explore and elaborate continually within the scope of a domain, organization, or initiative. As the architectures are developed, elaborate to the extent needed to answer the question at hand. A good enough answer to support a decision or directionally guide is sufficient to make progress. Always develop to the least detail required to address the purpose of the Architecture Project. Always keep in mind that working on more detailed architecture is guided and constrained by less detailed or superior architecture. Lastly, the more detail required, the longer the TTM. Detail takes time to gather, analyze, describe, and get approved.

**G186:p0028:c000 · PDF 28**

Figure 3-1: Characteristics of the EA Landscape

**G186:p0028:c001 · PDF 28**

• Time: Every architecture development project will have a planning horizon; the point in time when you expect to reach the Target Architecture

**G186:p0028:c002 · PDF 28**

Time creates challenges because the future is in motion. Typically, the longer the planning horizon, the less detailed the architecture. This is often true but does not provide a universal rule. Lastly, care must be taken where one or more transition architectures exist before reaching the planning horizon. The more detailed architecture must carefully conform to the guidance and constraints active at the point in time. This can be a challenge as the guidance and constraints change through different transition states.

**G186:p0028:c003 · PDF 28**

• Recency: Each architecture description, specification, and view were created at a point in time

**G186:p0028:c004 · PDF 28**

They are always built for a purpose, with an eye to the minimum information gathering and analysis to address the question at hand. All EAs age, often gracefully or suddenly. Recency is a hint that prior EA may need to be reviewed and either reaffirmed or replaced. A good repository can distinguish between architecture that is under development, architecture that has been approved, architecture that has been realized, and architecture that has been reaffirmed. During the development period, the architecture may be very current, but may not be valid for governance. Without approval, it should be considered as hints only. Recency may be used as an organizing factor for historical architectures.

**G186:p0028:c005 · PDF 28**

The essential point is to recognize that EA Landscape contents are only developed when needed. Once approved, it constrains all further EA development and use of the EA. For a broader discussion of time,

**G186:p0028:c006 · PDF 28**

[3]

**G186:p0028:c007 · PDF 28**

sequence, and business cycle, see Section 5.3.

**G186:p0028:c008 · PDF 28**

The dimensions of the EA Landscape help us think about the EA. Keep in mind that, in most cases, it is easy to build a simplification that is not valid. Architecture Projects are not neat cubes similar to what is shown in Figure 3-2. A real representation would look more like a sea urchin – a consolidated center but with spikes going in all directions.

**G186:p0029:c000 · PDF 29**

Figure 3-2: EA Landscape with an Architecture Project

**G186:p0029:c001 · PDF 29**

Looking at Figure 3-2, the essential point is that the Architecture Project covers a specific portion of the EA Landscape – the portion defined regarding breadth, planning horizon, and detail. Prior work may already exist within the scope. The example does not cover the least or the most detailed layers, nor all time periods nor subjects. Rather the example addresses a specific portion of the landscape. The example Architecture Project will populate, or refresh, a portion of the EA Landscape. Because there is higher-level work, all work in the Architecture Project will be subject to the superior architecture. The example stops at a level of detail so the Practitioner will need to constrain the level of detail. Lastly, the example is within the total planning horizon of the Enterprise and will be constrained by what can and must be done within the planning horizon.

**G186:p0029:c002 · PDF 29**

Complicating our lives, the superior architecture may exist either as an unrealized target, unrealized transition, or a realized current state. It must always be kept in mind that where there is not an explicit change in superior architecture, the current state probably remains valid. Lastly, this Architecture Project is a subset of the potential breadth of the scope of the EA Landscape. TTM is a key feature of useful architecture; Practitioners must stick to the scope (breadth, time, detail) of what they have been asked. Work outside the scope may be interesting, potentially even needed in the future, but is not within the scope of this architecture initiative.

**G186:p0029:c003 · PDF 29**

The energy and efficacy of an EA team is diluted when it tries to be in every conversation by trying to do too much. The construct of a TOGAF Request for Architecture Work as the entry to Phase A exists to bound the current Architecture Project. The Request for Architecture Work tells the EA team that, within the context of the existing EA Landscape, its Enterprise is looking for a Target Architecture addressing a specific set of subjects at a necessary level of detail that can be accomplished within a particular planning horizon. A substantive output of the Architecture Project is to populate, replace, or reaffirm the contents of the EA Landscape. When stakeholders accept the target, all further EA work, change planning, and change execution are governed by the approved architecture.

</details>

<a id="3.2.2"></a>
## 3.2.2 目的简介

原文：3.2.2. Introduction to Purpose。PDF 物理页：[30, 31, 32]。状态：`source-compared-revised`。

基于目的的 EA 能力模型识别四种目的，它们通常界定架构项目的规划期、深度与广度，以及 EA 存储库的内容。本文档采用的基于目的的 EA 能力模型由《世界级企业架构》白皮书 [W102] 引入，并在《TOGAF® 系列指南：TOGAF® 领导者指南——建立并演进 EA 能力》[G184] 中细化。

〔图示：图 3-3：企业架构的目的；参阅本节结构化引用。〕



通常，EA 能力有四种宽泛的目的：

- **支持战略的 EA**：交付 EA，提供端到端的目标架构，并开发 3 至 10 年期的变革路线图。
  这一目的的架构通常跨多个变革项目群或组合。在此语境中，架构用于识别变革举措及支撑其的组合与项目群，设定职权范围，识别协同点，并通过组合与项目群治理战略的执行。
- **支持组合的 EA**：交付 EA，支持跨职能、多阶段、多项目的变革举措。
  这一目的的架构通常跨单个组合。在此语境中，架构用于识别项目、设定其职权范围、对齐其方法、识别协同点，并治理项目的执行。
- **支持项目的 EA**：交付 EA，支持企业的项目交付方法。
  这一目的的架构通常跨单个项目。在此语境中，架构用于澄清项目的目的与价值，识别为处理协同与未来依赖所需的需求，确保符合架构治理，并支持项目之间的集成与对齐。
- **支持解决方案交付的 EA**：交付用于支持解决方案部署的 EA。
  这一目的的架构通常是单个项目或项目的重要部分。在此语境中，架构用于界定变革将如何被设计与交付，识别对设计的约束、控制与架构需求，并最终充当变革的治理框架。

不同目的的架构通常会在 EA 全景中产生不同内容，其特征组合也不同。表 3-1 汇总了这些典型特征。表 3-1 描述的是这样一个场景：战略规划者用同一套概念、方法、技术与框架开发 EA，以制定支持企业方向的路线图。其架构项目将从战略逐层下探，直至创建一个通过支持解决方案交付来实现未来状态的组合。该表展示了战略规划者或架构实践者的工作如何对应 EA 全景的四个维度。

表 3-1：目的与 EA 全景特征

| 目的 | 广度 | 详细程度 | 时间 | 时效性 |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| 支持战略的架构 | 无固定模式。有些战略影响广泛，有些战略只覆盖狭窄主题。 | 不很详细。可能包含“当价值依赖于严格控制时非常详细”的**点约束**（point constraint）。通常指导多于约束。 | 目标通常面向 3 至 10 年。支持战略的现状架构有效期往往较短。 | 通常，更新并保持该架构最新的需求高度可变。 |
| 支持组合的架构 | 覆盖单一主题（即该组合）。 | 通常不很详细。当价值依赖于严格控制时，可能包含“非常详细”的离散约束。 | 目标通常在 2 至 5 年内有效。支持组合的现状架构应被视为已过最佳使用期——没有未来视角的组合毫无意义。 | 通常，更新并保持该架构最新的需求高度可变。 |
| 支持项目的架构 | 广度狭窄，通常是组合内的离散项目。 | 通常较详细。会包含详细约束，而这些约束可能并未被详细的架构描述完全支撑。通常约束多于指导。 | 作为目标通常有效期不足 2 年。作为现状（实现之后）会有很长的存续期。 |通常，在从目标转为现状之后，仍会在 EA 全景中长期保留。 若没有新的架构项目，该架构及其相关约束与指导将无限期延续。 |
| 支持解决方案交付的架构 | 通常广度非常狭窄。 | 最详细的 EA。会包含最详细的约束。通常只开发约束，因为指导会从上层架构承继而来。 | 作为目标通常有效期不足 2 年。作为现状（实现之后）会有很长的存续期。 |通常，在从目标转为现状之后，仍会在 EA 全景中长期保留。 若没有新的架构项目，该架构及其相关约束与指导将无限期延续。 |

相关结构化资料：[source-tables](../tables/G186.json)。本节表格全文已对照；独立结构化表保留单元格与位置。
相关结构化资料：[figure-3-3](../figures/G186/fig3-3.json)。本节核对图注及正文语义；图内标签、拓扑和甘特任务的完整审校状态以独立图记录为准，本轮正文覆盖不会自动升级。

### 语义规则与适用边界

- 四种目的是界定规划期、广度、深度和存储库内容的常见模型。
  - 适用边界：它们不是四个人员等级，也不是固定执行的四个阶段。
  - PDF 30：A purpose-based EA Capability model identifies four purposes that typically frame the planning horizon, depth and breadth of an Architecture Project, and the contents of the EA Repository. The purpose-based EA Capability model used in this document was introduced in the World-Class Enterprise Architecture White Paper [W102] and refined in the TOGAF® Series Guide: The TOGAF Leader’s Guide to Establishing and Evolving an EA Capability [G184].
- 战略目的通常识别变革举措及组合、项目群，设定边界并治理战略执行。
  - 适用边界：通常跨多个组合或项目群；3–10 年是典型时间特征。
  - PDF 30：An architecture for this purpose will typically span many change programs or portfolios. In this context, architecture is used to identify change initiatives and supporting portfolio and programs. Set terms of reference, identify synergies, and govern the execution of strategy via portfolio and programs.
- 组合目的通常跨一个组合，识别项目、对齐方法并治理其执行。
  - 适用边界：多项目跨职能变革是适用情境，不能退化为项目列表。
  - PDF 30：An architecture for this purpose will typically span a single portfolio. In this context, architecture is used to identify projects, and set their terms of reference, align their approaches, identify synergies, and govern their execution of projects.
- 项目目的澄清项目目的与价值、协同与未来依赖需求、治理符合性以及项目间对齐。
  - 适用边界：支持项目交付方法，不等于替实施者做完全部设计。
  - PDF 30：An architecture for this purpose will typically span a single project. In this context, the architecture is used to clarify the purpose and value of the project, identify requirements to address synergy and future dependency, assure compliance with architectural governance, and to support integration and alignment between projects.
- 解决方案交付目的规定设计与交付变革的约束、控制和架构需求。
  - 适用边界：通常为一个项目或其中重要部分，形成变革治理框架。
  - PDF 30：An architecture for this purpose will typically be a single project or a significant part of it. In this context, the architecture is used to define how the change will be designed and delivered, identify constraints, controls and architecture requirements to the design, and, finally, act as a governance framework for change.
- 表 3-1 的范围、细节、时间和时效性均为典型特征，允许有详细点约束及不同保留期限。
  - 适用边界：不能硬编码为战略必广、必粗、全部两年过期。
  - PDF 31：| Purpose | Breadth | Level of Detail | Time | Recency |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| Architecture to<br>Support Strategy | No pattern.<br>Some Strategy will<br>have a broad<br>impact while other<br>Strategy will cover<br>a narrow subject. | Not very detailed.<br>May contain point<br>constraints that are very<br>detailed when the value is<br>dependent upon tight<br>control.<br>Typically, more guidance<br>than constraint. | Typically, looking<br>ahead for a 3 to 10-<br>year period when<br>Target.<br>Current Architecture<br>to Support Strategy<br>tends to have a short<br>timeframe of validity. | Typically, the need to update<br>and keeping current this<br>architecture is highly<br>variable. |
| Architecture to<br>Support Portfolio | Will cover single<br>subjects (the<br>Portfolio). | Typically, not very<br>detailed.<br>May contain discrete<br>constraints that are very<br>detailed when the value is<br>dependent upon tight<br>control. | Typically, valid for 2<br>to 5-year period<br>when Target.<br>Current Architecture<br>to Support Portfolio<br>should be considered<br>past its best-before<br>date. A portfolio<br>without a view to the<br>future is pointless. | Typically, the need to update<br>and keeping current this<br>architecture is highly<br>variable. |
| Architecture to<br>Support Project | Narrow breadth,<br>typically discrete<br>Projects within a<br>Portfolio. | Typically detailed.<br>Will contain detailed<br>constraints, that may not<br>be fully supported by<br>detailed architecture<br>descriptions.<br>Typically, more constraint<br>than guidance is<br>developed. | Typically, valid as a<br>target for <2 years.<br>Will have very long-<br>lived timeframes as<br>current (post<br>realization). | Typically, will be retained in<br>the EA Landscape for an<br>extended period after<br>transition from Target to<br>Current.[4]<br>In the absence of an<br>Architecture Project, the<br>architecture and associated<br>constraints and guidance<br>will continue indefinitely. |
