### 语义规则与适用边界

- 差距、架构需求规格和控制是存储库的核心组件。
  - 适用边界：细节须足以指导与约束；缺少必要分析不能用“刚好足够”掩盖。
  - PDF 48：The three most powerful components of an EA Repository are the Architecture Requirements Specification, controls, and gaps. Managing the transition from levels of detail can be greatly simplified when, instead of modeling for the sake of building a comprehensive end to end model, its integrity is preserved, avoiding incomplete analysis for areas of the architecture where sufficient detail is not available. When there is sufficient detail to guide and constrain, the Practitioner’s work is done.
- 视图、约束和指导必须从架构推导，并按适用性判断充分性。
  - 适用边界：利益相关者批准视图；漂亮图示并非可接受的来源替代。
  - PDF 48：The test of sufficiency is a function of fitness for purpose. Best practice governance has the architect demonstrate that the views produced for the stakeholders and any constraints and guidance are derived from the architecture. Stakeholders approve views, not architecture descriptions.
- 建模分析软件与文档管理系统需要保持模型—文档链接，并提供候选探索空间。
  - 适用边界：单套或多套工具均可，重点是链接和隔离分析能力。
  - PDF 48：A high-functioning EA team will be supported by modeling and analytic software, as well as a document management system. Whether these software functions are provided in a single suite or a set of software tools is not material. A Practitioner requires the linkage between any models and documentation, as well as a space to perform necessary analysis to develop their candidate architecture.
- 表 5-1 是工作产品与企业流程对齐的部分清单。
  - 适用边界：常不使用、可能不使用、有限使用与关键交付物有不同情态；不能据此自动删除必要输入。
  - PDF 49：| Practice Supports | Architecture to<br>Support Strategy | Architecture to<br>Support Portfolio | Architecture to<br>Support Project | Architecture to Support<br>Solution Delivery |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| Phase A Work<br>Product:<br>Vision | Key deliverable<br>Before framing of a<br>strategic planning<br>session<br>Refresh before<br>initiation of program<br>budgeting | Key deliverable<br>Before start of<br>budget planning | Often not used<br>Activity to produce a<br>vision overlaps with<br>portfolio/program<br>candidate<br>architecture and<br>roadmap<br>Technique may be<br>used at initiation of<br>business case | Limited use<br>Primary use is early in<br>implementation cycle (via<br>internal providers or<br>execution partners) |
| Phase E Work<br>Product:<br>Candidate<br>Architecture | During strategic<br>planning session<br>Refresh as required<br>in program<br>budgeting | Key deliverable<br>Before start of<br>budget planning<br>Primary use is<br>stakeholder<br>acceptance of target<br>and definition of gap | Before project<br>initiation and<br>finalization of<br>business case<br>Primary use is<br>creation of<br>Architecture<br>Requirements<br>Specification | Before engagement of<br>execution partners<br>(including internal<br>providers)<br>Primary use is creation of<br>Architecture Requirements<br>Specification |
| Roadmap | During strategic<br>planning session<br>Refresh as required<br>in program<br>budgeting | Before start of<br>budget planning<br>Refresh as required<br>to support budgeting<br>and program<br>management | Limited use<br>Can be used as an<br>input to projects with<br>multiple interactive<br>changes | Before engagement of<br>execution partners<br>(including internal<br>providers)<br>Primary use is identification<br>of required change, and<br>preferences of how to<br>execute change, to manage<br>solution delivery partner<br>selection and engagement |
| Phase F Work<br>Product:<br>Architecture<br>Contract &<br>Architecture<br>Requirements<br>Specification | Likely not used | Limited use | Key deliverable<br>Before completion of<br>project initiation | Key deliverable<br>Before engagement and<br>contracting |
- 先依据利益相关者/关注点选择视角，再决定需要采集与分析的信息。
  - 适用边界：不得从工具支持的所有字段反推必须采集的完整数据集。
  - PDF 50：Successful Practitioners will strictly follow the first step of the architecture development phases (Phase B, Phase C, and Phase D) that says to select appropriate viewpoints. In order to select viewpoints, the Practitioner needs to know the stakeholder and concern. From these, the viewpoint that addresses the stakeholder/concern pair will identify the information necessary to address the stakeholder’s concern. ### 原文内容单元

<details><summary>展开英文原文及文本块定位</summary>

**G186:p0046:c007 · PDF 46**

5.1. What to Expect in a Well-Run Architecture Repository &

**G186:p0046:c008 · PDF 46**

EA Landscape

**G186:p0046:c009 · PDF 46**

Note: In order to provide concrete examples of working in a repository, this document presents a few screenshots using a modeling tool. These represent one way that the challenges of a managing an EA Landscape can be met. As outlined in Section 1.3, this

**G186:p0046:c010 · PDF 46**

NOTE

**G186:p0046:c011 · PDF 46**

document does not mean to suggest that the referenced tool, techniques, and literature are definitive. These examples are intended to illustrate the TOGAF concepts. Other tools and techniques are available.

**G186:p0046:c012 · PDF 46**

The TOGAF Standard identifies a broad set of materials that will be contained within the Architecture Repository (as shown in Figure 5-1). As a Practitioner, you will be directly concerned with the Architecture Landscape, Reference Library, Standards Library, Architecture Requirements Repository, and the Compliance Assessments in the Governance Repository. Typically, these are implemented by a modeling and analytic tool, and a file repository.

**G186:p0047:c000 · PDF 47**

Figure 5-1: TOGAF Architecture Repository

**G186:p0047:c001 · PDF 47**

A high-functioning EA team cannot deliver without using modeling and analytic software. Some Practitioners sketch diagrams casually as initial steps in understanding a system, or explaining one. Maintenance of a collection of sketches is not practical. It does not matter where they use a marker and 11” x 17” paper or spend hours connecting objects in drawing software, these sketches are not modeling and do not provide a meaningful contribution to the EA Landscape. Further, the gaps and errors inherent in casual sketching preclude considering the sketches as a model.

**G186:p0047:c002 · PDF 47**

Do not confuse the guidance about managing an EA Landscape and EA Repository with commentary on effective communication. Most things an EA Capability needs to represent are complex. Visualization of complex situations to support the Practitioner, the stakeholder, and others that need to be communicated with is critical. Hand sketches are one of the most powerful communication tools available to a Practitioner. Beyond ideation it is a serious error to present poorly thought-out visualizations to stakeholders and decision-makers. This document strongly recommends the inclusion of information visualization skills in any EA team to address the needs of different communities – decision-makers, implementers, and stakeholders. One of the most significant challenges to developing a high-functioning EA team is overcoming poor information management and information presentation practice.

**G186:p0048:c000 · PDF 48**

A significant factor that results in a well-run sustainable EA Repository is the ruthless minimization of information gathered and maintained. Any information that is not required for the current Architecture Project, or supports minimal traceability, should not be captured. EA teams routinely drown in an information overload after capturing and maintaining extraneous information – information that is typically only useful for more detailed architecture analysis or implementation. Good Practitioners will not confuse ruthless minimization of work with skipping necessary work: all stakeholders’ concerns must be addressed. Leading Practitioners will understand that stakeholder management is necessary and attention to non-key stakeholders is rarely on the critical path.

**G186:p0048:c001 · PDF 48**

The three most powerful components of an EA Repository are the Architecture Requirements Specification, controls, and gaps. Managing the transition from levels of detail can be greatly simplified when, instead of modeling for the sake of building a comprehensive end to end model, its integrity is preserved, avoiding incomplete analysis for areas of the architecture where sufficient detail is not available. When there is sufficient detail to guide and constrain, the Practitioner’s work is done.

**G186:p0048:c002 · PDF 48**

The test of sufficiency is a function of fitness for purpose. Best practice governance has the architect demonstrate that the views produced for the stakeholders and any constraints and guidance are derived from the architecture. Stakeholders approve views, not architecture descriptions.

**G186:p0048:c003 · PDF 48**

More detail is always available to be captured and represented in the architecture model; additional model kinds; additional refinement. When a Practitioner models for the sake of modeling, there is no endpoint. The test of success is whether the stakeholder’s concern can be addressed. As an example, the Enterprise is attempting to improve agility — can the view demonstrate to the satisfaction of the stakeholder that this Target Architecture and all associated change delivers agility? When sufficient information is gathered, and analyzed to demonstrate agility, the Practitioner is done. When the implementer can be provided with a list of gaps that need to be filled, Architecture Requirements Specifications, and controls that must be followed, the Practitioner is done. Do not do the work that comes after the decision, or activity that you are currently architecting to support.

**G186:p0048:c004 · PDF 48**

A high-functioning EA team will be supported by modeling and analytic software, as well as a document management system. Whether these software functions are provided in a single suite or a set of software tools is not material. A Practitioner requires the linkage between any models and documentation, as well as a space to perform necessary analysis to develop their candidate architecture.

**G186:p0048:c005 · PDF 48**

What is produced is either a work product that is actively consumed or the intermediate work products the Practitioner needs to produce the requested work product. Table 5-1 provides a summary of work products that are actively consumed by key Enterprise processes.

Successful Practitioners will strictly follow the first step of the architecture development phases (Phase B, Phase C, and Phase D) that says to select appropriate viewpoints. In order to select viewpoints, the Practitioner needs to know the stakeholder and concern. From these, the viewpoint that addresses the stakeholder/concern pair will identify the information necessary to address the stakeholder’s concern. Any information that is not required information to address a stakeholder concern should not be

[1]

**G186:p0050:c003 · PDF 50**

gathered and analyzed. Extra information is pointless.

**G186:p0050:c004 · PDF 50**

When the Practitioner focuses on effective communication with stakeholders, implementers, and decision-makers, pointless activity is eliminated.

<a id="5.1.1"></a>
## 5.1.1 运行良好的存储库：EA 全景

原文：5.1.1. What to Expect in a Well-Run EA Repository: EA Landscape。PDF 物理页：[50, 51, 52]。状态：`source-compared-revised`。

运行良好存储库最具挑战的方面之一，是管理随时间发生的状态转换。最简单的表述：每个架构最多存在四种状态：

- **现状**（Current）：企业今天存在的状态；为所有变更提供基线参照；
- **目标**（Target）：利益相关者已批准的状态；为治理所有变更活动提供参照；
- **过渡**（Transition）：现状与目标之间部分实现的目标状态；
- **候选**（Candidate）：EA 团队已开发、但尚未批准到足以治理变更的状态。

实践中，过渡与候选状态在 EA 存储库中制造最多复杂性。概念上探索差距很容易：只看现状与目标之间变化了什么；但把 EA 全景的四个特征（广度、深度、时间、时效）混合、再把多个状态混合、再把时间推进导致架构变化混合、再把不同架构项目在不同时间以不同详细程度处理同一主题混合——**变异性是信息管理问题的核心**。为看到最佳所需变更集，实践者必须无情最小化所维护信息，并最大化利用**决策记录**。

〔图示：图 5-2：EA 存储库示例；参阅本节结构化引用。〕



图 5-2 是一张 EA 存储库的截图。存储库中维护着一份公用的现状架构描述；这份公用现状会被定期更新，并作为所有差距分析的基础。治理上的检验标准是：现状是否合理地代表了“实际存在的东西”。存储库中还包含一份合并的目标状态以及若干过渡状态。当架构项目结束时，其架构描述会被移入合并的目标状态。与现状一样，合并目标被用于所有差距分析。尽管合并目标中的各过渡状态之间存在差异，实践者仍能据此评估当前项目是否正朝着企业的偏好推进。

正在开发中的架构会带来额外的信息管理挑战。应为每个架构项目在 EA 存储库中创建一个独立容器。该容器使实践者可以自由探索候选目标状态的各种选项、不同权衡决策及其影响，而不影响任何其他实践者的工作。运行良好的 EA 全景只进行支持当前决策/问题所需的建模与分析，不多做；实践者理解并执行“更详细的工作将来自另一个架构周期（决策之后讨论实施）”的理念。图 5-3 为分别探索组合、项目与解决方案交付的三个架构项目给出了相互独立的架构。

〔图示：图 5-3：多个候选架构；参阅本节结构化引用。〕



图 5-2 与图 5-3 提供了一个示例。不同的 EA 建模与分析软件，甚至同一工具中的不同方法，截图都会不同。本质要素是：确保 EA 存储库支持不同状态，并为架构师提供灵活空间，使其能探索潜在未来而不影响任何其他架构师的工作。

维护的支持性文档必须清楚标识其状态；否则实践者无法务实判断所看文档是否相关、有效或有用。文档必须便于判断时效性；实践中，候选、目标或较远的已实现现状架构都可能有用，但有用性以“状态与时间线的自我标识”为前提；没有这些标记，每份支持文档都只是噪音。

相关结构化资料：[figure-5-2](../figures/G186/fig5-2.json)。本节核对图注及正文语义；图内标签、拓扑和甘特任务的完整审校状态以独立图记录为准，本轮正文覆盖不会自动升级。
相关结构化资料：[figure-5-3](../figures/G186/fig5-3.json)。本节核对图注及正文语义；图内标签、拓扑和甘特任务的完整审校状态以独立图记录为准，本轮正文覆盖不会自动升级。

### 语义规则与适用边界

- 四种状态为现状、目标、过渡与候选，目标以利益相关者批准为前提。
  - 适用边界：候选尚无足够批准来治理变更；过渡是部分实现的目标，不等于任意草稿。
  - PDF 50：One of the most challenging aspects of a well-run repository is managing transitions over time. In most simple terms, every architecture will exist in up to four states. The current state is what exists in the Enterprise today; this baseline provides the reference for all change. The target state[2] is what stakeholders have approved; this state provides the reference for governing all change activity. Transition states are partially realized targets between the current state in the target state. The candidate state is what has been developed by the EA team but has not been approved for a status sufficient to govern change.
- 每个架构项目使用独立容器，允许探索方案而不影响其他架构师的工作。
  - 适用边界：合并目标与共同现状为一致差距分析提供基础；未经治理不应直接覆盖。
  - PDF 51：Architecture under development creates an additional information management challenge. For every Architecture Project, create a separate container in the EA Repository. This container allows the Practitioner freely to explore candidate target state options, different trade-off decisions, and impacts without affecting any other Practitioner’s work. A well-run EA Landscape will perform its modeling
- 支持文档必须自标状态和时间线，使相关性、有效性与时效性可判定。
  - 适用边界：旧的已实现架构仍可能有用，不能以最近修改时间替代有效期。
  - PDF 52：Supporting documents maintained must clearly identify their state. Without this ability, the Practitioner is pragmatically uncertain whether the document they are looking at is relevant, valid, or useful. They must readily allow the Practitioner to determine their recency. In practice, a candidate or target, or distantly realized current state architecture might be useful to the Practitioner. Usefulness is predicated on the “self-identification” of state and timeline. Without such markers, each supporting document is nothing but noise.

### 原文内容单元

<details><summary>展开英文原文及文本块定位</summary>

**G186:p0050:c005 · PDF 50**

5.1.1. What to Expect in a Well-Run EA Repository: EA Landscape

**G186:p0050:c006 · PDF 50**

One of the most challenging aspects of a well-run repository is managing transitions over time. In most simple terms, every architecture will exist in up to four states. The current state is what exists in the Enterprise today; this baseline provides the reference for all change. The target state[2] is what stakeholders have approved; this state provides the reference for governing all change activity. Transition states are partially realized targets between the current state in the target state. The candidate state is what has been developed by the EA team but has not been approved for a status sufficient to govern change.

**G186:p0051:c000 · PDF 51**

In practice, transition and candidate states create the most complexity in an EA Repository. Conceptually exploring gaps is easy; only look at what changed between the current and target states. Consider the four characteristics of the EA Landscape: breadth, depth, time, and recency. Now mix in multiple states. Now mix in that as time progresses the architecture can change. Now mix in that different Architecture Projects can work on the same subject at different times and different levels of detail. Variability is the nub of the information management problem. To be able to see the best set of required changes, the Practitioner must ruthlessly minimize the information maintained, and maximize the use of decision records.

**G186:p0051:c001 · PDF 51**

Figure 5-2: Example EA Repository

**G186:p0051:c002 · PDF 51**

Figure 5-2 is a screenshot from an EA Repository. A common current state description of the architecture is maintained in the repository. This common current state is periodically updated and used as the basis of all gap analysis. The governance test is that the current state reasonably represents what is. The repository also contains a consolidated target state and several transition states. When Architecture Projects come to a close, their architecture descriptions are moved into the consolidated target state. As the current state, the consolidated target is used in all gap analysis. While there is variance between transition states in the consolidated target, the Practitioner is in a position to assess whether the current project is moving towards the Enterprise’s preference.

**G186:p0051:c003 · PDF 51**

Architecture under development creates an additional information management challenge. For every Architecture Project, create a separate container in the EA Repository. This container allows the Practitioner freely to explore candidate target state options, different trade-off decisions, and impacts without affecting any other Practitioner’s work. A well-run EA Landscape will perform its modeling

**G186:p0052:c000 · PDF 52**

and analysis to support the decisions/questions at hand only to the extent necessary and nothing more. These Practitioners understand and execute with the notion that more detailed work would come from another architecture cycle, post-decision to discuss implementation. Figure 5-3 has separate architectures for an Architecture Project exploring a Portfolio, Project, and Solution Delivery.

**G186:p0052:c001 · PDF 52**

Figure 5-3: Multiple Candidate Architectures

**G186:p0052:c002 · PDF 52**

Figure 5-2 and Figure 5-3 provide an example. Different EA modeling and analytic software, or even a different approach in an EA tool, would have different screenshots. The essential component is ensuring that the EA Repository supports different states, and provides flexibility for an architect to explore a potential future without impacting any other architect’s work.

**G186:p0052:c003 · PDF 52**

Supporting documents maintained must clearly identify their state. Without this ability, the Practitioner is pragmatically uncertain whether the document they are looking at is relevant, valid, or useful. They must readily allow the Practitioner to determine their recency. In practice, a candidate or target, or distantly realized current state architecture might be useful to the Practitioner. Usefulness is predicated on the “self-identification” of state and timeline. Without such markers, each supporting document is nothing but noise.

</details>

<a id="5.1.2"></a>
## 5.1.2 运行良好的存储库：参考库

原文：5.1.2. What to Expect in a Well-Run EA Repository: Reference Library。PDF 物理页：[52, 53, 54]。状态：`source-compared-revised`。

参考库提供指南、模板、模式及其他形式的参考材料，可被利用来加速企业新架构的创建。

运行良好的 EA 存储库，其参考库中充满**加速器**。加速器缩短上市时间。本文档反复出现的主题是：确保产出足够的架构工作以支持关于企业变革活动的决策与行动。变革活动中最宝贵的资源是时间。

实践者使用的参考材料范围很广。大体上可分两类，区别在于它们是**直接用于架构开发**，还是提供**背景材料**。第一类是用于 EA 全景之中的材料，包括参考模型、参考架构与模式。这些参考材料提供经过验证的方法。经过验证的方法就是加速器，因为它们无需像全新方法那样被同等严谨地探索。例如 IT4IT 参考架构与 APQC 的流程分类框架。这两种情况下都无需再发明一套新流程。这类参考材料提供完整的起步集，简化沟通，并使 EA 团队内部可以复用——每位实践者都用相同的术语描述问题。图 5-4 给出了 EA 存储库中可用于改进架构开发的参考材料示例。

〔图示：图 5-4：建模与分析工具中的参考材料；参阅本节结构化引用。〕



模式及其他**架构构建块**（ABB），在实践者看来通常与 EA 全景中的其他参考材料难以区分。无论来自参考来源还是组织内部创建，它们都提供一种一致且已知的处理问题的方式。

第二类是**文档型参考材料**，可能包括白皮书、关于 EA 全景参考材料的讨论、模板、素材库与指南。同样，参考材料是加速器。当实践者能够获得一致的背景思路时，实践者之间的沟通会改善；与 EA 团队之外的沟通，也会因一致性而改善。

图 5-4 是一张截图，展示了以离散架构形式存在的不同参考架构与参考模型。把参考架构作为离散架构维护，使架构师能够把“参考架构在当前候选或目标中的使用方式”与基础参考材料进行比较。在存续较久的存储库中，常见多个相互重叠的参考架构。设想一家以 APQC 流程分类框架为基础参考模型的组织：若其实施主流 ERP，很可能还会产生基于 ERP 厂商流程分类与系统集成商流程分类的工作成果；后来同一组织采用 IT4IT 参考架构时，又会有另一套流程分类。

在建模与分析软件中为上述每一套维护清晰的引用，将使未来的架构师能够理解架构开发与实施治理期间所做的决策，尤其是在只有部分参考被引入架构开发并保留在架构中时。本文档承认有必要把架构工具与支持规划、解决方案交付、解决方案验证等的工具集成。实践者有时可能需要查阅此类工具中的文档，或提供适当的可追溯性。工具族及其集成超出本文档范围。

参考架构、规划数据、分析数据等，通常由文档管理系统中管理的详细文档支撑。关心“为何完整或部分使用此类数据”的实践者，会去查找支撑文档，以便恰当地用于建模或分析。在建立到文档管理系统的链接时，不要被“实践者是否真的会去读”这种想法左右。

相关结构化资料：[figure-5-4](../figures/G186/fig5-4.json)。本节核对图注及正文语义；图内标签、拓扑和甘特任务的完整审校状态以独立图记录为准，本轮正文覆盖不会自动升级。

### 语义规则与适用边界

- 独立保留参考架构，能够比较实际采用部分与原始基线。
  - 适用边界：多个重叠参考模型可以并存，不应丢弃未采用部分或混淆来源版本。
  - PDF 53：Figure 5-4 is a screenshot showing different reference architectures, and reference models, as discrete architectures. Maintaining discrete architectures allows the architect to be able to compare how the reference architecture was used in the current candidate or target against the base reference material. In longer-lived repositories, it is common to find multiple overlapping reference architectures. Consider an organization that uses APQC’s Process Classification Framework as a base reference model. Should they implement a mainstream ERP, they will likely have work produced in the ERP vendor’s process classification and the system integrator’s process classification. Later, when the same organization adopts the IT4IT Reference Architecture, they will likely have another process classification.
- 参考数据的完整/部分使用须有支持文档解释目的与理由。
  - 适用边界：是否预计被阅读不应决定是否保留必要链接；工具集成细节不在本指南范围。
  - PDF 54：Reference architectures, planning data, analytic data, etc. are normally supported by detailed documentation managed in a document management system. A Practitioner concerned with the purpose and rationale for complete or partial use of such data will seek the supporting documents, to use them appropriately for modeling or analysis. Do not get swayed by looking at whether the Practitioner is likely to read them when creating the links to the document management system.

### 原文内容单元

<details><summary>展开英文原文及文本块定位</summary>

**G186:p0052:c004 · PDF 52**

5.1.2. What to Expect in a Well-Run EA Repository: Reference Library

**G186:p0052:c005 · PDF 52**

The Reference Library provides guidelines, templates, patterns, and other forms of reference material that can be leveraged in order to accelerate the creation of new architectures for the Enterprise.

**G186:p0052:c006 · PDF 52**

The Reference Library of a well-run EA Repository is filled with accelerators. Accelerators speed time to market. A recurrent theme in this document is ensuring sufficient architecture work is produced to support decisions and actions about the Enterprise’s change activity. The most precious resource in change activity is time.

**G186:p0052:c007 · PDF 52**

There is a broad set of reference materials used by a Practitioner. Broadly there will be two sets of reference material distinguished on whether they are directly used in architecture development, or provide background material. The first are materials that are used within the EA Landscape. These will include reference models, reference architectures, and patterns. These reference materials provide proven approaches. Proven approaches are accelerators, as they do not need to be explored with the

**G186:p0053:c000 · PDF 53**

same rigor as a novel approach. For example, the IT4IT Reference Architecture and APQC’s Process Classification Framework.[3] In both cases there is no need to invent a novel set of processes. This type of reference material provides a complete starter set, simplifies communication, and enables re-use within the EA team. Each Practitioner will use the same terms to describe a problem. Figure 5-4 provides an example of reference material available in an EA Repository to improve architecture development.

**G186:p0053:c001 · PDF 53**

Figure 5-4: Reference Material in Modeling and Analytic Tool

**G186:p0053:c002 · PDF 53**

Patterns, and other Architecture Building Blocks (ABBs), are typically indistinguishable to a Practitioner from other reference material in the EA Landscape. Whether brought in from reference sources, or created inside the organization, they provide a consistent and known way of approaching a problem.

**G186:p0053:c003 · PDF 53**

The second set are documentary reference materials. This material may include white papers, discussions of EA Landscape reference material, templates, stock material, and guides. Again, reference material is an accelerator. Communication between Practitioners is improved when they have access to consistent background thinking. Communication outside the EA team is improved with consistency.

**G186:p0053:c004 · PDF 53**

Figure 5-4 is a screenshot showing different reference architectures, and reference models, as discrete architectures. Maintaining discrete architectures allows the architect to be able to compare how the reference architecture was used in the current candidate or target against the base reference material. In longer-lived repositories, it is common to find multiple overlapping reference architectures. Consider an organization that uses APQC’s Process Classification Framework as a base reference model. Should they implement a mainstream ERP, they will likely have work produced in the ERP vendor’s process classification and the system integrator’s process classification. Later, when the same organization adopts the IT4IT Reference Architecture, they will likely have another process classification.

**G186:p0054:c000 · PDF 54**

Maintaining each of these has a clear reference in the modeling, and analytic software will allow future architects to understand the decisions made during architecture development and implementation governance, especially when only part of a reference is brought into architecture development and maintained in the architecture. This document acknowledges the need to integrate an architecture tool with tools supporting planning, solution delivery, solution validation, etc. A Practitioner may have to refer to documentation in such tools on occasion or provide appropriate traceability. The family of tools and integration is beyond the scope of this document.

**G186:p0054:c001 · PDF 54**

Reference architectures, planning data, analytic data, etc. are normally supported by detailed documentation managed in a document management system. A Practitioner concerned with the purpose and rationale for complete or partial use of such data will seek the supporting documents, to use them appropriately for modeling or analysis. Do not get swayed by looking at whether the Practitioner is likely to read them when creating the links to the document management system.

</details>

<a id="5.1.3"></a>
## 5.1.3 运行良好的存储库：标准库

原文：5.1.3. What to Expect in a Well-Run EA Repository: Standards Library。PDF 物理页：[54]。状态：`source-compared-revised`。

在运行良好的 EA 存储库中，标准库承担两项职能。第一，它存放**架构必须遵守的标准**；第二，它存放**架构施加于所有实施的标准**。这一区分至关重要：前者用于**测试架构**，后者用于**测试实施**。

在实践中，这两组标准必须分开。PCI 标准就是一个简单例子。使用信用卡的企业受 PCI 标准约束。任何拥有良好 EA 的企业，都不会简单地把 PCI 标准放进存储库让实施去遵守——“如何合规”这个问题对实施团队并不合适。对 PCI 的合规可能简单到：由 EA 派生出一项标准，要求使用第三方支付处理商，从而确保 PCI 所涉信息不落在企业手中。后者才是由 EA 派生的标准。

通常还会扩展标准库，把选定的产品与第三方服务纳入其中。这是一种务实的选择：当除架构需求规格或控制之外还存在符合要求的产品或服务时，它可以简化实施项目的治理。仍以上述例子说明：例如，除在架构需求规格中要求使用第三方支付处理商外，也可以把一个具体的第三方支付处理商列入标准库。

把具体产品与服务放入标准库时，最佳实践是把这些选择直接追溯到催生它们的架构需求规格或控制。缺少对架构的追溯，产品或服务的选择就会被视为任意决定。通过产品与服务标准来做架构，其陷阱之一正是缺少对需求或风险的追溯。如果只是把某个产品或服务规格当作任意选择，治理流程会变得极其复杂，因为备选产品或服务可以依据其他标准来考量，而不是依据导致架构支持决策的那些标准。


### 语义规则与适用边界

- 标准库区分架构必须遵循的标准，与架构施加于实施的标准。
  - 适用边界：前者检验架构，后者检验实施；PCI 支付处理商是示例，不是普遍消除合规义务的规则。
  - PDF 54：In a well-run EA Repository, the Standards Library will perform two functions. First, it provides a repository for the standards that the architecture must comply to. Second, it provides a repository for the standards imposed on all implementations by the architecture. The distinction is critical. One is used to test the architecture; the second is used to test an implementation.
- 产品/服务标准应追溯到选型依据的架构需求规格或控制。
  - 适用边界：否则替代品评估会偏离当初的需求或风险准则。
  - PDF 54：Where specific products and services are placed in the Standards Library, it is best practice to trace those choices directly to the Architecture Requirements Specification or control that brought these products and services to life. Without traceability to the architecture, product or service selection can be viewed as an arbitrary choice. One of the traps of architecting through product and service standards is the lack of traceability to the requirement or risk. When there is simply the specification of a product or service as an arbitrary choice, the governance process is dramatically complicated because alternative products or services can be considered on criteria other than those that lead to an architecture supported decision.

**G186:p0054:c002 · PDF 54**

5.1.3. What to Expect in a Well-Run EA Repository: Standards Library

**G186:p0054:c003 · PDF 54**

In a well-run EA Repository, the Standards Library will perform two functions. First, it provides a repository for the standards that the architecture must comply to. Second, it provides a repository for the standards imposed on all implementations by the architecture. The distinction is critical. One is used to test the architecture; the second is used to test an implementation.

**G186:p0054:c004 · PDF 54**

In practice, these two sets of standards have to be separated. A simple example is provided by the PCI standards. An Enterprise that uses credit cards is subject to PCI standards. No Enterprise with a good EA will simply place PCI standards in a repository for an implementation to comply with. The question of how to comply is inappropriate for an implementation team. The compliance with PCI may be as simple as a standard derived from the EA that requires the use of a third-party payment processor ensuring that PCI subject information is not in the hands of the Enterprise. The latter is a standard derived from the EA.

**G186:p0054:c005 · PDF 54**

It is common to extend the Standards Library to include selected products and third-party services. This pragmatic choice simplifies the governance of Implementation Projects where, in addition to an architecture requirement specification or control, there exists a product or service that conforms. To further the example above, rather than the Architecture Requirements Specification requiring the use of the third-party payment processor, a specific third-party payment processor can be placed in the Standards Library.

**G186:p0054:c006 · PDF 54**

Where specific products and services are placed in the Standards Library, it is best practice to trace those choices directly to the Architecture Requirements Specification or control that brought these products and services to life. Without traceability to the architecture, product or service selection can be viewed as an arbitrary choice. One of the traps of architecting through product and service standards is the lack of traceability to the requirement or risk. When there is simply the specification of a product or service as an arbitrary choice, the governance process is dramatically complicated because alternative products or services can be considered on criteria other than those that lead to an architecture supported decision.

</details>

<a id="5.1.4"></a>
## 5.1.4 运行良好的存储库：架构需求库

原文：5.1.4. What to Expect in a Well-Run EA Repository: Architecture Requirements Repository。PDF 物理页：[55, 56]。状态：`source-compared-revised`。

管理整个 EA 全景的需求，是实践者面临的最复杂活动之一。第一项挑战是细节的广度；第二项是跨 EA 管理需求时的重叠性质；第三项是长期维护存储库；第四项可能是与其他存储库集成。

有一点必须考虑：需求会因架构目的与详细程度不同而显得截然不同。一个极端的例子是，具备解决方案交付架构与实施经验的实践者，可能不把支持战略的架构所开发的需求当作需求。习惯于实施的实践者往往在寻找非常细粒度的需求，以表达需求陈述；而“要敏捷、要高效、要整合新事业部、要保护市场领先的差异化能力”同样是支持战略与支持组合的架构的关键需求。

领先实践发现，支持组合与支持项目的架构需求，很大一部分通常以**评分**形式捕获：请利益相关者评估某流程、应用、服务或能力所需的效率、成熟度与自动化程度；给应用所需的业务契合度或技术契合度打分；给基础设施的首选寿命打分。最佳实践是采用 1 至 5 分制记录这些评估。所有这些评分都是需求——它们明确表达了利益相关者的偏好。

任何需求库中的一个重要问题是：这些是架构需求还是实施需求。区别可能很细微，但差异巨大。可用于区分架构与实施设计的一项测试是：该描述只能以一种方式完成，还是可以用多种方式实现？前者倾向于架构，后者是实施设计。当架构存储库与实施需求库集成时，应使用适当的集成选项来保持可追溯性与完整性。

许多架构需求的生命周期特别长，尤其是表达企业差异化特征的需求。以客户体验领先的市场领导者，什么时候会愿意放松“一流客户体验”这一需求？实践者真正的挑战，是把市场领先的客户体验转化为适用于架构组件的清晰架构规格。为不同目的做架构时的心智挑战之一就在这里：**需求与规格之间的界线，可能取决于由谁提出**。面向市场领先客户体验的组合架构需求，可能形成一项架构规格，要求“客户偏好”这一信息对象成为 CRM、客户门户与服务台的公共信息对象；而对支持新 CRM 解决方案交付的架构师来说，这项规格读起来就像一项需求。

来自组织更高层的需求也往往用不同名称讨论，常见的是“目标”与“指令”，并被赋予特殊的尊崇。同样，功能需求与非功能需求、业务需求与技术需求之间的区分也被看得很重。归根结底，无论一项需求是指令、非功能需求还是业务需求，从实践者的角度看，它都是一项需求陈述，将在上层架构以及所有利益相关者提供的目标集合的语境中处理。

实践者通常不太愿意做的一项核心活动，是**评估需求的有效性**。当实践者拥有描述良好的战略、识别了差距的组合以及填补差距的工作包时，就很容易审视注入项目或解决方案交付架构的需求，判断它是否符合企业优先级，或是否与上层架构冲突。设想一个聚焦提升客户体验敏捷性的组合举措：该组合会识别一组明确设计用于改善客户体验某方面、并提升企业变革能力的项目。随着时间接近执行，与项目目的不一致的需求常常被注入流程。需求管理的核心要素是良好的治理——实践者是价值陈述的守护者。

当实践者拥有良好的架构，识别了目标及沿途的过渡步骤时，需求与架构规格可能随时间变化，在目标架构与过渡架构中有所不同。设想一个组合路线图，在第一个过渡中刻意牺牲客户体验以换取敏捷性；到第二个过渡，优先级翻转，牺牲敏捷性以换取客户体验。针对架构需求的符合性测试以及优先级指导随之切换。本文档刻意使用“牺牲”一词，因为需求库的内在要求就是明确先后次序与优先级。缺少清晰的先后次序与优先级时，指导周期早期权衡的数据就不存在，进展受阻；而把先后次序与优先级的评估转移到其他偏好已明确、且更接近最适合做选择的组织的决策上，则更为可行。

把架构规格显式链接到需求，再把需求追溯到“利益相关者/关注点”对，以跟踪价值与偏好。这种可追溯性用于治理，评估设计与实施选择在多大程度上回应了利益相关者的价值偏好。

最佳实践的 EA 存储库会在架构的每一步促进对企业方向与优先级的追溯。实践者在参与需求管理与权衡时，交付的是最高价值的一部分工作。所有聪明的利益相关者都想要全部、想要更多、还想免费；所有聪明的利益相关者也都知道不可能全都要，也不可能免费。利益相关者不知道、而实践者的职责正在于帮助他们理解：为了实现不同的偏好组合，他们必须放弃什么。

拥有运行良好 EA 存储库的实践者，能够维护一套有语境的需求全集。有语境的需求使实践者能够积极为利益相关者的偏好工作，而不是只针对利益相关者偏好的一个子集做架构；更糟的是，针对实践者个人偏好的那套偏好做架构。


### 语义规则与适用边界

- 需求粒度随目的不同，敏捷、高效等战略偏好仍是需求。
  - 适用边界：不能仅接受细粒度实施需求，也不能把示例分数当成当前组织评估。
  - PDF 55：One thing that is important to consider is that requirements appear radically different depending upon the purpose of the architecture and the level of detail. As an extreme example, Practitioners with experience in solution delivery architecture and implementation may not recognize requirements for architecture developed to support strategy as requirements. Practitioners used to implementation tend to be looking for very granular requirements to express statements of need. Be agile, be efficient, integrate the new division, and protect the market-leading differentiators are all examples of key requirements for Architecture that supports Strategy and Portfolio.
- 上层架构的规格可能成为下层架构的输入需求。
  - 适用边界：身份由来源与上下文决定；保留跨层追溯，避免按同一个名词全局去重。
  - PDF 55：Many architecture requirements are remarkably long-lived. Especially when the requirement is articulating aspects of the Enterprise that differentiate it. When does a market leader who leads through customer experience want to relax the requirement requesting best-in-class customer experience? The real challenge for the Practitioner is translating market-leading customer experience into clear architecture specifications applied to components in the architecture. Herein lies one of the mental challenges when architecting for different purposes – the line between a requirement and a specification may be in who stated it. A requirement into a portfolio architecture aimed at market- leading customer experience may result in an architecture specification requiring that the information object “customer preference” be a common information object to the CRM, customer portal, and service desk. That specification reads like a requirement to the architect supporting solution delivery of the new CRM.
- 需求与规格可以在不同过渡状态变化，符合性测试必须按当期优先级切换。
  - 适用边界：目标期约束不能不经时点判断直接用于第一过渡；变更需经过治理。
  - PDF 56：When Practitioners have a good architecture identifying the target and transition steps along the way, requirements, and architecture specifications, may vary over time; be different in the target and the transition architectures. Imagine a portfolio roadmap that deliberately sacrifices customer experience for agility in the first transition. Then in the second transition the priority switches and agility is sacrificed for customer experience. The conformance test to architecture requirement, and guidance on priority, switches. This document deliberately uses the term “sacrifice” because inherent in this requirements repository is clarity of precedence and priority. When clarity of precedence and priority is not available, data to guide trade-off early in the cycle is absent, hindering progress. Just as the assessment of precedence and priority shifts context to other decisions where a set of preferences are well defined and is closer to the organization most suited to make the choice.
- 架构规格→需求→利益相关者/关注点的链路用于价值偏好治理。
  - 适用边界：必须保留关系而非只有字段文本；也要检查注入需求与项目目的、上层架构是否一致。
  - PDF 56：Explicitly link the architecture specification to requirements, and trace the requirements to a stakeholder/concern pair to track the value and preference. This traceability is used in governance to assess how well the design and implementation choices address the stakeholder’s value preferences.

### 原文内容单元

<details><summary>展开英文原文及文本块定位</summary>

**G186:p0055:c000 · PDF 55**

5.1.4. What to Expect in a Well-Run EA Repository: Architecture Requirements Repository

**G186:p0055:c001 · PDF 55**

Managing requirements to the entire EA Landscape is one of the most complex activities facing the Practitioner. The first challenge is simply the breadth of detail; the second challenge is the overlapping nature of managing requirements across the EA; the third challenge is maintaining the repository over time; and potentially the fourth is integrating with other repositories.

**G186:p0055:c002 · PDF 55**

One thing that is important to consider is that requirements appear radically different depending upon the purpose of the architecture and the level of detail. As an extreme example, Practitioners with experience in solution delivery architecture and implementation may not recognize requirements for architecture developed to support strategy as requirements. Practitioners used to implementation tend to be looking for very granular requirements to express statements of need. Be agile, be efficient, integrate the new division, and protect the market-leading differentiators are all examples of key requirements for Architecture that supports Strategy and Portfolio.

**G186:p0055:c003 · PDF 55**

Leading practices find that a large number of requirements for Architecture that supports Portfolio and Project are normally captured in the form of scores. Ask the stakeholders to assess the required efficiency, maturity, automation of a process, application, service or capability; score the required business fit or technical fit of applications; and score the preferred lifespan of the infrastructure. Best practice is to use a scale of one to five to capture their assessments. All of these scores are requirements; they clearly state the preferences of the stakeholders.

**G186:p0055:c004 · PDF 55**

An important question in any requirements repository is whether these are architectural requirements or implementation requirements. The distinction can be fine, but it is a distinction with a very large difference. One of the tests that can be used for distinguishing between architecture and implementation design is whether the description can only be done one way, or can it be realized multiple ways. The former tends to be architecture, while the latter is implementation design. When an Architecture Repository is integrated with a requirements repository for implementation, use appropriate integration options to maintain traceability and integrity.

**G186:p0055:c005 · PDF 55**

Many architecture requirements are remarkably long-lived. Especially when the requirement is articulating aspects of the Enterprise that differentiate it. When does a market leader who leads through customer experience want to relax the requirement requesting best-in-class customer experience? The real challenge for the Practitioner is translating market-leading customer experience into clear architecture specifications applied to components in the architecture. Herein lies one of the mental challenges when architecting for different purposes – the line between a requirement and a specification may be in who stated it. A requirement into a portfolio architecture aimed at market- leading customer experience may result in an architecture specification requiring that the information object “customer preference” be a common information object to the CRM, customer portal, and service desk. That specification reads like a requirement to the architect supporting solution delivery of the new CRM.

**G186:p0055:c006 · PDF 55**

Requirements from higher in the organization also tend to be discussed using different names. It is common to speak of objectives and mandates, and treat them with special reverence. Likewise, the distinction between types of requirement – functional versus non-functional, business requirements

**G186:p0056:c000 · PDF 56**

versus technical requirements – is treated very seriously. In the final analysis, whether a requirement is a mandate, a non-functional requirement, or a business requirement, from the perspective of a Practitioner it is a statement of need that will be addressed in the context of the superior architecture and the set of objectives provided by all stakeholders.

**G186:p0056:c001 · PDF 56**

One central activity Practitioners typically are not comfortable doing is assessing the validity of requirements. When the Practitioner has a well-described strategy, a portfolio that identifies gaps, and gap-filling work packages, it becomes easy to look at a requirement being injected in the project or solution delivery architecture and assess whether this requirement is in conformance with what the Enterprise priority is or whether this requirement conflicts with the superior architecture. Consider a portfolio initiative focused on improving agility for customer experience: this portfolio will identify a set of projects explicitly designed to improve some aspect of the customer experience and improve the ability of the Enterprise to change. As time progresses close to execution, it is common for requirements not aligned with the project’s purpose to be injected into the process. The central element of requirements management is good governance. Practitioners are guardians of the statements of value.

**G186:p0056:c002 · PDF 56**

When Practitioners have a good architecture identifying the target and transition steps along the way, requirements, and architecture specifications, may vary over time; be different in the target and the transition architectures. Imagine a portfolio roadmap that deliberately sacrifices customer experience for agility in the first transition. Then in the second transition the priority switches and agility is sacrificed for customer experience. The conformance test to architecture requirement, and guidance on priority, switches. This document deliberately uses the term “sacrifice” because inherent in this requirements repository is clarity of precedence and priority. When clarity of precedence and priority is not available, data to guide trade-off early in the cycle is absent, hindering progress. Just as the assessment of precedence and priority shifts context to other decisions where a set of preferences are well defined and is closer to the organization most suited to make the choice.

**G186:p0056:c003 · PDF 56**

Explicitly link the architecture specification to requirements, and trace the requirements to a stakeholder/concern pair to track the value and preference. This traceability is used in governance to assess how well the design and implementation choices address the stakeholder’s value preferences.

**G186:p0056:c004 · PDF 56**

Best practice EA Repositories facilitate traceability at every step of the architecture to the direction and priorities of the Enterprise. Practitioners are delivering some of the highest value when they are engaged in requirements management and trade-off. All smart stakeholders want all, want more, and for free. All smart stakeholders know they can’t have it all, nor can they have it for free. What stakeholders don’t know – and what the role of the Practitioner is – is to assist the stakeholders in understanding what they have to give up in order to realize different sets of preferences.

**G186:p0056:c005 · PDF 56**

A Practitioner with a well-run EA Repository is in a position to maintain a comprehensive set of requirements in context. Requirements in context enable the Practitioner to work actively for the preferences of the stakeholders rather than architecting to a subset of the preferences of the stakeholders; or worse, a set of preferences that the Practitioner personally prefers.

</details>

<a id="5.1.5"></a>
## 5.1.5 运行良好的存储库：合规评估

原文：5.1.5. What to Expect in a Well-Run EA Repository: Compliance Assessments。PDF 物理页：[57, 58]。状态：`source-compared-revised`。

多数 EA 存储库缺少合规评估中最重要的组成部分：差距、架构需求规格、控制，以及回应利益相关者所关注问题的视图。运行良好的 EA 存储库既包含有效开展合规评估所需的全部组件，也包含合规评估本身。

合规评估的第一步，是明确**依据什么**评估合规。最佳实践的合规评估与 TOGAF 的架构契约概念紧密关联。架构契约识别实施项目应交付什么，以及项目运行所受的一组约束。没有清晰记录的期望与约束，实践者就辜负了实施团队。

运行良好的 EA 存储库会为每个实施项目保存一份相当于架构契约的文件；架构契约示例见附录 D。期望与约束清晰之后，才能评估合规。

TOGAF 阶段 G 识别了两个评估合规的领域：第一是项目范围；第二是实际实施，无论指设计还是绩效变化。阶段 H 还包含一项基于价值的合规评估。

阶段 G 的第一项评估，是把实施项目的范围与预期填补的差距（或工作包）相比较。工作包识别将填补哪些差距；工作包的唯一目的，是澄清处理架构差距所需的工作。作为支持组合的架构项目的一部分而开发的良好路线图，会容纳描述良好的工作包。描述良好的工作包会明确所填补的差距，以及处理差距的实施策略或方法。若某处没有架构意义，优秀的实践者不会通过实施策略用不必要的指导或约束去限制实施项目；若处理差距的方法具有架构意义，优秀的实践者总会提供适当的指导或约束。

开展范围与实施方法的合规评估，是保护价值的第一步。良好的 EA 会清晰指出为企业实现价值最大化的最佳路径。通常，企业价值最大化不会与实施项目赞助人或实施团队的局部偏好一致。坦率地说，如果一致，就不需要 EA 团队了。因此，评估实施项目的范围是保护价值的第一处关口。等到项目已获资金并启动才评估，与“决策之后再做架构”并无区别；见第 15 章。

阶段 G 的第二项合规评估，确认具体的架构需求规格是否被遵循。TOGAF 的架构需求规格概念识别什么必须存在、什么必须做、什么被禁止；它提供了一组约束，作用于更详细的架构开发、设计与实施。

阶段 H 的合规评估基于价值实现。通常，实施项目宣告成功之后，预期价值要在相当长一段时间后才会实现。利用架构契约提供的链接，可以反复开展价值实现评估。保持从规格到利益相关者期望的链接，有助于一致的评审。

尽管运行良好的 EA 存储库会聚焦于价值实现的证明，传统上大部分注意力仍放在“遵守规则”式的合规上。遵守规则固然重要，但除非假定遵守规则的价值不言自明，它往往难以一致地证明价值。“遵守规则”式评估常见于架构需求规格消除了所有设计与实施选择的情形；把评估聚焦于遵守规则，也最可能与“豁免请求”相伴，因为规则的总成本与可获价值不匹配；见第 15 章。

最佳实践不止于对条文的合规，还包括**对意图的合规**。目的仍是保护目标架构的预期价值。当一项约束与利益相关者需求相连时，合规评估就能评估设计与实施选择在多大程度上交付预期价值。表明实施将无法实现预期价值的合规评估，是后续架构开发的关键输入。


### 语义规则与适用边界

- 阶段 G 分别评估项目范围与实际实施；阶段 H 另做价值实现评估。
  - 适用边界：项目宣告完成不等于预期价值已实现。
  - PDF 57：TOGAF Phase G identifies two areas where compliance is assessed. The first is the scope of the project. Second is the actual implementation, whether designed or the performance change. Phase H contains a further value-based compliance assessment.
- 工作包明确填补的差距及有架构意义的实施方法。
  - 适用边界：没有架构意义时不添加不必要的约束。
  - PDF 57：The first assessment in Phase G considers the scope of the Implementation Project compared to the gap, or work package, expected to be filled. The work package identifies which gaps are going to be filled. The singular purpose of the work package is clarifying the work necessary to address the gaps in the architecture. Good roadmaps developed as part of an Architecture Project support portfolio will house well described work packages. Well described work packages are clear about gaps being filled, and the implementation strategy, or approach, of how the gap will be addressed. Where there is no architectural significance, no good Practitioner will bother constraining an Implementation Project with unnecessary guidance or constraint through the implementation strategy. Where the approach to addressing the gap is significant, a good Practitioner will always provide the appropriate guidance of constraint.
- 合规应包含意图及预期价值，而不止条文。
  - 适用边界：实施无法产生价值的评估应进入后续架构开发。
  - PDF 58：Best practice is to go beyond simple compliance with the statement, to include compliance with intent. The purpose is again to protect the expected value of the Target Architecture. When a constraint is connected to a stakeholder requirement, the compliance assessment is able to assess how well the design and implementation choices deliver on expected value. Compliance assessments that indicate the implementation will fail to enable expected value are key inputs to future architecture development.

### 原文内容单元

<details><summary>展开英文原文及文本块定位</summary>

**G186:p0057:c000 · PDF 57**

5.1.5. What to Expect in a Well-Run EA Repository: Compliance Assessments

**G186:p0057:c001 · PDF 57**

Most EA Repositories are missing the most important component of a compliance assessment: gaps, Architecture Requirements Specifications, controls, and views that address concerns stakeholders find interesting. A well-run EA Repository will contain all of the components necessary to perform effective compliance assessments as well as the compliance assessments.

**G186:p0057:c002 · PDF 57**

The first step of compliance assessment is clarity on what compliance will be assessed against. Best practice compliance assessments are tightly linked with the TOGAF concept of an Architecture Contract. The Architecture Contract identifies what an Implementation Project is expected to deliver and the set of constraints the project operates under. Without clearly documented expectations and constraints the Practitioner has failed the implementation team.

**G186:p0057:c003 · PDF 57**

A well-run EA Repository will contain the equivalent of an Architecture Contract for every Implementation Project. See Appendix D for an example of an Architecture Contract. With clarity on expectation and constraint, compliance may be assessed.

**G186:p0057:c004 · PDF 57**

TOGAF Phase G identifies two areas where compliance is assessed. The first is the scope of the project. Second is the actual implementation, whether designed or the performance change. Phase H contains a further value-based compliance assessment.

**G186:p0057:c005 · PDF 57**

The first assessment in Phase G considers the scope of the Implementation Project compared to the gap, or work package, expected to be filled. The work package identifies which gaps are going to be filled. The singular purpose of the work package is clarifying the work necessary to address the gaps in the architecture. Good roadmaps developed as part of an Architecture Project support portfolio will house well described work packages. Well described work packages are clear about gaps being filled, and the implementation strategy, or approach, of how the gap will be addressed. Where there is no architectural significance, no good Practitioner will bother constraining an Implementation Project with unnecessary guidance or constraint through the implementation strategy. Where the approach to addressing the gap is significant, a good Practitioner will always provide the appropriate guidance of constraint.

**G186:p0057:c006 · PDF 57**

Performing scope, and implementation approach, compliance is the first step in protecting value. A good EA will provide clarity about the best path to maximized value for the Enterprise. Typically, maximized value to the Enterprise will not align with parochial preferences of the Implementation Project sponsor, or the implementation team. Frankly, if there was alignment, there would not be a need for an EA team. It follows that assessing the scope of an Implementation Project is the first place to protect value. Waiting until the project is funded and underway is indistinct from developing architecture after the decision; see Chapter 15.

**G186:p0057:c007 · PDF 57**

The second Phase G compliance assessment confirms whether specific Architecture Requirements Specifications have been followed. The TOGAF concept of an Architecture Requirements Specification identifies what must be, what must be done, and what is prohibited. It provides the set of constraints

**G186:p0057:c008 · PDF 57**

[4]

**G186:p0057:c009 · PDF 57**

on more detailed architecture development, design, and implementation.

**G186:p0058:c000 · PDF 58**

Phase H’s compliance assessment is based on value realization. Typically, expected value will not be realized for a significant period of time after an Implementation Project has declared victory. Using the linkage provided by the Architecture Contract, recurrent value realization assessments can be performed. Maintaining the linkage from specification to stakeholder expectation facilitates consistent review.

**G186:p0058:c001 · PDF 58**

Although a well-run EA Repository will be focused on demonstration of realizing value, traditionally most attention is placed on rule-following compliance. While rule-following is important, it tends to struggle with a consistent demonstration of value, unless it is assumed the value of following the rule is self-evident. Rule-following compliance assessment is common where the Architecture Requirements Specification eliminates all design and implementation choice. Focusing assessment on rule-following is also most likely to be tied to requests for relief from the rule because the total cost of the rule is not in alignment with available value; see Chapter 15.

**G186:p0058:c002 · PDF 58**

Best practice is to go beyond simple compliance with the statement, to include compliance with intent. The purpose is again to protect the expected value of the Target Architecture. When a constraint is connected to a stakeholder requirement, the compliance assessment is able to assess how well the design and implementation choices deliver on expected value. Compliance assessments that indicate the implementation will fail to enable expected value are key inputs to future architecture development.

</details>

<a id="5.2"></a>
## 5.2 ADM 迭代在实践中如何实现？

原文：5.2. How is ADM Iteration Realized in Practice?。PDF 物理页：[58, 59]。状态：`source-compared-revised`。

TOGAF 框架中常被误解的一个要素是 ADM 与迭代概念。TOGAF ADM 图是一种程式化的表示，常被误读为线性的瀑布式过程模型，由此产生了许多令人困惑的图和解释。TOGAF ADM 是一种逻辑方法，它把关键活动步骤放在一起，目的是理解活动之间的关系、澄清信息流。经典的 TOGAF “麦田圈”图是一条程式化路径，用来展示关键信息流。

不应把 TOGAF ADM 理解为过程模型。ADM 图是展示关键信息流的程式化表示，不是活动顺序的表示。

要认识到的要点是：EA 团队在 ADM 范围内开展任何活动时，都是在执行某个阶段，并在开发 EA 全景的内容。例如，实践者做路线图开发时，就是在执行 TOGAF ADM 阶段 E（机会与解决方案）的步骤，需要消费强制输入并产生强制输出。所有 ADM 阶段皆然。

首先应认识到，开发目标架构具有相互依赖的性质：必须同时考虑整个架构、由此产生的差距，以及消除差距所需的工作。实践者不能只考虑某一项变更，而不考虑它对所有其他领域的影响、由此产生的差距集合，以及消除差距的工作集合。

不幸的是，描述这种程度的交互并不现实。为应对复杂性，TOGAF 框架为每项关键输出提供一个 ADM 阶段。最佳实践确保实践者使用有效的信息输入并产生有用的输出。

实践者被要求开发的内容不同，其工作计划的架构路径也不同。考虑下列请求会用到 ADM 的哪些阶段：

1. 在组织设计、客户界面与流程保持不变的前提下，还有哪些变更可以实现“迁移到云”？
2. 从 50 多个各自追逐小项目的独立组织，转变为一家能够组织并控制比当前平均规模大 100 倍的建设项目的一体化公司，需要哪些变更？
3. 核心理赔平台需要哪些变更，才能支撑客户与交易量增长 300%，并支持保单条款的持续变更？
4. 在保留 ERP 与现行财务和人力资源流程的前提下，支持把人工分配到资本项目所需的最小变更是什么？
5. 如何以最小变更完成并购整合，同时保持现有高效率流程与并购带来的独特能力？
6. 如何在客户亲密度项目上支持第三方开发者的敏捷方法与微服务？
7. 如何在不影响 IT 之外任何人的前提下实现某个平台的现代化？

这些请求都曾用 TOGAF 框架与技术解决。每个请求都始于不同的目的，各自走过一条不同的路径，使用了不同配置的 TOGAF ADM。

唯一的例外是阶段 A：实践者必须从阶段 A 开始。架构项目必须被启动。


### 语义规则与适用边界

- ADM 图表达必要信息流，不是固定活动顺序。
  - 适用边界：配置执行路径仍须提供阶段必要输入和输出；不能把非线性当成任意省略。
  - PDF 58：The TOGAF ADM should not be understood as a processes model. The ADM graphic is a stylized representation showing essential information flows and is not a representation of activity sequence.
- 本指南要求架构开发项目从阶段 A 的启动与设置开始。
  - 适用边界：并非每次细节活动都重建一整轮 A；与第 5.2.3 节既有输出复用结合理解。
  - PDF 59：The only exception is Phase A; the Practitioner must start with Phase A. An Architecture Project must be initiated.

### 原文内容单元

<details><summary>展开英文原文及文本块定位</summary>

**G186:p0058:c003 · PDF 58**

5.2. How is ADM Iteration Realized in Practice?

**G186:p0058:c004 · PDF 58**

An often-misunderstood element of the TOGAF framework is the ADM and the concept of iteration. The TOGAF ADM graphic provides a stylized representation that is often misinterpreted as a linear waterfall process model. This approach leads to some of the most confusing diagrams and explanations. The TOGAF ADM is a logical method that places key activity steps together for the purpose of understanding relationship of activity and clarifying information flow. The classic TOGAF crop-circle diagram is a stylized path that demonstrates essential information flow.

**G186:p0058:c005 · PDF 58**

The TOGAF ADM should not be understood as a processes model. The ADM graphic is a stylized representation showing essential information flows and is not a representation of activity sequence.

**G186:p0058:c006 · PDF 58**

The important thing to realize is every time the EA team is undertaking any activity within the scope of the ADM it is executing a Phase and developing the contents of the EA Landscape. For example, if a Practitioner is working on roadmap development, the Practitioner is exercising the steps in the TOGAF ADM Phase E (Opportunities and Solutions). The Practitioner needs to consume the mandatory inputs and produce the mandatory outputs. This applies to all ADM phases.

**G186:p0058:c007 · PDF 58**

Start with recognizing that the inter-dependent nature of developing a Target Architecture requires considering the entire architecture, resulting gaps, and resulting work to clear the gap simultaneously. No Practitioner can consider a change, without considering the impact on all other domains, the resulting set of gaps, and the resulting set of work to clear the gap.

**G186:p0059:c000 · PDF 59**

Unfortunately, describing that level of interaction is not practical. To address the complexity, the TOGAF framework provides an ADM phase for each essential output. Best practice ensures Practitioners use effective information inputs and produce useful outputs.

**G186:p0059:c001 · PDF 59**

Depending on what a Practitioner is requested to develop, an architecture for the Practitioner’s work plan will vary. Consider the impact on which phases of the ADM would be used for the following requests:

**G186:p0059:c002 · PDF 59**

1. Given that the organizational design, customer interface, and processes are to be left unmodified, what other changes would allow “moving to the cloud”?

**G186:p0059:c003 · PDF 59**

2. What changes are required to switch from more than 50 independent organizations pursuing small projects, to an integrated company capable of organizing, and controlling, construction projects 100 times larger than the current average?

**G186:p0059:c004 · PDF 59**

3. What changes are required to the core claims platform to allow a 300% growth in customers and transactions, and enable continuous change to policy terms?

**G186:p0059:c005 · PDF 59**

4. Given that the ERP and current Finance & HR processes will be kept, what are the minimum changes to support allocating labor to capital projects?

**G186:p0059:c006 · PDF 59**

5. How to integrate the acquisition with the minimum change, while sustaining both the current high- efficiency processes and the unique capability from the acquisition?

**G186:p0059:c007 · PDF 59**

6. How to enable a third-party developer’s agile approach, and Microservices, on the customer intimacy project?

**G186:p0059:c008 · PDF 59**

7. How to modernize a particular platform without impacting anyone outside IT?

**G186:p0059:c009 · PDF 59**

Each of these requests has been addressed using the TOGAF framework, and the techniques. Each started with a different purpose, and each traversed a distinct path that used a different configuration of the TOGAF ADM.

**G186:p0059:c010 · PDF 59**

The only exception is Phase A; the Practitioner must start with Phase A. An Architecture Project must be initiated.

</details>

<a id="5.2.1"></a>
## 5.2.1 阶段 A：起点

原文：5.2.1. Phase A: The Starting Point。PDF 物理页：[59, 60]。状态：`source-compared-revised`。

所有架构开发都必须从阶段 A 开始。缺少阶段 A 固有的设置，实践者可以预期工作会偏离航道，无法交付有用的架构。

阶段 A 的设置要点是：

- **确定架构项目的范围。**
  你在解决什么问题？就 EA 全景（广度与规划期）和目的而言——目的往往会确认所需的详细程度。要彻底清楚该架构将在业务周期的哪个位置使用。

- **识别利益相关者、关注点及相关需求。**
  在 EA 存储库中查找上层架构的约束与指导；完成利益相关者地图；彻底清楚必须服务哪些利益相关者、他们担心什么。

- **评估 EA 团队的能力。**
  严格审视 EA 团队，确认团队交付本架构开发项目的能力。好的 EA 团队能弥补经验、技能与偏见上的缺口，交付有用的架构，克服少数成员的弱点。

阶段 A 的完成要点是：

- **关键利益相关者就目标摘要以及达成目标的工作达成一致。**
  在所有领域开展足够的架构开发，使你能够向关键利益相关者沟通：被指派的问题可以如何解决，以及达到他们所述偏好所需变更的范围。要清楚目标、目标的价值以及变更工作。

坦率地说，阶段 A 经常被跳过或草草了事。优秀的实践者知道，在开展任何详细工作之前，关键利益相关者已就摘要目标、价值与变更工作量达成一致。如果关键利益相关者一开始就不同意，那么在实践者做了大量工作、详细说明他们不想要什么、交付了不足的价值、或者不愿同意变更之后，他们也不太可能同意。

完成阶段 A 的输出需要探索所有领域——无论探索是为了理解应该改变什么，还是在不能改变的地方确定保留现状架构的影响。

如果在初步探索之后出现多个潜在目标，实践者不必惊讶。对关键利益相关者而言，有不止一种解决问题的方法是可以接受的，这有助于在更详细分析时进行更好的权衡。请记住：在目标最终确定之前，实践者是在探索最好的潜在未来，而不是在推销某个特定未来。


### 语义规则与适用边界

- 详细工作前，关键利益相关者须就目标摘要、价值与达成目标的工作达成一致。
  - 适用边界：需跨所有领域探查，包括不能改变的领域对方案的影响。
  - PDF 60：Perform sufficient architecture development in all domains to enable you to communicate to the key stakeholders how the problem you have been assigned can be addressed and the scope of change to reach their articulated preferences. Be clear on the target, the value of the target, and the work to change.
- 初步探索允许多个候选目标，以支持后续权衡。
  - 适用边界：确定目标前不得把探索变成推销已选定方案。
  - PDF 60：Practitioners should not be surprised if there are multiple potential targets after the initial exploration. Having more than one approach to addressing the problem is acceptable to key stakeholders. It facilitates better trade-off when performing more detailed analysis. Keep in mind that until the target is finalized, the Practitioner is exploring the best potential future, not selling a particular future.

### 原文内容单元

<details><summary>展开英文原文及文本块定位</summary>

**G186:p0059:c011 · PDF 59**

5.2.1. Phase A: The Starting Point

**G186:p0059:c012 · PDF 59**

All architecture development needs to start with Phase A. Without the set-up inherent in Phase A Practitioners can expect to slide off-course and fail to deliver useful architecture.

**G186:p0059:c013 · PDF 59**

The set-up essentials of Phase A are:

**G186:p0059:c014 · PDF 59**

• Define the scope of the Architecture Project

**G186:p0059:c015 · PDF 59**

What problem are you solving? In terms of the EA Landscape (breadth and planning-horizon) and in terms of purpose, which will tend to confirm the necessary level of detail? Be completely clear where in the business cycle this architecture will be used.

**G186:p0060:c000 · PDF 60**

• Identify stakeholders, concerns, and associated requirements

**G186:p0060:c001 · PDF 60**

Explore the EA Repository for superior architecture constraints and guidance. Do the Stakeholder Map. Be completely clear which stakeholders must be served and what they are worrying about.

**G186:p0060:c002 · PDF 60**

• Assess the capability of the EA team

**G186:p0060:c003 · PDF 60**

Take a hard look at the EA team and confirm the ability of the team to deliver on this architecture development project. A good EA team covers gaps in experience, skill, and bias to deliver the architecture that is useful, overcoming weaknesses of few members of the team.

**G186:p0060:c004 · PDF 60**

The completion essentials of Phase A:

**G186:p0060:c005 · PDF 60**

• Key stakeholder agreement on a summary of the target and the work to reach the target

**G186:p0060:c006 · PDF 60**

Perform sufficient architecture development in all domains to enable you to communicate to the key stakeholders how the problem you have been assigned can be addressed and the scope of change to reach their articulated preferences. Be clear on the target, the value of the target, and the work to change.

**G186:p0060:c007 · PDF 60**

Frankly, Phase A is routinely skipped, or skimmed. Good Practitioners know the key stakeholders agree on the summary target, the value, and the effort of change before any detailed work is undertaken. If key stakeholders won’t agree at the outset, they are unlikely to agree after the Practitioners have performed a lot of work detailing what they do not want, delivered insufficient value, or will not agree to change.

**G186:p0060:c008 · PDF 60**

Completing the outputs of Phase A requires exploring all of the domains – whether the exploration is to understand what should change, or where change is not an option to determine the impact of retaining current architecture.

**G186:p0060:c009 · PDF 60**

Practitioners should not be surprised if there are multiple potential targets after the initial exploration. Having more than one approach to addressing the problem is acceptable to key stakeholders. It facilitates better trade-off when performing more detailed analysis. Keep in mind that until the target is finalized, the Practitioner is exploring the best potential future, not selling a particular future.

</details>

<a id="5.2.2"></a>
## 5.2.2 关键的 ADM 输出与知识

原文：5.2.2. Essential ADM Output and Knowledge。PDF 物理页：[60, 61, 62]。状态：`source-compared-revised`。

表 5-2 汇总了关键成果与输出。请记住，关键输出是利益相关者、赞助人以及老板的老板的老板想要的东西。没有人想要一份架构；他们想要的是关于规划和执行有效变革的指导。实践者用架构化的方法，提供关于有效变革的最佳可用指导。关键成果与输出源自阶段目标——即说明实践者为何要开展这项活动。

企业所看重和消费的，通常不同于实践者所生产的。实践者交付一项关键输出，以视图、路线图、架构规格、控制及其他有用之物的形式提供。架构被开发出来，EA 全景被填充。为此，实践者需要一套关键知识；企业消费的是关于变革的有效指导，以及治理变革的能力。

请结合表 5-1 阅读表 5-2，以确认对于特定目的而言，某阶段的输出是已经存在、需要创建，还是与当前架构项目无关。优秀的实践者会据此调整工作。表 5-2 只列出关键输出与成果；完整清单请参阅 TOGAF 标准。为实现这些成果，实践者可能需要开展比下表更多的活动，或创建更多交付物。其意图是让关注点保持在“追求什么”，而不是“做了什么”。

表 5-2：关键的 ADM 输出、成果与知识

| 阶段 | 输出与成果 | 关键知识 |
|---|---|---|
| 阶段 A：架构愿景 | 足以获得继续推进许可的文档。获得许可去开发目标架构，以验证摘要目标。 | 所解决问题范围。其利益对所解决问题具有根本重要性的人（利益相关者与关注点）。利益相关者可接受的摘要答案（架构愿景）。利益相关者优先级与偏好。摘要答案提供什么价值。 |
| 阶段 B、C、D | 一组针对所解决问题的领域架构，经利益相关者批准，并附差距及消除差距的工作，且利益相关者理解这些内容。 | 当前企业为何未能满足利益相关者偏好。必须改变什么（差距）。实现变更所需工作，且与所创造的价值一致（工作包）。利益相关者优先级与偏好如何随价值、工作量和风险调整（利益相关者需求）。 |
| 阶段 E：机会与解决方案 | 一组处理差距的工作包，标明产生的价值与所需工作量，以及工作包之间的依赖关系，以达到调整后的目标。 | 变更集合之间的依赖（工作包与差距依赖）。每项变更与工作包的价值、工作量和风险。利益相关者优先级与偏好如何随变革的价值、工作量与风险调整。 |
| 阶段 F：实施与迁移规划 | 一组经批准的项目，包含目标及必要约束、所需资源、开始与结束日期。 | 可用于开展变革的资源。利益相关者优先级与偏好如何随变革的价值、工作量与风险调整（利益相关者需求）。 |
| 阶段 G：实施治理 | 完成实施必要变更的项目，达到调整后的目标状态。 | 实施团队的目的与约束（差距、架构需求规格、控制）。利益相关者优先级与偏好如何随成功、价值、工作量和风险调整（利益相关者需求）。 |
| 阶段 H：架构变更管理 | 继续推进的方向，并开始开发目标架构，以处理企业相对于利益相关者偏好的已感知、真实或预期不足。 | 已批准目标或偏好与先前工作实现之间的差距（价值实现）。偏好或优先级的变化（利益相关者需求）。 |

相关结构化资料：[source-tables](../tables/G186.json)。本节表格全文已对照；独立结构化表保留单元格与位置。

### 语义规则与适用边界

- 表 5-2 仅列关键成果；应结合表 5-1 判断已有、待建和当前无关的输出。
  - 适用边界：不是 TOGAF 的穷尽交付物清单，为达到成果可能需更多活动和交付物。
  - PDF 61：Read Table 5-2 in conjunction with Table 5-1 to confirm whether for a particular purpose the output of the phase is already in existence, needs to be created, or is extraneous to the current Architecture Project. Good Practitioners will adjust their work accordingly. Table 5-2 lists only key outputs and outcomes. For an exhaustive list, refer to the TOGAF Standard. In order to achieve these outcomes, the Practitioner may have to perform more activities or create more deliverables than those listed in the table below. The intent is to keep the focus on what is pursued, not what is done.
- 阶段 G 的完成项目是要支持实现的成果，阶段 H 以实际价值缺口触发后续工作。
  - 适用边界：不据此把实施者的执行责任转给架构师。
  - PDF 62：| Phase | Output & Outcome | Essential Knowledge |
| --- | --- | --- |
| Phase F:<br>Implementation and<br>Migration Plan | An approved set of projects,[5]<br>containing the objective and any<br>necessary constraints, resources<br>required, and start and finish<br>dates. | Resources available to undertake the change.<br>How stakeholder priority and preference adjust in<br>response to value, effort, and risk of change. (Stakeholder<br>Requirements) |
| Phase G:<br>Implementation<br>Governance | Completion of the projects to<br>implement the changes necessary<br>to reach the adjusted target state. | Purpose and constraints on the implementation team.<br>(Gap, Architecture Requirement Specification, Control)<br>How stakeholder priority and preference adjust in<br>response to success, value, effort, and risk of change.<br>(Stakeholder Requirements) |
| Phase H: Architecture<br>Change Management | Direction to proceed and start<br>developing a Target Architecture<br>that addresses perceived, real, or<br>anticipated shortfalls in the<br>Enterprise relative to stakeholder<br>preferences. | Gaps between approved target, or preference, and<br>realization from prior work. (Value Realization)<br>Changes in preference or priority. (Stakeholder<br>Requirements) |

5.2.2. Essential ADM Output and Knowledge

**G186:p0060:c011 · PDF 60**

A summary of the essential outcome and output is provided in Table 5-2. Keep in mind that the essential output is what stakeholders, sponsor, and boss’ boss’ boss wants. No-one wants an architecture; they want guidance on planning and executing an effective change. Practitioners use an architected approach to providing the best available guidance on effective change. The essential outcomes and outputs are derived from the objectives of the phase – the statement of why a Practitioner should perform this activity.

**G186:p0060:c012 · PDF 60**

What the Enterprise values and consumes is typically different than what the Practitioner produces. Practitioners deliver an essential output. It is provided as views, roadmaps, architecture specifications, controls, and other useful things. Architecture is developed, and the EA Landscape populated. To do

**G186:p0061:c000 · PDF 61**

this, Practitioners require a set of essential knowledge. The Enterprise consumes effective guidance about and the ability to govern change.

In order to achieve these outcomes, the Practitioner may have to perform more activities or create more deliverables than those listed in the table below. The intent is to keep the focus on what is pursued, not what is done.

<a id="5.2.3"></a>
## 5.2.3 迭代

原文：5.2.3. Iteration。PDF 物理页：[62, 63, 64]。状态：`source-compared-revised`。

ADM 提供了一种活动模型：通过产生一个或多个工作产品来支持产生关键输出。核心问题决定某个架构项目是否需要某阶段的关键目的。如果需要，你将在某个时点进入该阶段；如果不需要或已经处理过，则该架构项目不进入该阶段。

TOGAF 标准中关于 ADM 迭代的多数论述，都是为了说明一点：如果实践者手边没有 EA 全景中的信息，就必须把信息生产出来。这些论述谈的是活动而不是输出。与其从“重排和循环 ADM”的角度理解迭代，实践者应当探索 EA 全景：如果所需信息（就主题、详细程度、时间和时效性而言）已经具备，就继续推进；如果没有，就生产所需材料。生产材料，就是在执行某个 TOGAF ADM 阶段。

例如，参见图 5-5 的程式化甘特图。该图提供了执行 ADM 的流程视角。甘特图表明，EA 的相互依赖性质要求：所有开发候选架构并测试其可接受性的 ADM 阶段必须同时打开。这些阶段保持打开以满足所需信息；信息一旦提供，阶段就关闭。此外，无论实践者处于什么时间点、什么目的或哪个架构项目，只要在开发业务架构，就是在执行阶段 B。执行阶段 B 就是：从业务架构领域的视角处理利益相关者关注点，识别业务架构中的差距，并审视对整个 EA 全景的影响。该图强调，ADM 阶段中的许多步骤可以同时执行。优秀的实践者会探索整个架构的影响，并处理利益相关者关注点。

〔图示：图 5-5：程式化的架构开发甘特图；参阅本节结构化引用。〕



考虑不同目的以及随时间级联的情形，如图 4-1 所示。把图 5-5 的程式化甘特计划应用到每个目的上，就会清楚看到：实践者会在适当的详细程度上不断重访所需阶段。

大多数常规的问题解决模型提供带步骤关卡的线性方法。线性方法有助于我们理解过程，也可能代表业务周期的阶段关卡；但它们并不代表人实际解决问题的过程。图 5-6 源自 Jeff Conklin《Dialog Mapping 中的棘手问题与社会复杂性》（2005），勾勒了标准的线性问题解决进程，以及专业人员通常如何应对问题。交互式地测试概念与潜在实现是最佳实践：反复考虑高层方向在执行上是否合理，以及执行在高层方向上是否合理。

〔图示：图 5-6：问题解决方法（源自 Conklin 的棘手问题）；参阅本节结构化引用。〕



所有迭代都由当前项目的信息需求驱动。所形成的流程并不取决于 EA 能力承担的工作，而取决于完成的时点。关键问题是 EA 能力必须在何时交付特定的工作产品。表 5-1 汇总了被企业关键流程积极消费的工作产品。

相关结构化资料：[figure-5-5](../figures/G186/fig5-5.json)。本节核对图注及正文语义；图内标签、拓扑和甘特任务的完整审校状态以独立图记录为准，本轮正文覆盖不会自动升级。
相关结构化资料：[figure-5-6](../figures/G186/fig5-6.json)。本节核对图注及正文语义；图内标签、拓扑和甘特任务的完整审校状态以独立图记录为准，本轮正文覆盖不会自动升级。

### 语义规则与适用边界

- 只有当前项目需要且尚未满足某阶段目的时，才进入该阶段。
  - 适用边界：本书同时强调 A 的项目启动；“已处理”必须有符合范围、细节、时间与时效性的信息依据。
  - PDF 62：The ADM provides a model of activity that supports producing the essential output by producing one or more work products. The central question determines whether there is a need for the essential purpose of a phase on a particular Architecture Project. If so, you will enter the phase at some point in time. If the essential purpose is not needed or has already been addressed, then this Architecture Project does not enter the phase.
- 候选开发与接受测试的相关阶段可同时开放，以解决跨域相互依赖。
  - 适用边界：不是一个人包办所有阶段；工作在信息满足后关闭，不按瀑布门逐个等待。
  - PDF 62：As an example, see the stylized Gantt chart in Figure 5-5. This figure provides a process-oriented view of executing the ADM. The Gantt shows the inter-dependent nature of EA requires all ADM phases that develop a candidate architecture and test it for acceptance to be open simultaneously. The ADM phases stay open to address the information required; once it is provided they close. Also, regardless of where the Practitioner is in time or purpose or Architecture Project, if the Business Architecture is being developed the Practitioner is executing Phase B. Executing Phase B is all about addressing the stakeholder concerns from the perspective of the Business Architecture domain, identifying the gaps in the Business Architecture, and looking at impacts across the EA Landscape. The figure highlights that many of the steps in the ADM phases can be executed simultaneously. Good Practitioners will explore
- 迭代由当前项目的信息需要和工作产品交付时点驱动。
  - 适用边界：方法图的循环次数不是成果或成熟度指标。
  - PDF 64：All iteration is driven by the information needs of the current project. The process created is not dependent upon the work the EA Capability undertakes to produce, but the timing of completion. The essential question is when an EA Capability must deliver specific work products. Table 5-1 provides a summary of work products that are actively consumed by key Enterprise processes.

5.2.3. Iteration

**G186:p0062:c002 · PDF 62**

The ADM provides a model of activity that supports producing the essential output by producing one or more work products. The central question determines whether there is a need for the essential purpose of a phase on a particular Architecture Project. If so, you will enter the phase at some point in time. If the essential purpose is not needed or has already been addressed, then this Architecture Project does not enter the phase.

**G186:p0062:c003 · PDF 62**

Most commentary in the TOGAF Standard on the iteration of the ADM is designed to address the point that if the Practitioner does not have the information at hand in the EA Landscape, the information must be produced. These commentaries speak in terms of activity rather than output. Instead of considering iteration in terms of re-sequencing and looping the ADM, the Practitioner should explore the EA Landscape. If the information required, in terms of subject, detail, time, and recency is available – move on. If not, produce the material required. To produce material, the Practitioner is exercising a TOGAF ADM phase.

**G186:p0062:c004 · PDF 62**

As an example, see the stylized Gantt chart in Figure 5-5. This figure provides a process-oriented view of executing the ADM. The Gantt shows the inter-dependent nature of EA requires all ADM phases that develop a candidate architecture and test it for acceptance to be open simultaneously. The ADM phases stay open to address the information required; once it is provided they close. Also, regardless of where the Practitioner is in time or purpose or Architecture Project, if the Business Architecture is being developed the Practitioner is executing Phase B. Executing Phase B is all about addressing the stakeholder concerns from the perspective of the Business Architecture domain, identifying the gaps in the Business Architecture, and looking at impacts across the EA Landscape. The figure highlights that many of the steps in the ADM phases can be executed simultaneously. Good Practitioners will explore

**G186:p0062:c005 · PDF 62**

[6]

**G186:p0062:c006 · PDF 62**

impacts and address stakeholder concerns across the entire architecture.

**G186:p0063:c000 · PDF 63**

Figure 5-5: Stylized Architecture Development Gantt Chart

**G186:p0063:c001 · PDF 63**

Consider the different purposes and a cascade through time as shown in Figure 4-1. When the plan in the stylized Gant chart in Figure 5-5 is applied to each purpose, it becomes clear that the Practitioner continually revisits the required phases, at the appropriate level of detail.

**G186:p0063:c002 · PDF 63**

Most of the normal problem-solving models provide linear approaches with step gates. The linear approach helps us understand the process, and may represent the business cycle stage gates. However, they do not represent how people actually solve problems. Figure 5-6 is derived from Jeff Conklin’s Wicked Problems & Social Complexity within Dialog Mapping [Conklin, 2005], and outlines a standard linear problem solving progression and how professionals typically address a problem. Testing the concept and potential implementation interactively is a best practice. Iteratively considering whether the high-level direction makes sense in terms of execution, and does execution make sense in terms of high-level direction?

**G186:p0063:c003 · PDF 63**

Figure 5-6: Problem Solving Approach (Derived from Conklin’s Wicked Problems)

**G186:p0064:c000 · PDF 64**

All iteration is driven by the information needs of the current project. The process created is not dependent upon the work the EA Capability undertakes to produce, but the timing of completion. The essential question is when an EA Capability must deliver specific work products. Table 5-1 provides a summary of work products that are actively consumed by key Enterprise processes.

</details>

## 5.2.7 支持解决方案交付的架构的 ADM 计划

- 对齐实施者
- 指导交付
- 实现解决方案

这些过程在 ADM 中迭代，交付促进解决方案交付的架构（关于企业架构与解决方案架构区别的讨论，见 4.1.4）。该架构用于约束变更如何被设计和交付：它澄清目的、差距与预期价值，以约束所有设计与实施；它提供用于测试符合性的控制与架构需求；它直接促进价值实现语境下的实施与运营变更治理。

  - 适用边界：实现解决方案不等于项目团队已经通过合规或价值验收。

It directly facilitates governance of implementation and operational change in the context of value realization.
