<a id="5.2.8"></a>
## 5.2.8 迭代结论

原文：5.2.8. Iteration Conclusion。PDF 物理页：[72]。状态：`source-compared-revised`。

在本章开头，本文档提出许多实践者把 TOGAF ADM 理解为过程模型。如果你曾这样理解并仍然持此观念，请停下来想一想。经典的 TOGAF ADM 图不是活动图。TOGAF ADM 是一种逻辑方法，把关键活动步骤放在一起，以便把活动与信息流连接起来产生特定输出。

要认识到的要点是：实践者在 ADM 范围内开展任何活动，都是在开发 EA 全景的内容，都是通过迭代开发 EA 全景；所执行的阶段就是相应的领域。如果你仍执意把 ADM 排成一次性线性顺序，就会画出怪异的循环阶段图。把步骤当作检查清单。


### 语义规则与适用边界

- 把 ADM 步骤作为检查清单，并按工作所属领域理解正在执行的阶段。
  - 适用边界：这不意味着丢弃信息依赖、治理或阶段目标。
  - PDF 72：The important thing to realize is every time a Practitioner undertakes any activity within the scope of the ADM it is developing the contents of the EA Landscape. It is developing the EA Landscape through iteration. The phase being executed is the appropriate domain. If you remain stuck on trying to put the ADM in a one-pass linear order, you will draw bizarre looping phase diagrams. Think of the steps as a checklist.

### 原文内容单元

<details><summary>展开英文原文及文本块定位</summary>

**G186:p0072:c000 · PDF 72**

5.2.8. Iteration Conclusion

**G186:p0072:c001 · PDF 72**

At the start of this chapter, this document suggested that many Practitioners interpret the TOGAF ADM as a process model. If you did and continue to carry that notion, stop and think. The classic TOGAF diagram of the ADM is not an activity diagram. The TOGAF ADM is a logical method that places key activity steps together for the purpose of linking activity and information flow to produce specific outputs.

**G186:p0072:c002 · PDF 72**

The important thing to realize is every time a Practitioner undertakes any activity within the scope of the ADM it is developing the contents of the EA Landscape. It is developing the EA Landscape through iteration. The phase being executed is the appropriate domain. If you remain stuck on trying to put the ADM in a one-pass linear order, you will draw bizarre looping phase diagrams. Think of the steps as a checklist.

</details>

<a id="5.3"></a>
## 5.3 在上层架构的语境中运行

原文：5.3. Operating in the Context of Superior Architecture。PDF 物理页：[72]。状态：`source-compared-revised`。

上层架构始终指导并约束更详细架构的开发。简而言之，上层架构是在广度上与之重叠的、较不详细的已批准目标。这一简明概括会因上层架构在 EA 全景中实际所处的不同状态而变得复杂。

上层架构可能无法在详细程度、广度、规划期与时效性上完全对齐。此外，上层架构可能处于现状、过渡与目标状态的某种混合之中。

实践者必须把上层架构视为当前架构开发的指导与约束。利益相关者已经批准了 EA 全景中的上层架构；除非发生实质性变化，实践者应把先前工作当作基石，在其上构建当前工作。

若发生实质性变化，当前架构项目以及对上层架构的变更，都必须通过治理流程获得适当批准并发布。


### 语义规则与适用边界

- 实质性变化要求当前项目及上层架构变更都经适当治理批准并发布。
  - 适用边界：不能用下层项目自行批准来覆盖上层利益相关者授权。
  - PDF 72：Where there is a material change, both the current Architecture Project and the changes to the superior architecture must be properly approved and published through the governance process.

### 原文内容单元

<details><summary>展开英文原文及文本块定位</summary>

**G186:p0072:c003 · PDF 72**

5.3. Operating in the Context of Superior Architecture

**G186:p0072:c004 · PDF 72**

The superior architecture always guides and constrains the development of more detailed architecture. As a quick summary, superior architecture is the less detailed approved target that overlaps in terms of breadth. This quick summary is complicated by the different states the superior architecture may actually exist in the EA Landscape.

**G186:p0072:c005 · PDF 72**

The superior architecture may not perfectly align to detail, breadth, time-horizon, and recency. Further, the superior architecture may be in some mixture of current, transition, and target state.

**G186:p0072:c006 · PDF 72**

Practitioners must treat the superior architecture as guides and constraints to current architecture development. Stakeholders have already approved the superior architecture in the EA Landscape; barring a material change, the Practitioner accepts prior work as cornerstones to build a current workaround.

**G186:p0072:c007 · PDF 72**

Where there is a material change, both the current Architecture Project and the changes to the superior architecture must be properly approved and published through the governance process.

</details>

<a id="5.4"></a>
## 5.4 管理多种状态（候选、现状、过渡与目标）

原文：5.4. Managing Multiple States (Candidate, Current, Transition, and Target)。PDF 物理页：[72, 73]。状态：`source-compared-revised`。

实践者必须沿两个特征跟踪过渡状态：其一是时间，其二是符合性测试。理论上，用过渡来跟踪价值驻留点与符合性变化可能更好。良好实践是架构到**价值驻留状态**——即如果所有变革活动暂停，企业仍能获得价值的状态。然而，预算周期的压力迫使我们把时间作为务实的过渡标记。跟踪符合性变化有助于实施项目与运营变革治理。应尽可能减少过渡状态。

考虑过渡状态时，实践者需要记住架构需求规格与已实现系统之间的区别。把 EA 存储库当作 CMDB，会混淆实施记录与架构。实践者必须记住，许多实施或运营变更并不具有架构意义。关于开发和使用架构所涉及的不同角色，见第 15 章。


### 语义规则与适用边界

- 过渡状态按时间和符合性测试跟踪，优先形成暂停变革仍可产生价值的驻留状态。
  - 适用边界：预算时间标记是实务约束；尽可能减少状态，不能把所有配置记录都当过渡架构。
  - PDF 72：The Practitioner must track transition states across two characteristics: the first being time, and the second being a conformance test. Theoretically, it might be preferable to use transitions to track the value resting places and changes in conformance. Good practice is to architect to value resting states; a state where the Enterprise can receive value if all change activity is suspended. However, the pressure of the budget cycle forces us to use time as a pragmatic transition marker. Tracking to change in conformance facilitates the Implementation Project and operational change governance. To the extent possible, minimize transition states.

### 原文内容单元

<details><summary>展开英文原文及文本块定位</summary>

**G186:p0072:c008 · PDF 72**

5.4. Managing Multiple States (Candidate, Current,

**G186:p0072:c009 · PDF 72**

Transition, and Target)

**G186:p0072:c010 · PDF 72**

The Practitioner must track transition states across two characteristics: the first being time, and the second being a conformance test. Theoretically, it might be preferable to use transitions to track the value resting places and changes in conformance. Good practice is to architect to value resting states; a state where the Enterprise can receive value if all change activity is suspended. However, the pressure of the budget cycle forces us to use time as a pragmatic transition marker. Tracking to change in conformance facilitates the Implementation Project and operational change governance. To the extent possible, minimize transition states.

**G186:p0072:c011 · PDF 72**

When considering transition states, the Practitioner needs to keep in mind the distinction between an Architecture Requirements Specification and an implemented system. Using the EA Repository as a

**G186:p0073:c000 · PDF 73**

CMDB confuses implementation record keeping and architecture. Practitioners have to keep in mind that many implementations or operational changes are not architecturally significant. See Chapter 15 for a discussion of the different roles involved in developing and using architecture.

</details>

<a id="5.5"></a>
## 5.5 ABB 在哪里？

原文：5.5. Where are ABBs?。PDF 物理页：[73, 74]。状态：`source-compared-revised`。

TOGAF 的架构构建块（ABB）概念是高效实践者的朋友。好的 ABB 有助于上市时间与完整性。与大多数 TOGAF 定义一样，知道 ABB 是“描述整体模型某一方面的架构模型的组成部分”，并不能立即告诉我们它在 EA 存储库中长什么样。

ABB 的外观取决于它必须是什么，才能描述整体架构的某一部分——试图仔细定义这一概念的内容与结构，会在 EA 全景可描述内容的可变性与范围面前失败。构建块是更大整体的一部分，它加速对候选架构的有效描述。

在某些情况下，它是对架构某一部分的可复用描述；再次使用它，实践者就能直接采用已知成功的方式来解决问题。此时 ABB 在所有方面都是完整的，提供完整描述以及处理重复需求的约束。在另一些情况下，它没有预定义的约束与规格；此时描述的组成部分是完整的，但细节会随需求而变化。

尾注

[1] 本文件以及同一批作者的其他文章在若干处给出了关于有效架构实践的非常……的论断〔原句缺词；未补定缺失形容词〕。这些论断源自作者与评审者的经验。收集、维护并分析毫无意义的信息，与为错误目的建立 EA 团队并无二致。最终，这将对 EA 团队造成致命后果。

[2] 本文件此前使用了“终态”（end state）一词。现实中，除非企业终止运营，否则企业不存在终态。本指南还使用“未来状态”（future state）来表示实现并体验改进所需经过的时间。自此之后，本文件将使用“目标状态”（target state），以表明它是企业努力实现的可预见的最佳情形。一旦实现，同样的权衡概念与方法可应用于新的场景，或对其进行微调。

[3] 美国生产力与质量中心（APQC）；参见：www.apqc.org。

[4] 架构需求规格可以按不同详细程度并以多种方式交付。为清晰起见，本文件区分了用于满足利益相关者需求的架构规格与用于应对风险的控制。这种语义区分用于评估价值。通常，利益相关者需求带来上行收益，而风险带来下行损失。本文件通常将架构规格分为四类：原则（Principle），用于就如何看待该决策提供指导；模式（Pattern），用于提供可复用的决策方法；标准（Standard），用于规定解决问题的正确方法；规则（Rule），用于规定唯一正确答案并消除任何决策空间。所需的约束程度决定实践者采用哪一类型。

[5] 不要执着于“项目”一词的定义或项目的本质。它只是为达成某个明确结果而组织工作的一种努力。你组织内部对项目的定义及所用标签，很可能与任何其他组织都不一致。我的助理把预订一趟航班也称为一个项目。

[6] 这并不意味着由一个人完成所有工作。开发 EA 是一项团队运动，设有各类专业岗位。沿用这个比喻，团队必须在同一时间打同一场比赛。

[7] 为便于讨论，本文件用“组合”指为共同结果而工作的一组项目。实践者所在组织使用“举措”“组合”“项目群”还是某种组合，取决于该组织的变革方式、其 PMO 的结构以及企业战略的结构。探讨这些术语恰当用法之间的理论差异不在本文件范围内。


### 语义规则与适用边界

- ABB 可以是约束已完整的可复用描述，也可以仅描述组成完整而细节随需求变化。
  - 适用边界：不能要求每个 ABB 都有预定义规格，也不能把 ABB 等同产品。
  - PDF 73：In some cases, it will be a re-usable description of part of the architecture; using it again enables the Practitioner to simply adopt a known successful way to address a problem. In this case, the ABB is complete in all regards, providing a complete description, and constraints that address repeated requirements. In other cases, it will not have the constraints and specifications predefined. In this latter case, the components of the description will be complete, but the detail will vary depending upon the requirements.
- 本书用规格应对需求，用控制应对风险，并把规格区分为原则、模式、标准和规则。
  - 适用边界：该四类型表达不同约束力度；规则消除选择，模式提供可复用方式，不能统一成 MUST。
  - PDF 74：The level of constraint required determines the type used by the Practitioner.

<details><summary>展开英文原文及文本块定位</summary>

**G186:p0073:c001 · PDF 73**

5.5. Where are ABBs?

**G186:p0073:c002 · PDF 73**

The TOGAF concept of the Architecture Building Block (ABB) is the effective Practitioner’s friend. A good ABB facilitates time-to-market and completeness. As with most TOGAF definitions, knowing that an ABB is “a constituent of the architecture model that describes a single aspect of the overall model” does not immediately tell us what they look like in an EA Repository.

**G186:p0073:c003 · PDF 73**

An ABB will look like whatever it must be to describe part of the overall architecture – efforts to carefully define the contents and structure of this concept will flounder on the variability and scope of what can be described within an EA Landscape. A building block is part of a greater whole that accelerates the effective description of the candidate architecture.

**G186:p0073:c004 · PDF 73**

In some cases, it will be a re-usable description of part of the architecture; using it again enables the Practitioner to simply adopt a known successful way to address a problem. In this case, the ABB is complete in all regards, providing a complete description, and constraints that address repeated requirements. In other cases, it will not have the constraints and specifications predefined. In this latter case, the components of the description will be complete, but the detail will vary depending upon the requirements.

**G186:p0074:c000 · PDF 74**

Endnotes

**G186:p0074:c001 · PDF 74**

[1] At several points in this document, and other papers from the same authors, there are very statements about effective architecture practice. These statements are drawn from the experience of the authors and reviewers. Gathering, maintaining, and analyzing pointless information is no different than establishing an EA team for the wrong purpose. Eventually, it will be fatal for the EA team. [2] Earlier this document used the term “end state”. In reality, there is no end state for an Enterprise, unless it is terminating its operations. The Guide also used “future state” to indicate lapse of time to achieve and experience the improvement. From this point onward this document will use “target state” to indicate that it is the foreseeable best case scenario the Enterprise is striving to achieve. Having achieved, the same concepts and approach for trade-off can be applied or fine-tuned to new scenarios. [3] American Productivity and Quality Center; refer to: www.apqc.org. [4] An Architecture Requirements Specification can be delivered through different levels of detail and in multiple ways. For clarity, this document distinguishes use of an architecture specification to address a stakeholder requirement, from a control to address a risk. The semantic distinction is used to assess for value. Typically, stakeholder requirements have an up-side, where risks have a downside. This document typically divides architecture specification into four types: Principle, used to provide guidance on how to think about the decision; Pattern, used to provide a reusable approach to the decision; Standard, used to specify a correct approach to the problem, and Rule, used to specify a correct answer and eliminate any decision. The level of constraint required determines the type used by the Practitioner. [5] Do not fixate on definition of the term “project” or what a project is. It is just an organizing effort for work to achieve an understood outcome. Your organization’s internal definition of a project, and the label used, will be unlikely to align with anyone else’s. My assistant refers to booking a flight as a project. [6] This does not suggest that one person does it all. Developing an EA is a team sport with specialist positions. Following the analogy, the team has to play the same game at the same time. [7] For the purpose of this discussion, this document uses “portfolio” to refer a collection of projects that work to a common outcome. Whether a Practitioner’s organization uses initiative, portfolio, program, or some combination will be determined by the organization’s approach to change, how it has structured its PMO, and how the Enterprise strategy is structured. It is not in the scope of this document to pursue the theoretical distinctions between appropriate use of these terms.

<a id="6"></a>
## 6. ADM 方法

原文：6. Approach to the ADM。PDF 物理页：[77]。状态：`source-compared-revised`。

TOGAF 架构开发方法（ADM）是 TOGAF 标准的核心。这一方法使 TOGAF 框架区别于其他所有 EA 框架，因为它包含了“**如何做**”。

围绕 ADM 各阶段、为不同目的开发架构的路径，既不简单也不线性。每个架构的详细程度与具体程度都不同。例如，要开发支持战略的架构，只需在战略层面上沿阶段 A 到阶段 D 走一遍；并非所有步骤都会执行，但驱动业务架构、应用架构与技术架构的逻辑实体都会被捕获并定义。支持战略的架构提供企业的端到端视图，以及实现目标状态的候选路线图。随后利用《TOGAF® 系列指南：TOGAF® 领导者指南——建立并演进 EA 能力》[G184] 所阐述的治理模型，追溯其余架构及其与目标状态的对齐。


### 语义规则与适用边界

- 不同目的在 ADM 的路径、细节与具体程度不同。
  - 适用边界：本节 A–D 概述与第 7 章多阶段映射合读；不能据此把战略阶段 E/F/G/H 从工作流删掉。
  - PDF 77：The path through and around the ADM phases to develop architectures for different purposes is not simple nor linear. The level of detail and specificity of each architecture is different. For instance, to develop an Architecture to Support Strategy, all that is needed is to follow a path from Phase A through Phase D at the strategic level. Not all the steps are executed, but logical entities that drive Business, Applications, and Technology Architectures are captured and defined. Architecture to Support Strategy provides an end-to-end view of the Enterprise and a candidate roadmap to achieve target state. The governance model, as articulated in the TOGAF® Series Guide: The TOGAF Leader’s Guide to Establishing and Evolving an EA Capability [G184], is leveraged to trace the rest of the architectures and their alignment to target state.

### 原文内容单元

<details><summary>展开英文原文及文本块定位</summary>

**G186:p0077:c000 · PDF 77**

6. Approach to the ADM

**G186:p0077:c001 · PDF 77**

The TOGAF Architecture Development Method (ADM) is the core of the TOGAF Standard. This method sets the TOGAF framework apart from every other EA framework because it contains the “how”.

**G186:p0077:c002 · PDF 77**

The path through and around the ADM phases to develop architectures for different purposes is not simple nor linear. The level of detail and specificity of each architecture is different. For instance, to develop an Architecture to Support Strategy, all that is needed is to follow a path from Phase A through Phase D at the strategic level. Not all the steps are executed, but logical entities that drive Business, Applications, and Technology Architectures are captured and defined. Architecture to Support Strategy provides an end-to-end view of the Enterprise and a candidate roadmap to achieve target state. The governance model, as articulated in the TOGAF® Series Guide: The TOGAF Leader’s Guide to Establishing and Evolving an EA Capability [G184], is leveraged to trace the rest of the architectures and their alignment to target state.

</details>

## 6.1 关键活动

### 语义规则与适用边界

- 不同架构目的保留基本一致的关键活动。
  - 适用边界：活动一致不代表详细程度、产出格式或排程一致。
  - PDF 77：All architecture development has a set of consistent key activity that is essentially unchanged for different purposes.

<a id="6.1.1"></a>
## 6.1.1 利益相关者参与与需求管理

原文：6.1.1. Stakeholder Engagement and Requirements Management。PDF 物理页：[77, 78]。状态：`source-compared-revised`。

TOGAF 框架把需求管理与利益相关者参与置于架构开发的核心。实践者按照本组织利益相关者的偏好与优先级开发 EA。**架构从不向利益相关者“推销”，利益相关者的偏好也从不被操纵。**

利益相关者拥有架构，也拥有架构被期望使之实现的价值偏好与优先级。实践者必须彻底放下自己的偏好、偏见与优先级。实践者必须**为**其利益相关者行事。

这是实践者必须执行的最困难的活动之一。优秀的实践者热忱投入组织的未来，同时也参与定义并实现目标状态。实践者通常扮演多个角色：除开发架构之外，他们还充当主题专家（SME）与利益相关者的代理人——关于角色的讨论见第 15 章。作为 SME，实践者是专家建议的来源；作为代理人，实践者可以代表利益相关者发言。为成功扮演这些角色，优秀的实践者必须清楚自己何时处于不同角色，并相应地调整行为。

有效的需求管理依赖于：从组织的愿景、使命、业务模型与战略，一直到最细粒度的需求陈述，都有清晰的可追溯性。为此，实践者必须仔细区分“直接有效的支撑”与“松散关联”。不能最好地促成完整利益相关者偏好集的东西，都是对主要机会的干扰。

与利益相关者互动时，实践者必须维护**每一位**利益相关者偏好的完整集合，以及这些偏好的含义。成功要求放弃“绝对最优”，进入**满意化**（satisficing）的领域。直白地说：如果存在单一显而易见的最佳答案，组织的利益相关者就不需要架构。

有效的互动基于有效的沟通；有效的沟通基于视图与视角的概念。不同利益相关者对架构有不同关注点。这些关注点必须被有效回应并向利益相关者表达，才能使利益相关者批准目标架构；参见表 3-2。


### 语义规则与适用边界

- 架构师按利益相关者偏好工作，不操纵偏好或推销既定架构。
  - 适用边界：专家建议、代理发言与开发职责须按角色分清；AI 不可把自己的方案偏好伪装成用户批准。
  - PDF 77：The TOGAF framework places requirements management and stakeholder engagement at the center of architecture development. Practitioners develop EA in accordance with the preferences and priorities of their organization’s stakeholders. Architecture is never sold to a stakeholder. Stakeholder preferences are never manipulated.
- 愿景、使命、业务模型、战略到细粒度需求应有清楚有效支撑链。
  - 适用边界：松散主题相似不足以证明需求与战略对齐。
  - PDF 77：Effective requirements management is dependent upon clear traceability from the organization’s vision, mission, business model, and strategies through the most detailed statement of requirement. In order to perform this, the Practitioner must carefully distinguish between direct effective support and loose association. Things that do not best enable the complete set of stakeholder preferences are distractions from the main chance.

### 原文内容单元

<details><summary>展开英文原文及文本块定位</summary>

**G186:p0077:c005 · PDF 77**

6.1.1. Stakeholder Engagement and Requirements Management

**G186:p0077:c006 · PDF 77**

The TOGAF framework places requirements management and stakeholder engagement at the center of architecture development. Practitioners develop EA in accordance with the preferences and priorities of their organization’s stakeholders. Architecture is never sold to a stakeholder. Stakeholder preferences are never manipulated.

**G186:p0077:c007 · PDF 77**

Stakeholders own the architecture and the value preference and priority the architecture is expected to enable. Practitioners must completely submerge their preferences, biases, and priorities. Practitioners must act for their stakeholders.

**G186:p0077:c008 · PDF 77**

This is one of the most difficult activities a Practitioner must perform. Good Practitioners are passionately engaged in the future of their organization, as well as participating in defining and realizing the target state. Practitioners typically perform several roles: they will act as Subject Matter Experts (SMEs) and agents for their stakeholders in addition to developing architecture – see Chapter 15 for a discussion of roles. As an SME, the Practitioner is a source of expert advice. As an agent, the Practitioner may speak on behalf of a stakeholder. In order to be successful when performing these roles, the good Practitioner must understand when they are acting in a different role and behave appropriately.

**G186:p0077:c009 · PDF 77**

Effective requirements management is dependent upon clear traceability from the organization’s vision, mission, business model, and strategies through the most detailed statement of requirement. In order to perform this, the Practitioner must carefully distinguish between direct effective support and loose association. Things that do not best enable the complete set of stakeholder preferences are distractions from the main chance.

**G186:p0078:c000 · PDF 78**

When engaging with stakeholders, Practitioners must maintain the complete set of every stakeholder’s preference, and the implications of those preferences. Success requires abandoning absolute and entering the realm of satisficing. Bluntly, if there is a single obvious best answer, the organization’s stakeholders do not need an architecture.

**G186:p0078:c001 · PDF 78**

Effective engagement is based upon effective communication. Effective communication is based on the concept of view and viewpoint. Different stakeholders have different concerns about the architecture. These concerns must be addressed and represented effectively to the stakeholder to enable the stakeholder to approve the Target Architecture; see Table 3-2.

</details>

<a id="6.1.2"></a>
## 6.1.2 权衡

原文：6.1.2. Trade-Off。PDF 物理页：[78]。状态：`source-compared-revised`。

实践者在架构开发期间能执行的最有价值活动之一，是促成利益相关者的**权衡决策**。促成权衡往往比最终确定一份架构描述更有价值。好的架构处理复杂问题，而复杂问题没有清晰、无歧义的最佳答案，只有合理的折衷。

权衡要求在同一位利益相关者的偏好之间、以及不同利益相关者的偏好之间做有意识的选择。有效的权衡需要理解价值偏好与优先级，以及实现目标所需的变革范围。

通常，当权衡超出利益相关者的控制范围或兴趣范围时，其表现会不佳。特别是当权衡涉及不同利益相关者的偏好时，利益相关者往往表现不佳：他们会过度强调自己所在组织部分的制度性角色与偏好。

实践者最有价值的作用，是**在利益相关者之间、跨组织边界**促成权衡。这种促成使不同利益相关者能够有效衡量他们凭直觉无法理解的偏好、优先级与成本。最佳实践的 EA 会在相互竞争的偏好、优先级与价值之间找到最佳契合。在促成权衡讨论时，要追查所有影响，并想透终局需求；与企业风险管理流程协作，浮现必要的维度；想透所有过渡状态；利用架构工具处理 EA 全景的复杂性并加速过程。

实践者不应低估组织从“跨组织边界促成权衡”中获得的价值。


### 语义规则与适用边界

- 实践者跨利益相关者和组织边界促进权衡，并追查风险和所有过渡状态影响。
  - 适用边界：实践者促进知情决策，而不是取得利益相关者的最终决定权。
  - PDF 78：Practitioners are most valuable facilitating trade-off between stakeholders and across organizational boundaries. This facilitation allows different stakeholders to effectively measure preferences, priorities, and costs that they do not intuitively understand. Best practice EA finds the best fit across competing preference, priority, and value. In facilitating the trade-off discussion, chase down all impacts and think through the end game needs. Work with the Enterprise risk management process to surface requisite dimensions. Think through all transition states. Leverage the architecture tool to handle the complexities of the EA Landscape and to accelerate the process.

### 原文内容单元

<details><summary>展开英文原文及文本块定位</summary>

**G186:p0078:c002 · PDF 78**

6.1.2. Trade-Off

**G186:p0078:c003 · PDF 78**

One of the most valuable activities a Practitioner will perform during architecture development is facilitating the stakeholders’ trade-off decision. Facilitating trade-off is often more valuable than finalizing an architecture description. Good architecture addresses complex problems. Complex problems[1] do not have clear, unambiguous best answers. Instead, they have reasonable compromises.

**G186:p0078:c004 · PDF 78**

Trade-off requires deliberate selection between one stakeholder’s preferences as well as between different stakeholders’ preferences. Effective trade-off requires understanding value preference and priority as well as the scope of change necessary to realize the target.

**G186:p0078:c005 · PDF 78**

As a rule, stakeholders underperform when that trade-off stands beyond their span of control or span of interest. In particular, stakeholders underperform when the trade-off involves the preferences of different stakeholders. Stakeholders typically overemphasize the institutional role and preferences of their portion of the organization.

**G186:p0078:c006 · PDF 78**

Practitioners are most valuable facilitating trade-off between stakeholders and across organizational boundaries. This facilitation allows different stakeholders to effectively measure preferences, priorities, and costs that they do not intuitively understand. Best practice EA finds the best fit across competing preference, priority, and value. In facilitating the trade-off discussion, chase down all impacts and think through the end game needs. Work with the Enterprise risk management process to surface requisite dimensions. Think through all transition states. Leverage the architecture tool to handle the complexities of the EA Landscape and to accelerate the process.

**G186:p0078:c007 · PDF 78**

Practitioners should not underestimate the value their organization receives from facilitation of trade- off across organizational boundaries.

</details>

<a id="6.2"></a>
## 6.2 权衡决策

原文：6.2. Trade-Off Decisions。PDF 物理页：[78, 79]。状态：`source-compared-revised`。

对“权衡”（trade-off）最常见的两种理解是：“在两个合意但互不兼容的特性之间取得的平衡；一种折衷”，以及“牺牲某物的某一质量、方面或数量，以换取另一质量、方面或数量”。在开发企业架构时，权衡从来不是寻求中间地带的折衷。权衡要求理解全体利益相关者偏好的完整集合及其相互作用。权衡评估一组复杂的收益与成本，以找到最优价值点：在该点上，所有最低收益都得到满足，同时在可接受的成本下达到最高的总价值。利益相关者关注点在评估潜在收益与成本时至关重要。当企业所处的语境或目标分析不充分时，某些选择会显得显而易见或低成本；草率地把这些选择当作可行候选，将导致目标达成次优，或使举措彻底失败。

例如，当实践者探索候选目标架构时，若发现某个看似显而易见的改进却**没有拥护者**，那很可能是在跳向一个基于糟糕分析的决策。面对这种情况，实践者应寻找**隐藏价值**。隐藏价值永远不会以显而易见的成本节约形式被描述出来。


### 语义规则与适用边界

- 权衡寻求包含所有最低收益、成本可接受的最高总体价值。
  - 适用边界：不是机械平均、折半或只按显性成本节约选方案。
  - PDF 78：The most common interpretations of trade-off are “a balance achieved between two desirable but incompatible features; a compromise” and “losing one quality, aspect, or amount of something in return for gaining another quality, aspect, or amount”. In developing an Enterprise Architecture, trade- offs are never about compromises where a middle ground is sought. Trade-off requires understanding the complete set of all stakeholder preferences and their interaction. Trade-off assesses a complex set of benefits and costs to find the optimal value point where all minimum benefits are included, and the highest total value is reached at an acceptable cost. Stakeholder concerns are critical in assessing

### 原文内容单元

<details><summary>展开英文原文及文本块定位</summary>

**G186:p0078:c008 · PDF 78**

6.2. Trade-Off Decisions

**G186:p0078:c009 · PDF 78**

The most common interpretations of trade-off are “a balance achieved between two desirable but incompatible features; a compromise” and “losing one quality, aspect, or amount of something in return for gaining another quality, aspect, or amount”. In developing an Enterprise Architecture, trade- offs are never about compromises where a middle ground is sought. Trade-off requires understanding the complete set of all stakeholder preferences and their interaction. Trade-off assesses a complex set of benefits and costs to find the optimal value point where all minimum benefits are included, and the highest total value is reached at an acceptable cost. Stakeholder concerns are critical in assessing

**G186:p0079:c000 · PDF 79**

potential benefits and cost. When the context or the objective of the Enterprise is poorly analyzed, some choices will appear obvious or low-cost. Jumping to employ those choices as a viable candidate will result in sub-optimal achievement of the target or total failure of the initiative.

**G186:p0079:c001 · PDF 79**

For example, when a Practitioner is exploring a candidate target architecture and discovers what appears to be an obvious improvement without a champion, they are likely to be jumping to a decision that is based on poor analysis. When faced with such circumstances, the Practitioner should look for the hidden value. Hidden value will never be described in terms of the obvious cost savings.

</details>

<a id="6.3"></a>
## 6.3 阶段 B、C、D——开发架构

原文：6.3. Phases B, C, and D – Developing the Architecture。PDF 物理页：[79]。状态：`source-compared-revised`。

实践者常常感到惊讶：TOGAF 标准中阶段 B、C、D 用于开发架构的步骤竟然完全相同。步骤相同，是因为“开发架构、确认所开发的工作产品适配、确认批准”这三者所采用的方法相同。这些步骤也是**强制性的**。步骤可以跳过，但最终结果可能因此面临风险。

从一种目的到另一种目的、从一个领域到另一个领域、从一个项目到另一个项目、从一个 EA 团队到另一个 EA 团队，变化的只是详细程度、精确程度与形式化程度。所有实践者都应把这些步骤当作**检查清单**使用。


### 语义规则与适用边界

- B/C/D 开发、验证适配和批准的步骤一致并被称为强制；跳过意味着成果风险。
  - 适用边界：与第 5 章复用已有输出区分：不重复做已有分析不等于未满足必要结果。
  - PDF 79：Practitioners often find it surprising that the steps outlined in the TOGAF Standard to develop architecture in Phases B, C, and D are identical. The steps are identical because the approach to developing an architecture, confirming the work product developed fits, and confirming approval are identical. These steps are also mandatory. Steps can be skipped, but the final outcome could be at risk.

6.3. Phases B, C, and D – Developing the Architecture

**G186:p0079:c003 · PDF 79**

Practitioners often find it surprising that the steps outlined in the TOGAF Standard to develop architecture in Phases B, C, and D are identical. The steps are identical because the approach to developing an architecture, confirming the work product developed fits, and confirming approval are identical. These steps are also mandatory. Steps can be skipped, but the final outcome could be at risk.

What changes from purpose to purpose, domain to domain, project to project, and EA team to EA team is the level of detail, precision, and formality. All Practitioners should use the steps as a checklist.

## 6.3.1 选择参考模型、视角与工具

原文：6.3.1. Select Reference Models, Viewpoints, and Tools。PDF 物理页：[79]。状态：`source-compared-revised`。

- 是否存在可让你直接进入收集与分析、而不必从头发明的参考模型？
- 当前 EA 全景中缺少什么信息？

- 模型与工具选择从利益相关者关注点所需信息出发。

<a id="6.3.2"></a>
## 6.3.2 开发目标、基线与差距

原文：6.3.2. Develop Target, Baseline, and Gap。PDF 物理页：[79]。状态：`source-compared-revised`。

**刚好足够满足目的。** 如果现状已被接受，描述基线的唯一理由就是推导差距。若利益相关者或 SME 对现状有争议——尤其是对“现状是否适合目标”有争议——那么描述现状以取得共识是有用的；否则再强调一次：只在确定差距所必需的程度上描述。

请考虑“把描述限制在有差距之处”这一做法的局限。如果 EA 全景的某部分不会发生变化，也不用于可追溯性，实践者花时间去描述它有什么有用的理由？

一个反复出现的问题是：如何描述现状。坦率地说，使用与候选架构**完全相同**的技术。用同样的技术、同样的详细程度来描述，才能识别差距。**差距就是一切发生变化的东西。**


### 语义规则与适用边界

- 已认可现状只描述到辨识差距所需；现状受质疑时先取得共识。
  - 适用边界：若需追溯则保留未变化部分，不得以“无差距”为由删除全部基线证据。
  - PDF 79：Just enough for the purpose. If the current state is accepted, the only reason to describe the baseline is to develop gaps. If stakeholders, or SMEs, dispute the current state, especially its fitness to objective, then describing current state to get an alignment is useful. Otherwise, let us re-iterate: only to the extent necessary to determine gaps.
- 当前与候选采用同样技术和细节层级，以便识别变化。
  - 适用边界：差距是一切变化，不只是短缺功能。
  - PDF 79：A recurrent question is how to describe the current state. Frankly, use the exact same techniques as the candidate. Description using the same technique at the same level of detail enables identification of gaps. A gap is simply everything that changes.

6.3.2. Develop Target, Baseline, and Gap

**G186:p0079:c012 · PDF 79**

If stakeholders, or SMEs, dispute the current state, especially its fitness to objective, then describing current state to get an alignment is useful. Otherwise, let us re-iterate: only to the extent necessary to determine gaps.

<a id="6.3.3"></a>
## 6.3.3 考虑成本与价值，识别达成目标的工作

原文：6.3.3. Identify the Work to Reach the Target Considering Cost and Value。PDF 物理页：[80]。状态：`source-compared-revised`。

如果不理解达成目标所需的工作，利益相关者就会批准不可能的事。如果读心头盔和自制造产品既免费又容易，他们凭什么不想要？

实践者对**守护价值**负有责任。目标带来价值的提升，代价是变革成本。如果你不了解达成目标所需的工作，实践者又如何能向利益相关者说明“任何目标都是好主意、且回应了组织的偏好”？


### 语义规则与适用边界

- 价值判断必须同时理解目标带来的增益与达成它所需的工作成本。
  - 适用边界：未估算工作就请求批准会使不可能目标被接受。
  - PDF 80：The Practitioner is accountable for guarding value. A target provides an increase in value, at a cost of change. If you do not have an understanding of the work to reach the target, how can a Practitioner represent to a stakeholder that any target is a good idea and addresses the organization’s preferences?

<a id="6.3.4"></a>
## 6.3.4 解决影响

原文：6.3.4. Resolving Impacts。PDF 物理页：[80]。状态：`source-compared-revised`。

在整个 EA 全景中解决影响，是管理 EA 全景最重要的步骤之一。实践者要探索其候选架构对以下各方面的影响：其他候选架构、过渡状态、目标状态，以及正在进行的实施项目。实践者还要与企业风险管理流程协作，评估对企业风险的影响。总的来说，这是投入程度高、运转良好的 EA 团队最复杂的活动之一。它需要可用的 EA 存储库，以及扎实的分析与报告软件。每个组织都是一组持续变化、相互连接的部分；所有架构描述都是近似。

务实地说，EA 全景越复杂，解决影响就越困难，也越必要。试图在没有有效建模与分析工具的情况下管理 EA 全景的实践者，将难以解决影响。所有影响都必须以**价值期望**来衡量并解决，而价值期望基于一条清晰的可追溯链：从实现目标架构所需的工作，经差距，到预期价值。

若不小心谨慎地处理架构全景在所有状态下的影响，实践者就无法确信其候选架构最服务于企业。

要把信息量压到最低，并持续追踪那组能在关键利益相关者眼中明显支撑价值的最小关注点集。


### 语义规则与适用边界

- 候选方案须评估其他候选、过渡、目标及在建项目影响，并接入企业风险过程。
  - 适用边界：评估不限于当前模型内部，所有架构描述都是近似。
  - PDF 80：Resolving impacts across the EA Landscape is one of the most important steps in managing the EA Landscape. The Practitioner explores the impact of their candidate architecture against other candidate architectures, transition states, the target state, and in-flight Implementation Projects. The Practitioner also works with the Enterprise risk management process to assess impact to the Enterprise’s risk. Altogether, this is one of the most complex activities for an engaged high-functioning EA team. It requires a functioning EA Repository and solid analytic and reporting software. Every organization is a set of constantly changing interconnected parts. All architecture descriptions are approximations.
- 跨全景影响要沿工作→差距→预期价值的链路解决。
  - 适用边界：没有追溯的影响分数不足以说明企业总体偏好得到满足。
  - PDF 80：In practical terms, the more complex the EA Landscape is, the more difficult, and the more necessary, resolving impacts is. Practitioners attempting to manage an EA Landscape without an effective model and analytic tooling will struggle to resolve impacts. All impacts need to be resolved in terms of value expectation which is based upon clear traceability from the work required to realize the Target Architecture through the gap to the expected value.

### 原文内容单元

<details><summary>展开英文原文及文本块定位</summary>

**G186:p0080:c003 · PDF 80**

6.3.4. Resolving Impacts

**G186:p0080:c004 · PDF 80**

Resolving impacts across the EA Landscape is one of the most important steps in managing the EA Landscape. The Practitioner explores the impact of their candidate architecture against other candidate architectures, transition states, the target state, and in-flight Implementation Projects. The Practitioner also works with the Enterprise risk management process to assess impact to the Enterprise’s risk. Altogether, this is one of the most complex activities for an engaged high-functioning EA team. It requires a functioning EA Repository and solid analytic and reporting software. Every organization is a set of constantly changing interconnected parts. All architecture descriptions are approximations.

**G186:p0080:c005 · PDF 80**

In practical terms, the more complex the EA Landscape is, the more difficult, and the more necessary, resolving impacts is. Practitioners attempting to manage an EA Landscape without an effective model and analytic tooling will struggle to resolve impacts. All impacts need to be resolved in terms of value expectation which is based upon clear traceability from the work required to realize the Target Architecture through the gap to the expected value.

**G186:p0080:c006 · PDF 80**

Without care and attention to addressing the impacts across the architecture landscape in all of its states, the Practitioner cannot have confidence that their candidate architecture best serves the Enterprise.

**G186:p0080:c007 · PDF 80**

Manage the information volume down to the minimum and constantly chase the minimum set of concerns that visibly support value in the eyes of key stakeholders.

</details>

<a id="6.3.5"></a>
## 6.3.5 批准

原文：6.3.5. Approval。PDF 物理页：[80]。状态：`source-compared-revised`。

没有利益相关者的批准，就不可能有实施治理，也不可能有对更详细架构的治理。没有批准，实践者手里只是一份有记录的**意见**；而利益相关者、SME、实施者与决策者也都有意见。

真正的批准是复杂的；真正的批准**应当**复杂。实践者是在协助组织，在一组随时间相互竞争的偏好中选出最佳可行路径。他们花时间探索了选项与影响。

有了已批准的目标架构，未来就被定义，到目标的可追溯性就可用，权衡也已完成。好的架构权衡会探索选项、成本与收益，以达成对组织而言的最优答案——通常也是相互竞争利益之间的折衷。


### 语义规则与适用边界

- 未经利益相关者批准的架构只是一份已记录意见，不能作为实施或下层架构治理依据。
  - 适用边界：文档生成、专家赞同与已批准有严格区别。
  - PDF 80：Without approval by the stakeholders, no implementation governance is possible, and no governance of more detailed architecture is possible. Without approval, the Practitioner has a documented opinion. Stakeholders, SMEs, implementers, and decision-makers also have opinions.

<a id="6.3.6"></a>
## 6.3.6 最小必要与查阅 EA 存储库

原文：6.3.6. Minimum Needed and Look in the EA Repository。PDF 物理页：[81]。状态：`source-compared-revised`。

实践者从 EA 存储库的内容开始，也以其内容结束。

无论何时需要分析或报告，第一站都是 EA 存储库。实践者应运用以下检验：

- 回应当前问题所需的信息是否已经存在？
- 是否存在指导并约束当前任务的上层架构？
- 为弥补 EA 存储库的缺口，最少需要哪些信息？

无论 EA 存储库是一个结构良好的建模分析工具，还是一堆演示文稿，都无所谓——**从 EA 存储库开始**。收集并分析回应当前问题所需的最低限度信息。那些与“理解系统以回应利益相关者关注点”没有清晰视线的问题，都是离题的。优秀的实践者不会因为“还可以做很多分析”而瘫痪；他们只做必须做的分析。


### 语义规则与适用边界

- 首先复用存储库，补足当前关注点需要的最少信息与分析。
  - 适用边界：即使只是演示稿集合仍应先检查；本节不证明其等同高效长期模型仓库。
  - PDF 81：It does not matter whether the EA Repository is a well-structured modeling and analysis tool or a collection of presentations, start with the EA Repository. Gather and analyze the minimum to address the question at hand. Questions that do not have a clear line of site to understanding the system to address a stakeholder concern are beside the point. Good Practitioners are not paralyzed by the potential analysis that could be done; they perform the analysis that must be done.

### 原文内容单元

<details><summary>展开英文原文及文本块定位</summary>

**G186:p0081:c000 · PDF 81**

6.3.6. Minimum Needed and Look in the EA Repository

**G186:p0081:c001 · PDF 81**

Practitioners start and finish with the contents of the EA Repository.

**G186:p0081:c002 · PDF 81**

Whenever analysis, or reporting, is needed, the first stop is the EA Repository. Practitioners should apply the following tests:

**G186:p0081:c003 · PDF 81**

• Is the information that will address the question at hand already available?

**G186:p0081:c004 · PDF 81**

• Is there a superior architecture that guides and constrains the task at hand?

**G186:p0081:c005 · PDF 81**

• What is the minimum information needed to cover shortfalls in the EA Repository?

**G186:p0081:c006 · PDF 81**

It does not matter whether the EA Repository is a well-structured modeling and analysis tool or a collection of presentations, start with the EA Repository. Gather and analyze the minimum to address the question at hand. Questions that do not have a clear line of site to understanding the system to address a stakeholder concern are beside the point. Good Practitioners are not paralyzed by the potential analysis that could be done; they perform the analysis that must be done.

</details>

<a id="6.4"></a>
## 6.4 ADM 结论

原文：6.4. ADM Conclusion。PDF 物理页：[81]。状态：`source-compared-revised`。

TOGAF ADM 使 TOGAF 框架区别于其他所有 EA 框架，因为它包含了如何开发并使用有效 EA。围绕 ADM 各阶段、为不同目的开发架构的路径，既不简单也不线性；但它充满了**强制性的任务**。再次强调：跳过任务就要承担风险。



尾注

[1] 参见 Jeff Conklin 的《Wicked Problems & Social Complexity》中关于对话映射（Dialog Mapping）的内容（见 Conklin，2005）。


### 语义规则与适用边界

- ADM 为不同目的提供非线性路径，但跳过必要任务仍有风险。
  - 适用边界：不把裁剪当成忽略任务成果或利益相关者批准。
  - PDF 81：The TOGAF ADM sets the TOGAF framework apart from every other EA frameworks because it contains how to develop and use effective EA. It is not a simple nor a linear path around the ADM phases to develop the architectures for different purposes. It is, however, filled with tasks that are mandatory. Again, to skip tasks undertakes risks.

<a id="7.1"></a>
## 7.1 引言

原文：7.1. Introduction。PDF 物理页：[82, 83, 84]。状态：`source-compared-revised`。

该架构的目标是定义端到端的目标架构，以及在规划期（通常为 3 至 10 年）约束下实现它的路线图。该架构将驱动多项有针对性的变革举措的产生，界定治理的边界条件，并确定价值报告的验收标准。开发该架构的活动至少要迭代 ADM 一次（企业层面），并为 EA 能力再迭代一次。

多数情况下，EA 举措是在业务周期中途触发的，而且很可能由一家已运营多年的企业发起。开始架构工作的一个合理起点，是理解开展 EA 工作的理由。表 7-1 汇总了各 ADM 阶段如何执行、达到什么结果；本章其余部分将详细讨论表中的内容。

表 7-1：汇总表——ADM 阶段与支持战略的架构

| 主题 | 对应的 TOGAF ADM 阶段 |
|---|---|
| 理解语境 | 部分战略层阶段 H。<br>企业语境：审查现有路线图；理解/推断差距；（背景）架构工作请求。<br>部分战略层阶段 A。<br>企业语境：目标、目的、举措、竞争与战术分析；重申规划期；运营模型；现有治理与风险管理模型；利益相关者与关注点识别。<br>EA 能力特有的语境：EA 能力模型；定制的 EA 过程模型；内容模型与（行业）参考架构；覆盖 EA 全景的方法。 |
| 开展评估与分析 | 部分战略层阶段 B、C、D。<br>企业语境：评估流程、业务术语、信息系统（应用、数据、技术等）与能力的当前及目标运营水平；评估企业与扩展语境的当前及目标水平（针对本企业）；识别候选 ABB（可选）；记录并定义当前与目标之间的鸿沟。<br>部分能力层阶段 B、C、D。<br>企业语境：评估当前及目标运营挑战、与伙伴和供应商的互动；组织结构与利益相关者矩阵；重申企业的价值主张。<br>EA 能力特有的语境：修订 EA 内容模型；修订 EA 存储库。<br>部分战略层阶段 A。<br>企业语境：识别并分析差距；识别视角。<br>部分战略层阶段 G。<br>企业语境：合规审查；完整性与置信度评估。 |
| 确定达成目标状态的方法 | 部分战略层阶段 B、C、D。<br>企业语境：为每个差距定义规格与工作包；识别需要改进的能力；创建候选组织模型；创建候选运营模型；填充需求管理（或 EA 存储库）。<br>部分战略层阶段 A。<br>企业语境：开发架构愿景；开发架构定义；重申愿景、定义、工作包、运营模型与组织模型的相关性。<br>EA 能力特有的语境：修订 EA 治理模型；修订 EA 参与模型。<br>部分战略层阶段 E。<br>企业语境：评估差异化流程的影响；识别缩小差距的选项。<br>部分战略层阶段 G。<br>企业语境：风险与合规审查；最终确定利益相关者矩阵。 |
| 最终确定架构愿景/目标状态 | 部分战略层阶段 F。<br>企业语境：完成路线图；定义治理模型；完成架构定义与规格。 |

相关结构化资料：[source-tables](../tables/G186.json)。本节表格全文已对照；独立结构化表保留单元格与位置。

### 语义规则与适用边界

- 战略架构定义受规划期约束的端到端目标、路线图、治理边界和价值报告准则。
  - 适用边界：本指南要求企业层面与 EA 能力层面各至少一次迭代；不是只画战略愿景。
  - PDF 82：The objective of this architecture is to define an end-to-end Target Architecture and a roadmap to achieve it constrained by the planning horizon (normally three to ten years). This architecture will drive creation of several targeted change initiatives, define the boundary conditions for governance, and acceptance criteria for value reporting. Activities to develop this architecture will iterate the ADM at least once at the Enterprise level and once for the EA Capability.
- 表 7-1 按企业及 EA 能力语境区分活动，候选 ABB 识别为可选。

**G186:p0082:c002 · PDF 82**

7.1. Introduction

**G186:p0082:c003 · PDF 82**

The objective of this architecture is to define an end-to-end Target Architecture and a roadmap to achieve it constrained by the planning horizon (normally three to ten years). This architecture will drive creation of several targeted change initiatives, define the boundary conditions for governance, and acceptance criteria for value reporting. Activities to develop this architecture will iterate the ADM at least once at the Enterprise level and once for the EA Capability.

**G186:p0082:c004 · PDF 82**

On most occasions, EA initiatives are triggered in the middle of a business cycle. It is most likely performed by an Enterprise that has been operating for many years. A logical point to start the architecture work is to understand the rationale for EA work. Table 7-1 summarizes how the ADM phases are executed and to what outcome. The content of the table is discussed in detail in the rest of this chapter.

**G186:p0082:c005 · PDF 82**

<a id="7.2"></a>
## 7.2 理解语境

原文：7.2. Understanding Context。PDF 物理页：[85, 86]。状态：`source-compared-revised`。

企业一直在用隐式的路线图与方向来执行当年的举措，其中多数意在处理某个差距。很可能是进展或影响方面的关注触发了架构工作的需要。把这些关注与举措记录为架构工作请求的草稿——这些关注很可能至今仍然成立。

在着手支持战略的架构时，实现架构愿景阶段的目标可以说最为重要：它既关系到后续 ADM 阶段能否正确铺开，也为后续架构奠定成功基础。开发战略架构的一个隐含约束是规划期的长度；目标架构应当与企业展望未来、竞争与投资实力等能力相称。另一个方面是现有的治理与风险管理模型，它可能没有被明确界定或陈述；而明确它，是让工作起步的最快路径。如果 EA 能力尚未记录该模型，就花时间把它做出来。

战略架构的范围通常广度大、深度浅、时间跨度长。为界定基线与目标架构工作在范围内外的内容，必须定义：

- 架构全景的广度、深度与时间跨度
- 各架构域要覆盖的详细程度
- 架构的分区特征
- 已知约束
- 可利用的架构资产，例如业界可得的框架、系统模型等

始终把握什么为企业创造价值——也就是让架构与眼前的问题匹配。范围应把架构限定在达成目标正好所需的部分，不多做。

这一步的一项关键交付物是创建**利益相关者地图**，它应清楚说明利益相关者的关注点、需求与视角，以及他们的分类与参与程度。理解利益相关者还能带来另一类输入：文化因素，它有助于 EA 团队理解如何呈现和沟通所提议的架构。

这一步对战略架构非常重要，因为清楚理解利益相关者的需求、利益与愿景，将决定赞助人如何理解战略架构，并引导 EA 团队据此行动。

从战略角度看，重要的是追问：业务语境是否与使命一致？能力是否匹配项目范围？我们是否背着上一个项目的包袱，或公司其他部门的包袱，而它们并不在架构的边界之内？了解工作的语境，有助于微调战略架构的愿景。

最后，验证 EA 能力所指定的、用于分析流程、与利益相关者互动以及交付架构的模型，是否相关且最新。


### 语义规则与适用边界

- 战略目标必须符合企业展望未来、竞争和投资的能力，并了解既有治理与风险模型。
  - 适用边界：没有显式治理模型时需先记录，不能凭默认模板声称已获组织授权。
  - PDF 85：When approaching Architecture for Strategy, achieving the goals of the Architecture Vision phase is arguably the most important step for achieving a proper rollout of the next phases of the ADM as well as setting the stage for success for subsequent architectures. An implicit constraint to developing the strategic architecture is the duration of planning horizon. The Target Architecture should be commensurate with the ability of the Enterprise to look into the future, competition, investment strengths, etc. Another aspect is the existing models for governance and risk management. It may not be defined or stated explicitly. It is the fastest path to getting the efforts off the ground. If the EA Capability has not documented the model, spend the time to get it done.
- 利益相关者地图要记录关注点、需求、视角、分类和参与程度。
  - 适用边界：文化影响沟通方式，但不能改变已确认的决策权。
  - PDF 85：A key deliverable to this step is the creation of a Stakeholder Map which should clearly state the stakeholder concerns, requirements, and viewpoints as well as their classification and level of involvement. Other inputs from gaining an understanding of stakeholders are cultural factors, which can help the EA team understand how to present and communicate the proposed architecture.

### 原文内容单元

<details><summary>展开英文原文及文本块定位</summary>

**G186:p0085:c000 · PDF 85**

7.2. Understanding Context

**G186:p0085:c001 · PDF 85**

Implicit roadmaps and direction have been used to execute the current year’s initiatives. Most of them are meant to address a gap. Most likely the progress or the impact concerns triggered the need for architecture work. Document such concerns and initiatives as the draft Request for Architecture Work. Those concerns are probably valid even now.

**G186:p0085:c002 · PDF 85**

When approaching Architecture for Strategy, achieving the goals of the Architecture Vision phase is arguably the most important step for achieving a proper rollout of the next phases of the ADM as well as setting the stage for success for subsequent architectures. An implicit constraint to developing the strategic architecture is the duration of planning horizon. The Target Architecture should be commensurate with the ability of the Enterprise to look into the future, competition, investment strengths, etc. Another aspect is the existing models for governance and risk management. It may not be defined or stated explicitly. It is the fastest path to getting the efforts off the ground. If the EA Capability has not documented the model, spend the time to get it done.

**G186:p0085:c003 · PDF 85**

The scope of a strategy architecture usually involves a wide breadth, a shallower depth, and a long timeframe. In order to define what is inside and outside the scope of the baseline and Target Architecture efforts, the following must be defined:

**G186:p0085:c004 · PDF 85**

• The breadth, depth, and timeframe of the architecture landscape

**G186:p0085:c005 · PDF 85**

• The level of detail to be covered in each of the architecture domains

**G186:p0085:c006 · PDF 85**

• The partitioning characteristics of the architecture

**G186:p0085:c007 · PDF 85**

• The known constraints

**G186:p0085:c008 · PDF 85**

• The architectural assets to be leveraged, such as assets available elsewhere in the industry like frameworks, system models, etc.

**G186:p0085:c009 · PDF 85**

As always, stay on top of what creates value for the Enterprise – meaning match the architecture to the problem at hand. The scope will limit the architecture to exactly what is needed to achieve the goals and no more.

**G186:p0085:c010 · PDF 85**

A key deliverable to this step is the creation of a Stakeholder Map which should clearly state the stakeholder concerns, requirements, and viewpoints as well as their classification and level of involvement. Other inputs from gaining an understanding of stakeholders are cultural factors, which can help the EA team understand how to present and communicate the proposed architecture.

**G186:p0085:c011 · PDF 85**

This step is very important to strategy architecture since having a clear understanding of stakeholder needs, interests, visions, etc. will dictate how strategy architecture is understood by its sponsors and guide the EA team to act accordingly.

**G186:p0085:c012 · PDF 85**

From a strategy perspective, it is important to ask whether the context of a business aligns with the mission. Do the capabilities match to the project scope? Are we carrying baggage from a previous project or from a different part of the company that is outside the confines of the architecture? Knowing the context of the work can help fine-tune the vision of the strategy architecture.

**G186:p0086:c000 · PDF 86**

Finally, validate that the models specified by the EA Capability to analyze processes, engage with stakeholders, and deliver the architecture are relevant and current.

</details>

<a id="7.3"></a>
## 7.3 评估企业

原文：7.3. Assess the Enterprise。PDF 物理页：[86, 87]。状态：`source-compared-revised`。

这是交付支持战略的架构所需工作的核心。跨企业的广度，尽可能清晰地识别、定义并表述运营状态。该分析覆盖能力、业务流程、信息系统、技术、业务术语、安全、服务提供商、客户满意度等。对每一项，收集使企业能够实现其大部分或全部目标所需的期望运营状态。

完成评估可能需要使用战略地图或五力分析等技术。这类练习的结果将改变战略陈述与目标。当初步分析未能给出利益相关者所期望的增长放大效果时，就用这些技术引导利益相关者探索参与市场的新方式。所交付的架构是在驱动变革，但分析只是识别应该引入何种正确变革的路径；开发架构时产生的部分或全部工作产品，可能不会进入架构存储库，也不会成为交付物。

评估应围绕利益相关者的关键关注点开展。如果企业追求敏捷性，就评估当前与期望的敏捷水平；如果追求运营稳定性，就评估当前与期望的稳定性；如果需要能轻松更换供应商，就评估这一点。完全可以声明，一项或多项能力、信息系统或流程在期望状态中并不需要；同样，也可以把某项能力或服务从“与竞争对手的差异化”调整为“与竞争对手相当”。这些都是企业计划采取方向的间接陈述。要验证价值主张、目标与期望状态的评估值彼此一致。

企业追求什么，已在语境与架构工作请求中定义。利益相关者可能会提出新的关注点要求评估。评估之后，细化并最终确定架构工作请求。记住，目标是采集刚好足够识别差距的数据：每个流程、应用、服务或能力的结果相对于关注点的度量，足以完成评估；追问应用由谁开发、数据中心部署的是哪个版本，属于噪音，应当避免。

现状与期望状态之间的鸿沟，就是企业为实现目标必须跨越的鸿沟。这条鸿沟必须得到所有利益相关者的承认与认可。

为了沟通评估了哪些关注点、覆盖了哪些能力、流程与信息系统等，要识别适当的视角。验证开展评估的团队遵循了已记录的 EA 流程，并咨询了必要且相关的主题专家与利益相关者。

为了向利益相关者证明分析的完整性以及由此形成的目标状态与路线图的可靠性，要有详细的人员咨询记录。可采用访谈、问卷、检查等标准技术收集现状与目标状态信息；对每种技术，都有经过充分研究的关于应咨询多少利益相关者与主题专家的指标。评估的完整性与置信度，是这一架构的**阿喀琉斯之踵**。


### 语义规则与适用边界

- 评估围绕关键关注点比较当前与期望水平，允许取消能力或从差异化退回同等水平。
  - 适用边界：期望值在确认/批准前不是已批准目标；必须和价值主张一致。
  - PDF 86：The assessment should be performed to address key concerns of the stakeholders. If the Enterprise is chasing agility, assess for current and desired[1] agility levels. If it is after operational stability, assess current and desired. If the need is the ability to replace suppliers with ease, assess it. It is perfectly acceptable to state that one or more capabilities or information systems or processes are not needed in the desired state. Likewise, it is acceptable to move a capability or service from being a differentiator from competition to “on par” with competition. These are indirect statements of direction the Enterprise is planning to take. Validate that the value proposition, objectives, and the assessment values for the desired state are consistent.
- 评估完整性和置信度需要被咨询人员轨迹及适当采集方法。
  - 适用边界：本指南未提供样本量数值，不得虚构统一访谈人数门槛。
  - PDF 86：In order to provide confidence to the stakeholders of the completeness of analysis and resultant development of the target state and roadmap, have a detailed trail of the personnel consulted. Employ any of the standard techniques like interviews, surveys, inspections to gather the current and target state information. For each of these techniques, there are well researched metrics for the number of

**G186:p0086:c001 · PDF 86**

7.3. Assess the Enterprise

**G186:p0086:c002 · PDF 86**

This is the core of the effort required to deliver Architecture to Support Strategy. Working across the breadth of the Enterprise, identify, define, and articulate as clearly as possible the operational state. This analysis covers capabilities, business processes, information systems, technology, business terms, security, service providers, customer satisfaction, etc. For each of these, gather the desired operational state that would enable the Enterprise to achieve most or all of its objectives.

**G186:p0086:c003 · PDF 86**

Completing the assessment may require use of techniques like Strategy Map or Five Forces. The outcome from such exercise will change the strategy statements and objectives. When the initial analysis does not provide the growth amplification expectations of the stakeholder, employ these techniques to guide the stakeholder to explore new ways to play in the market. The architecture being delivered is driving a change, but the analysis is just a path to identify a right change to introduce. Some or all work products created while developing the architecture may not go into the Architecture Repository or become a deliverable.

**G186:p0086:c004 · PDF 86**

The assessment should be performed to address key concerns of the stakeholders. If the Enterprise is chasing agility, assess for current and desired[1] agility levels. If it is after operational stability, assess current and desired. If the need is the ability to replace suppliers with ease, assess it. It is perfectly acceptable to state that one or more capabilities or information systems or processes are not needed in the desired state. Likewise, it is acceptable to move a capability or service from being a differentiator from competition to “on par” with competition. These are indirect statements of direction the Enterprise is planning to take. Validate that the value proposition, objectives, and the assessment values for the desired state are consistent.

**G186:p0086:c005 · PDF 86**

What the Enterprise is after is defined in the context and Request for Architecture Work. It is likely that stakeholders may state new concerns to be assessed. Refine and finalize the Request for Architecture Work after assessments. Remember that the goal is to capture just enough data to identify the gaps. How the outcome of each process, application, service, or capability measures against the concern is sufficient to complete the assessment. Going after who made the application or what version is deployed in the data center are noise and should be avoided.

**G186:p0086:c006 · PDF 86**

The chasm between current state and desired state is the chasm the Enterprise has to cross to achieve its objectives. The chasm has to be acknowledged and agreed upon by all stakeholders.

**G186:p0086:c007 · PDF 86**

In order to communicate what concerns were assessed across what capabilities, processes, information systems, etc., identify appropriate viewpoints. Validate that the team performing the assessment followed the documented EA processes and consulted requisite and relevant SMEs and stakeholders.

**G186:p0086:c008 · PDF 86**

In order to provide confidence to the stakeholders of the completeness of analysis and resultant development of the target state and roadmap, have a detailed trail of the personnel consulted. Employ any of the standard techniques like interviews, surveys, inspections to gather the current and target state information. For each of these techniques, there are well researched metrics for the number of

**G186:p0087:c000 · PDF 87**

stakeholders and SMEs to be consulted. Completeness and confidence in the assessment is the Achilles heel of this architecture.

</details>

<a id="7.4"></a>
## 7.4 确定达成目标状态的方法

原文：7.4. Define an Approach to Target State。PDF 物理页：[87]。状态：`source-compared-revised`。

数据齐备之后，审视整体图景：企业想去哪里，作用于企业的外部与内部力量，企业拥有的资源，以及最终所需的结构性与行为性变革。每一项都提供新的规格，每一项都细化对差距的看法。有些需求可能尚未针对期望状态进行审查；只要不违反期望状态与目标，它就是需要记录的候选。

架构师最大的价值在于把事实关联起来，并识别企业应当投资和改进的潜在新运营模型、组织模型与能力。


### 语义规则与适用边界

- 未完整验证的需求若不违背期望状态和目标，可登记为候选。
  - 适用边界：记录候选不等于审批通过或自动进入实施。
  - PDF 87：With all the data gathered, look at the whole picture: where the Enterprise wants to go, the forces acting on the Enterprise from outside and within, resources it possesses, and finally the structural and behavioral changes needed. Each providing new specification. Each refining the view of the gaps. Some of the requirements may be not vetted against the desired state. As long as it is not in violation of the desired state and the objectives, it is a candidate that needs to be recorded.

7.4. Define an Approach to Target State

<a id="7.4.1"></a>
## 7.4.1 确认企业变革属性

原文：7.4.1. Confirm Enterprise Change Attributes。PDF 物理页：[87]。状态：`source-compared-revised`。

这一步考察如何在实施架构时考虑组织文化：从转型能力与技能组合的角度评估各业务单元与整个企业。这些评估应记录在**实施因素目录**中，作为决策档案与记录。文化对战略架构非常重要，因为战略是长期的，而文化往往也是长期形成的；让二者同步，是构建成功架构的关键。这一步与战略架构相关的其他内容包括：评估塑造战略需求的语境，以及对架构愿景与候选架构做差距分析。


### 语义规则与适用边界

- 变革能力、技能和文化评估记录在实施因素目录，保留决策依据。
  - 适用边界：文化不是表面沟通偏好，而影响长期变革可行性。
  - PDF 87：This step looks at how to implement an architecture taking the organization culture into consideration when assessing the business units and overall Enterprise in terms of their transition capabilities and skill sets. These assessments should be documented in an Implementation Factor catalog so that it can be used as an archive and record of decisions taken. Culture is very important to strategy architecture since strategies are long term, and often culture is set for the long term. Getting these two in sync is paramount to building a successful architecture. Other components of this step that are relevant to the strategy architecture include assessing the context that shaped the need for the strategy and performing a gap analysis of the Architecture Vision to the candidate architecture.

### 原文内容单元

<details><summary>展开英文原文及文本块定位</summary>

**G186:p0087:c004 · PDF 87**

7.4.1. Confirm Enterprise Change Attributes

**G186:p0087:c005 · PDF 87**

This step looks at how to implement an architecture taking the organization culture into consideration when assessing the business units and overall Enterprise in terms of their transition capabilities and skill sets. These assessments should be documented in an Implementation Factor catalog so that it can be used as an archive and record of decisions taken. Culture is very important to strategy architecture since strategies are long term, and often culture is set for the long term. Getting these two in sync is paramount to building a successful architecture. Other components of this step that are relevant to the strategy architecture include assessing the context that shaped the need for the strategy and performing a gap analysis of the Architecture Vision to the candidate architecture.

</details>

<a id="7.4.2"></a>
## 7.4.2 制定价值主张

原文：7.4.2. Develop Value Proposition。PDF 物理页：[87, 88]。状态：`source-compared-revised`。

重要的是，不仅战略架构的价值主张要被利益相关者理解，所需的工作量也要被完整接受。这种认可与理解应体现在一张简单的**解决方案概念图**中，说明解决方案的主要组件，以及解决方案将如何正面影响业务。由于价值主张特定于利益相关者的利益与关注点，这一步需要格外留意：定义良好的价值主张是战略架构成功的关键。对任何架构而言，子步骤包括：

- **风险评估**——借助风险管理流程，确定与愿景相称的风险水平
- **确定价值**——把价值链接到与利益相关者或利益相关者群体相关的工作包
- **确定关键绩效指标（KPI）**——可关联到关注点、风险评估与价值

在战略架构中，确定 KPI 对治理是必要的。

确定价值主张及其与各类利益相关者和交付物的链接，有助于从多个视角形成基线与目标环境的高层定义。战略全是高层概念，但就这些概念达成一致，是成功形成并坚持愿景的关键。


### 语义规则与适用边界

- 利益相关者不仅理解价值，也须接受实现价值所需的全部工作。
  - 适用边界：解决方案概念图表达这种共同理解，生成图本身不能证明接受。
  - PDF 87：It is important that not only the value proposition for strategy architecture be understood by stakeholders but also the effort needed is accepted in its entirety. Consent and understanding should be manifested in a simple Solution Concept diagram that illustrates the major components of the solution and how the solution will positively impact the business. Since the value proposition is specific to stakeholder interests and concerns, it is important to pay close attention in this step as well- defined value propositions are key to strategy architecture success. For any architecture, sub-steps involve:
- 战略架构为治理定义 KPI，并关联关注点、风险和价值。
  - 适用边界：KPI 选取应随具体利益相关者和工作包，不能自动套通用分数。
  - PDF 87：Determining the KPIs is necessary in the strategy architecture in connection to governance.

### 原文内容单元

<details><summary>展开英文原文及文本块定位</summary>

**G186:p0087:c006 · PDF 87**

7.4.2. Develop Value Proposition

**G186:p0087:c007 · PDF 87**

It is important that not only the value proposition for strategy architecture be understood by stakeholders but also the effort needed is accepted in its entirety. Consent and understanding should be manifested in a simple Solution Concept diagram that illustrates the major components of the solution and how the solution will positively impact the business. Since the value proposition is specific to stakeholder interests and concerns, it is important to pay close attention in this step as well- defined value propositions are key to strategy architecture success. For any architecture, sub-steps involve:

**G186:p0087:c008 · PDF 87**

• Risk Assessment – leverage risk management processes to determine the level of risk appropriate to the vision

**G186:p0087:c009 · PDF 87**

• Determine Value – link value to work packages as they pertain to stakeholders or stakeholder groupings

**G186:p0087:c010 · PDF 87**

• Determine Key Performance Indicators (KPIs) – can be associated with concerns, risk assessment, and value

**G186:p0087:c011 · PDF 87**

Determining the KPIs is necessary in the strategy architecture in connection to governance.

**G186:p0088:c000 · PDF 88**

Determining the value proposition and how it is linked to various stakeholders and deliverables will help formulate very high-level definitions of the baseline and target environments from multiple points of view. Strategy is all about high-level concepts, but agreement on these concepts is key for a successful vision to be formulated and adhered to.

</details>

<a id="7.4.3"></a>
## 7.4.3 识别并排序工作包

原文：7.4.3. Identify and Sequence Work Packages。PDF 物理页：[88]。状态：`source-compared-revised`。

把各项活动按逻辑归组为工作包。这样就能评估缺失的业务能力，并在解决方案栏中为差距提出候选解决方案，对可能指向新开发的活动提出建议。这一步为解决方案交付做准备，因为新开发可能已经暗示要使用外部服务提供商。

完成排序并筛选出相关的架构需求之后，候选路线图与候选目标架构就绪，可以构建架构愿景。通过整合前几步的工作包来创建路线图初版，同时记住该路线图将链接到后续阶段。

在最宽泛的层面上，路线图应定义业务想去哪里、如何到达、通过什么手段到达。始终关注该路线图得以实施所需的充分详细程度，应能防止架构滑向不同的结果。


### 语义规则与适用边界

- 工作包整合排序后形成候选路线图与候选目标，支持构造愿景。
  - 适用边界：路线图此时仍是候选，需后续资源与工作承诺。
  - PDF 88：Having done the sequencing and sifting down to relevant architecture requirements, the candidate roadmap and candidate Target Architecture are ready to construct the Architecture Vision. Create the initial version of the roadmap by consolidating the work packages from the previous steps while keeping in mind that this roadmap will link to subsequent phases.

### 原文内容单元

<details><summary>展开英文原文及文本块定位</summary>

**G186:p0088:c001 · PDF 88**

7.4.3. Identify and Sequence Work Packages

**G186:p0088:c002 · PDF 88**

Logically group the various activities into work packages. This way the missing business capabilities can be assessed and, in the solutions column, proposed solutions for the gaps and activities that might orient towards a new development can be recommended. This step allows us to prepare for solution delivery, as the new developments might already hint at using external service providers.

**G186:p0088:c003 · PDF 88**

Having done the sequencing and sifting down to relevant architecture requirements, the candidate roadmap and candidate Target Architecture are ready to construct the Architecture Vision. Create the initial version of the roadmap by consolidating the work packages from the previous steps while keeping in mind that this roadmap will link to subsequent phases.

**G186:p0088:c004 · PDF 88**

At the broadest level, the roadmap should define where the business wants to go, how it will get there, and by which means. Keeping an eye on the sufficient level of detail needed for this roadmap to be implemented should forbid the architecture to transition to different results.

</details>

<a id="7.5"></a>
## 7.5 最终确定架构愿景与目标架构

原文：7.5. Finalize Architecture Vision and Target Architecture。PDF 物理页：[88, 89]。状态：`source-compared-revised`。

收拾工作包与能力之间的所有松散环节或不匹配；解决对候选架构的影响，并通过利益相关者关注点权衡分析，解决跨目标架构的影响。路线图应在广度上足以呈现清晰成果，在深度上足够浅，以便勾勒工作包而不过度细化。过渡与迁移计划同样必须展示实现路线图所需的最小活动集。制定实施计划时必须考虑企业语境，因为不同业务会有不同的方法需要考虑。

针对上述两点，应遵循的子步骤包括：

- **语境评估**——在能力、价值与风险评估的语境中评估路线图组件与工作包
- **描述候选过渡架构**——若目标架构沿路线图存在重大变更点，就创建支持新模型的过渡架构，识别过渡中将使用的构建块，识别回应利益相关者关注点的视图，并识别规格
- **解决跨架构的影响**——确定影响，并与风险管理互动，制定过渡计划
- **执行权衡分析**——与需求管理流程互动以更新需求，与风险管理互动以根据权衡更新风险
- **由适当的利益相关者批准目标状态**——在组织承诺开展这项工作之前，你并没有路线图；没有对所需工作的同意，你只有变革的意向


### 语义规则与适用边界

- 组织同意实施所需工作且适当利益相关者批准后，才形成有承诺的路线图。
  - 适用边界：signed up 在这里是承诺，不必然要求法律合同。
  - PDF 89：• Have the Target State Approved by the Appropriate Stakeholder(s) – you do not have a roadmap until the organization has signed up to do the work. Without an agreement to do the required work you only have an intention to change

### 原文内容单元

<details><summary>展开英文原文及文本块定位</summary>

**G186:p0088:c005 · PDF 88**

7.5. Finalize Architecture Vision and Target Architecture

**G186:p0088:c006 · PDF 88**

Tie-up any loose ends or mismatch in work packages and capabilities; resolve the impacts to the candidate architecture, and resolve impacts across the Target Architecture by performing stakeholder concern trade-off analysis. The roadmap should be significant in breadth for clear outcomes but shallow enough in depth to outline work packages without going into too much detail. The transition and migration plan must likewise demonstrate a minimum activity necessary to realize the roadmap. It is key to take the context of the Enterprise into account when formulating the implementation plan since there will be different approaches to consider depending on the business.

**G186:p0088:c007 · PDF 88**

Sub-steps to follow for both of these points include:

**G186:p0088:c008 · PDF 88**

• Context Assessment – assess the roadmap components and work packages in the context of the capability, value, and risk assessment

**G186:p0088:c009 · PDF 88**

• Describe Candidate Transition Architecture – where there are significant points being changed in the Target Architecture along the roadmap, create a transition architecture that supports new models, identify building blocks to be used in the transition, identify views that address stakeholder concerns, and identify specifications

**G186:p0088:c010 · PDF 88**

• Resolve Impacts Across the Architecture – determine the impact and interact with risk management to create a plan for the transition

**G186:p0088:c011 · PDF 88**

• Perform Trade-off Analysis – interact with the requirements management process to update requirements and with risk management to update risk based on these trade-offs

**G186:p0089:c000 · PDF 89**

• Have the Target State Approved by the Appropriate Stakeholder(s) – you do not have a roadmap until the organization has signed up to do the work. Without an agreement to do the required work you only have an intention to change

</details>

<a id="7.6"></a>
## 7.6 结论

原文：7.6. Conclusion。PDF 物理页：[89]。状态：`source-compared-revised`。

沟通架构愿景，并与利益相关者、评审周期和目标一起填充治理模型与流程。确保利益相关者与决策者理解、同意并授予继续填充 EA 全景的许可。这一针对既定愿景、目标架构与路线图的推进许可，将约束并指导所有后续架构工作。当支持战略的架构就绪时，价值链、价值流、组织地图、战略地图或平衡计分卡的创建才能有意义地完成。

需要合理化处理的重复工作清单，以及保持与改进能力的图表，会被填充到路线图中。利益相关者已成功指导架构的创建，并填充了治理细节，以便进一步细化和实施架构。这就是**上层架构**，它将指导和引领支持组合的架构。

成功的衡量标准是：在目标状态上达成一致，且决策者与利益相关者清楚理解实现目标状态所需的工作量。

尾注

[1] 使用“期望的”（desired）一词是有意为之，意在表明人很难预见并考虑未来的变化参数。在关键决策者达成共识之前，评估期间收集的数据只是一种意见或愿望。一旦确认，它就成为候选目标状态；一旦获得资金或获批，它就成为目标状态。

[2] 上层架构是指在架构工作请求范围内约束、引导并指导 EA 全景内容填充的架构。支持战略的架构是支持组合的架构的上层架构；支持组合的架构是架构项目的上层架构；架构项目是支持解决方案交付的架构的上层架构。


### 语义规则与适用边界

- desired 数据在共识前是意见，确认后为候选，获资金或签署批准后为目标。
  - 适用边界：该状态转换是本指南语境，不能用自动生成的阶段标签代替真实授权。
  - PDF 89：[1] Use of the term “desired” is intentional to communicate the fact that it is difficult for a human to foresee and consider change parameters in the future. Until a consensus is reached across key decision-makers, data gathered during assessment is an opinion or a wish. Once confirmed, it becomes a candidate target state. Once funded or signed off, it becomes the target state. [2] Superior architecture is an architecture that constrains, guides, and directs population of the EA Landscape within the scope of the Request for Architecture Work. Architecture to Support Strategy is the superior architecture for Architecture to Support Portfolio. Architecture to Support Portfolio is the superior architecture for the Architecture Project. The Architecture Project is the superior architecture for Architecture to Support Solution Delivery.
- 继续推进许可及已同意愿景、目标和路线图约束后续全景填充。
  - 适用边界：相关图表的生成要反映已确认战略，不能逆向伪造批准。
  - PDF 89：Communicate the Architecture Vision and populate the governance model and process with stakeholders, review cycle, and objectives. Ensure that stakeholders and decisions-makers understand, agree with, and provide the license to proceed with populating the EA Landscape. This license to proceed with the stated vision, Target Architecture, and the roadmap constrains and guides all future architecture work. Creation of a value chain, value streams, organization maps, strategy map, or balanced scorecard can be completed meaningfully when the Architecture to Support Strategy is ready.

## 8. 走查：支持组合的架构

原文：8. Walk Through Architecture to Support Portfolio。PDF 物理页：[90]。状态：`section-container`。

本节仅为原文标题，正文见所属子节。


### 原文内容单元

8. Walk Through Architecture to Support

**G186:p0090:c001 · PDF 90**

Portfolio

<a id="8.1"></a>
## 8.1 引言

原文：8.1. Introduction。PDF 物理页：[90, 91, 92, 93, 94]。状态：`source-compared-revised`。

几乎所有的 EA 工作——无论是外部顾问还是内部 EA 团队——都是为一家已经存在了一段时间的企业启动的。无论是否被明确发起或承认，架构已经存在，解决方案正在依据该架构交付。即使是第一次创建支持战略的架构，也已有正在进行的努力及其影响需要纳入考虑。

支持组合的架构的首要目标，是识别项目、识别依赖与协同、并对项目进行优先级排序和启动。从这个角度看，似乎全部工作都限于阶段 F，以完成架构工作并转入解决方案交付工作。

企业的解决方案是在一个连续谱上交付的。该连续谱分为四个阶段，都聚焦于实现既定目标：

- 在给定能力上与其他市场参与者保持同等水平
- 保持某项能力对其他参与者的领先优势
- 在能力上创造新的差异化
- 创造新市场与收入流

一旦实现了新能力或能力差异化，就必须维持这一增量优势。

图 8-1：能力与项目连续谱。

支持组合的架构必须考虑现有的隐式或显式目标架构，以及在建项目带来的影响。因此，真正意义上的这项工作始于 ADM 的阶段 H。当约束支持项目的架构的所有规格都已定义、被理解并签署确认时，工作才算完成；换言之，组合所触发的解决方案交付项目所需的阶段 A 工作已经完成。

在此过程中，架构提供一种数据驱动的方法，降低某一组决策者因过去的惯性而攫取大部分可用预算的可能性。实现方式是开发适当的模型、同类比较，以及对 EA 全景的增量探索，以评估影响与依赖。

支持组合的架构必须在预算编制前至少 30 天结束。最佳实践是把这项工作与企业的业务周期错开至少一个季度（三个月）。

这项工作回答的问题是：

- 架构是否足够新、足够贴近现状，可以指导决策？
- 所分配的预算推动企业接近目标状态的可信度有多高？
- 针对风险的控制是否足以触发并指导可行的替代行动？
- 应以何种频率检查已交付的解决方案，以确认方向总体正确？
- 当任何权衡标准受到影响时，如何识别并启动变更？

从项目群与项目管理的概念看，组合可以包含没有明确结束日期的运营改进工作。当 EA 全景中相互关联、彼此衔接的部分得到改进时，企业的内在价值会提升。从 EA 的角度看，组合关注的是提升内在价值、降低风险因素。

表 8-1 汇总了相关活动以及 ADM 阶段中适当步骤的使用；本章其余部分将详细讨论表中内容。

表 8-1：汇总表——ADM 阶段与支持组合的架构

| 主题 | 对应的 TOGAF ADM 阶段 |
|---|---|
| 把工作包分组到主题（8.2 节） | 部分战略层阶段 H。<br>企业语境：在建项目与差距；本财年路线图。<br>EA 能力特有的语境：目标；架构工作请求。<br>部分战略层阶段 A。<br>企业语境：更新后的战略架构；更新后的路线图。<br>EA 能力特有的语境：工作包与主题；利益相关者优先级权衡；更新后的架构规格；价值主张的可追溯矩阵。<br>部分战略层阶段 G。<br>企业语境：开展架构合规审查；风险评估。 |
| 平衡机会与可行性（8.3 节） | 部分能力层阶段 B、C、D。<br>对组合中的每项能力或项目：细化规格以估算工作量规模；识别参考架构与市场基准；识别候选 ABB；识别解决方案构建块（SBB，可选）。<br>部分能力层阶段 E。<br>对组合中的每个项目：识别解决方案提供商；就绪度评估；收集估算；评估解决方案选项的可行性与适配性。<br>部分能力层阶段 F。<br>对组合中的每项能力：初版/草稿实施与迁移计划；草稿治理计划。<br>部分项目层阶段 A。<br>对组合中的每个项目：候选概念验证工作包（按需）；草稿成功度量。 |
| 进入预算（8.4 节） | 部分能力层阶段 A。<br>对组合中的每项能力或项目：更新路线图；更新风险矩阵；更新工作包与架构规格。<br>部分能力层阶段 F。<br>对组合中的每个项目：填充治理与批准计划。<br>部分能力层阶段 G。<br>对组合中的每个项目：最终确定治理模型与计划。 |
| 建立交付信心（8.5 节） | 部分企业层阶段 F。<br>企业语境：启动架构工作的完成；定义目标过渡架构；最终确定工作量与资源估算；在项目特定治理模型中定义偏差度量；更新风险矩阵。<br>EA 能力特有的语境：修订 EA 治理；修订 EA 参与模型；修订 EA 组织模型。 |

相关结构化资料：[source-tables](../tables/G186.json)。本节表格全文已对照；独立结构化表保留单元格与位置。
相关结构化资料：[figure-8-1](../figures/G186/fig8-1.json)。本节核对图注及正文语义；图内标签、拓扑和甘特任务的完整审校状态以独立图记录为准，本轮正文覆盖不会自动升级。

### 语义规则与适用边界

- 组合工作须计入既有隐式/显式目标与在建项目影响，从阶段 H 的观察输入着手。
  - 适用边界：与 A 项目启动并不互相替代；约束项目的规格定义、理解与签署是完成条件。
  - PDF 90：It is imperative that Architecture to Support Portfolio takes into account an existing implicit or explicit Target Architecture and the impact driven by in-flight projects. Hence, in true sense, this work starts in Phase H of the ADM. The work is considered complete when all the specifications that constrain the Architecture to Support Project are defined, understood, and signed off. In other words, the need to perform Phase A for the solution delivery projects that are triggered by the portfolio is complete.
- 本指南要求组合架构在预算编制前至少 30 天结束，并建议领先至少一季度。
  - 适用边界：这是指南的时间建议/要求层级，不是组织已经采用的财务日历。
  - PDF 91：It is imperative that the Architecture to Support Portfolio concludes at least 30 days before the budget preparation. A best practice is to is offset this work by at least a quarter (three months) from the business cycle of the Enterprise.

### 原文内容单元

<details><summary>展开英文原文及文本块定位</summary>

**G186:p0090:c002 · PDF 90**

8.1. Introduction

**G186:p0090:c003 · PDF 90**

Almost all EA engagements, external or with an in-house EA team, are initiated for an Enterprise that has been in existence for a while. Whether explicitly initiated or acknowledged, an architecture is in place and solutions are being delivered against that architecture. Even when the Architecture to Support Strategy has been created for the first time, there are ongoing efforts and their impact that will have to be accounted for.

**G186:p0090:c004 · PDF 90**

The primary objective of Architecture to Support Portfolio is to identify projects, identify dependencies and synergies, and prioritize and initiate the projects. From that perspective, it would appear that all of the work is confined to Phase F to complete the architecture work and transition to solution delivery work.

**G186:p0090:c005 · PDF 90**

The Enterprise’s solutions are delivered on a continuum. This continuum is split into four phases, all focused on achieving the objective to meet stated goals. These phases are:

**G186:p0090:c006 · PDF 90**

• Stay on par with other players in the market for a given capability

**G186:p0090:c007 · PDF 90**

• Maintain the edge a capability has over other players

**G186:p0090:c008 · PDF 90**

• Create new differentiations in capabilities

**G186:p0090:c009 · PDF 90**

• Create new markets and revenue streams

**G186:p0090:c010 · PDF 90**

Once a new capability or a differentiation in a capability is achieved, the incremental advantage will have to be maintained.

**G186:p0090:c011 · PDF 90**

Figure 8-1: Capability and Project Continuum

**G186:p0090:c012 · PDF 90**

It is imperative that Architecture to Support Portfolio takes into account an existing implicit or explicit Target Architecture and the impact driven by in-flight projects. Hence, in true sense, this work starts in Phase H of the ADM. The work is considered complete when all the specifications that constrain the Architecture to Support Project are defined, understood, and signed off. In other words, the need to perform Phase A for the solution delivery projects that are triggered by the portfolio is complete.

**G186:p0091:c000 · PDF 91**

In doing to so, the architecture provides a data-driven approach to reduce the possibility of one set of decision-makers netting the majority of the available budget because of the way it has been in the past. This is achieved by developing appropriate models, like-to-like comparison, and incremental exploration of the EA Landscape to assess impacts and dependencies.

**G186:p0091:c001 · PDF 91**

It is imperative that the Architecture to Support Portfolio concludes at least 30 days before the budget preparation. A best practice is to is offset this work by at least a quarter (three months) from the business cycle of the Enterprise.

**G186:p0091:c002 · PDF 91**

Questions answered by this effort are:

**G186:p0091:c003 · PDF 91**

• Is the architecture recent and current enough to guide decisions?

**G186:p0091:c004 · PDF 91**

• What is the confidence that the allocated budget drives the Enterprise closer to target state?

**G186:p0091:c005 · PDF 91**

• Are the controls on risks sufficient enough to trigger and guide viable alternate actions?

**G186:p0091:c006 · PDF 91**

• How often is the solution delivered to be inspected to assure general correctness of direction?

**G186:p0091:c007 · PDF 91**

• How to identify and initiate changes when any of the trade-off criteria are impacted?

**G186:p0091:c008 · PDF 91**

When pivoting on program and project management concepts, a portfolio can include operational improvement efforts; not a clearly defined end-date for closure. The intrinsic value of the Enterprise is elevated when related and cohesive parts of the EA Landscape are improved. From an EA point of view, a portfolio addresses improvement of the intrinsic value and reduction of risk factors.

**G186:p0091:c009 · PDF 91**

Table 8-1 summarizes the activities and use of appropriate steps from the ADM phases. The content of the table is discussed in detail in the rest of this chapter.

<a id="8.2"></a>
## 8.2 把工作包分组到主题

原文：8.2. Group Work Packages to Themes。PDF 物理页：[95, 96, 97]。状态：`source-compared-revised`。

> **术语说明**
>
> “举措”“组合”“项目群”这类术语带有组织特定的含义，常常使我们偏离要传达的信息。多数定义源自投资管理概念，其本质是把组合视为与目标匹配、在风险与绩效之间取得平衡的资产组合。
>
> 项目管理协会（PMI）的定义是：“组合是作为一组管理的项目群、项目和/或运营。组合的组成部分未必相互依赖甚至未必相关，但它们作为一组共同管理以实现战略目标。”以及：“项目群是以协调方式管理的一组相关项目，以获得单独管理它们所无法获得的收益。”
>
> 按 Robert G. Cooper 的说法：“组合是新建和既有产品或服务开发工作的动态集合，用于响应动态机会、多重目标与战略考量、项目间的相互依赖以及多个决策者与地点，对资源进行分配、降低优先级或重新分组。”
>
> 这些定义都没有明确处理企业语境下工作的连续性与关联性。为避免这类局限，本文档改用“主题”，表示工作包应按某种方式分组，以便填充 EA 全景中相邻的部分。一个主题可能填充运营卓越能力全景，另一个主题可能填充财务控制能力。

启动这项工作所需的最小数据集是：

- 本财年路线图（在可获范围内）
- 在建项目清单及其与目标的关系
- 下一财年的战略架构（差距、工作包与候选路线图），来自支持战略的架构
- 利益相关者、决策者与实施者目录
- 风险目录

> 注：本财年的待办事项不必担心，因为支持战略的架构已经处理了它们。

结合企业所处的语境与 EA 项目，从上层架构（支持战略的架构）创建的现状架构出发，开发**基线架构**。基线架构不是一件实体物品，而是时间上的一个参照点，定义用于价值报告的度量与测度。基线是一种集体视图，为在建项目所增加的价值记功。所有价值评估与权衡都应针对基线进行。

基线架构把在建项目对照目标架构中定义的新目标进行归组。在建项目可能无法与目标架构整齐对齐。当一个项目对齐多个目标时，把该工作的功劳分配给所有目标以创建基线，不会影响价值报告；现状与目标架构之间差距的影响通常会更大。

以架构愿景为参照、以工作包清单为基础，开发一组主题（如果先前的架构工作——支持战略的架构——尚未定义主题）。可能需要创建多个基线，每个主题一个。主题通过分解当前与目标的组织结构、生产力、差异化与扩展目标来定义。组织结构阐明了利益相关者、决策者与实施者及其利益与关注点。当工作包在主题之间移动时，评估对利益相关者、决策者与实施者的影响。由于依赖尚未完成的组织变革，工作包的分组结果可能不是最优的。

首次在企业中开展 EA 活动时，可以安全地假定：当年创建项目时并没有使用目标过渡架构。目标架构与差距是由推动预算编制和预算分配的人推断出来的。许多在建项目的目标完成日期可能超出未来几个业务周期。改变这些举措的进程需要时间，因此第一轮架构工作的成果可能不是最优的。重新审视架构项目创建的差距清单与工作包，并因在建项目和本财年任何推断出的路线图而做适当调整。确定这一清单的优先级、进行估算并排序，就是支持组合的架构的工作范围。

为识别工作的优先级，构建表 8-2。填充该表构成进一步细化 EA 全景的基础；表中任何没有数据的单元格都表明架构尚不完整。

表 8-2：工作包分组

| 组合主题 | 工作包名称 | 所需工作包 | 重要性 | 影响实现时间线 | 所需工作量 | 投资规模 |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
|  |  |  |  |  |  |  |
|  |  |  |  |  |  |  |
|  |  |  |  |  |  |  |

工作包的重要性从战略架构继承而来。后三列将随着架构进一步开发而填充。如前所述，工作包与“主题”的关联，是借助提升企业内在价值、填充 EA 全景中相互衔接部分的视角来建立的。

分析组合主题与利益相关者关注点之间的映射，可以识别每个组合需要参与的那部分利益相关者。对每个组合，重申催生该工作包的内部与外部力量没有变化。识别所需资源，并跟踪跨组织边界的资源；多数组织通常需要繁琐的流程才能调动资源，识别出这种需要会改变工作包的依赖与优先级。

利用先前架构工作中的利益相关者关注点以及工作包的新分组，执行权衡分析，量化差距的变化以及对实现目标状态时间的级联影响。识别新风险并制定适当的控制。利用表 8-2 以及通过差距建立的工作包到目标的映射，重申组合所交付的价值主张与目标一致。

工作包带有一个属性，标明它是新工作还是本财年工作的延续。从此刻起，决定是否投资的是工作包在缩短通往目标状态路径上的价值；当前工作的延续可以被纳入考虑，但不是决定因素。此时，每个主题（进而每个组合）都有了一个合理的候选架构愿景。

相关结构化资料：[source-tables](../tables/G186.json)。本节表格全文已对照；独立结构化表保留单元格与位置。

### 语义规则与适用边界

- 基线是特定时点的参照和度量基础，纳入在建项目贡献；价值评估和权衡针对基线。
  - 适用边界：不是仅保存物理配置快照；各主题可能有独立基线。
  - PDF 96：Given the context surrounding the Enterprise and the EA project, develop a Baseline Architecture from the current state architecture created by the superior architecture (Architecture to Support Strategy). The Baseline Architecture is not a physical thing. It is a point of reference in time, defining a metric and a measure to enable value reporting. The baseline is a collective view that provides credit for value added by in-flight projects. All value assessment and trade-off shall be performed against the baseline.
- 工作包分组表任何必需数据格缺失意味着该架构尚不完整。
  - 适用边界：空白模板不是已完成项目数据；后续估算逐步补齐。
  - PDF 96：To identify the prioritization of the effort, build Table 8-2. Populating the table forms the basis for performing further elaboration of the EA Landscape. Any cell in this table without data conveys that the architecture is not complete.
- 新建/延续属性要保留，投资取决于通向目标的价值，延续现有投入不是决定条件。
  - 适用边界：不能以沉没成本自动批准结转。
  - PDF 97：The work packages carry an attribute to identify whether they are new or a carry-over from the current fiscal year’s effort. From now on, the merits of the work package in shortening the path to target state drives decisions to invest. Continuation of the current efforts may be factored in, but they are not a determining factor. Now, a reasonable candidate Architecture Vision for each theme, and hence, a portfolio is created.
