### 原文内容单元

<details><summary>展开英文原文及文本块定位</summary>

**G186:p0095:c000 · PDF 95**

8.2. Group Work Packages to Themes

**G186:p0095:c001 · PDF 95**

Terminology

**G186:p0095:c002 · PDF 95**

Terms like “initiative”, “portfolio”, and “program” carry organizational connotations and often derail us from communicating the message. Most of the definitions derive from investment management concepts, which essentially states portfolio as a mix of assets that matches the objectives balancing risks against performance.

**G186:p0095:c003 · PDF 95**

As defined by the Project Management Institute: “A portfolio is a collection of programs, projects, and/or operations managed as a group. The components of a portfolio may not necessarily be interdependent or even related, but they are managed together as a group to achieve strategic objectives.” And: “A program is a group of related projects managed in a coordinated manner to obtain benefits not available from managing them individually.”

**G186:p0095:c004 · PDF 95**

NOTE

**G186:p0095:c005 · PDF 95**

According to Robert G. Cooper: “Portfolio is a dynamic collection of new and existing product or service development efforts, to allocate, de-prioritize, or regroup resources in response to dynamic opportunities, multiple goals, and strategic considerations, interdependence among projects, and multiple decision-makers and locations.”

**G186:p0095:c006 · PDF 95**

All of these definitions do not explicitly address the continuity and connectedness of the efforts in the context of an Enterprise. In order to stay away from such limitations, this document resorted to using “theme” to indicate that work packages should be grouped in such a way as to enable populating neighbors in the EA Landscape. One theme may populate the Operational Excellence capability landscape while another may populate the Financial Controls capability.

**G186:p0095:c007 · PDF 95**

The minimum dataset required to initiate this effort is:

**G186:p0095:c008 · PDF 95**

• Current fiscal year’s roadmap (to the extent available)

**G186:p0095:c009 · PDF 95**

• List of in-flight projects and relationship to objectives

**G186:p0095:c010 · PDF 95**

• Strategic architecture (gaps, work package, and candidate roadmap) for the next fiscal year, from Architecture to Support Strategy

**G186:p0095:c011 · PDF 95**

• Catalog of stakeholders, decision-makers, and implementers

**G186:p0095:c012 · PDF 95**

• Risk catalog

**G186:p0095:c013 · PDF 95**

The backlog from the current fiscal year is not of concern, as the Architecture to

**G186:p0095:c014 · PDF 95**

NOTE

**G186:p0095:c015 · PDF 95**

Support Strategy has accounted for them.

**G186:p0096:c000 · PDF 96**

Given the context surrounding the Enterprise and the EA project, develop a Baseline Architecture from the current state architecture created by the superior architecture (Architecture to Support Strategy). The Baseline Architecture is not a physical thing. It is a point of reference in time, defining a metric and a measure to enable value reporting. The baseline is a collective view that provides credit for value added by in-flight projects. All value assessment and trade-off shall be performed against the baseline.

**G186:p0096:c001 · PDF 96**

The Baseline Architecture groups the in-flight projects against the new objectives defined in the Target Architecture. It is possible that in-flight projects may not align cleanly with the Target Architecture. When a project aligns to more than one objective, assigning credit from such an effort to all objectives to create the baseline will not impact the value reporting. The impact of gaps between current state and Target Architecture will invariably outweigh.

**G186:p0096:c002 · PDF 96**

Using the Architecture Vision as reference and the list of work packages, develop a set of themes, if not previously defined by Architecture to Support Strategy (prior architecture work). It may be necessary to create multiple baselines, one for each theme. Themes are defined by factoring the current and target organizational structure, productivity, differentiation, and scaling objectives. The organization structure articulates stakeholders, decision-makers, and implementers, their interests and concerns. As the work packages are moved across themes, perform an assessment of impact to stakeholders, decision-makers, and implementers. The resulting grouping of the work packages can be suboptimal due to dependency on pending organizational change.

**G186:p0096:c003 · PDF 96**

When performing EA activity for the first time in the Enterprise it is safe to assume that there were no target transition architectures that were used to create projects in the current year. The Target Architecture and gaps were inferred by whoever drove the budget preparation and budget allocation. Many of the in-flight projects could have a target completion date that extends beyond the next couple of business cycles. Altering the course of these initiatives takes time and, hence, suboptimal architectures in the first go around of the architecture effort. Revisit the gaps list created by the Architecture Project and work packages, and make appropriate adjustment due to in-flight projects and any inferred roadmap for the current fiscal year. Prioritizing, estimating, and sequencing of this list is the scope of work for the Architecture to Support Portfolio.

**G186:p0096:c004 · PDF 96**

To identify the prioritization of the effort, build Table 8-2. Populating the table forms the basis for performing further elaboration of the EA Landscape. Any cell in this table without data conveys that the architecture is not complete.

**G186:p0096:c005 · PDF 96**

Table 8-2: Work Package Grouping

**G186:p0096:c006 · PDF 96**

| Portfolio<br>Theme | Work<br>Package<br>Name | Work<br>Package<br>Required | Importance | Impact<br>Realization<br>Timeline | Effort<br>Required | Magnitude of<br>Investment |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
|  |  |  |  |  |  |  |
|  |  |  |  |  |  |  |
|  |  |  |  |  |  |  |

**G186:p0097:c000 · PDF 97**

The importance of a work package is carried over from the strategic architecture. The last three columns will be populated as the architecture is developed further. As noted earlier, the work package to “theme” association is made using the lens of improving intrinsic value of the Enterprise, populating cohesive parts of the EA Landscape.

**G186:p0097:c001 · PDF 97**

Analysis of the mapping between portfolio themes and stakeholder concerns identifies the subset of stakeholders to engage for each portfolio. For each portfolio, reaffirm that there are no changes in the internal and external forces that created the work package. Identify resources required and track the resources that cross organizational boundaries. It is typical for most organizations to require an elaborate process to move resources. Identification of such a need changes the dependencies and priorities of the work packages.

**G186:p0097:c002 · PDF 97**

Using the stakeholder concerns from prior architecture work and the new grouping of the work packages, perform a trade-off analysis to quantify the changes to gaps and cascading impact on time to achieve the target state. Identify any new risks and develop appropriate controls. Using Table 8-2 and the mapping of work package to objectives via gaps, reaffirm that the value proposition delivered by the portfolio is aligned to the objectives.

**G186:p0097:c003 · PDF 97**

The work packages carry an attribute to identify whether they are new or a carry-over from the current fiscal year’s effort. From now on, the merits of the work package in shortening the path to target state drives decisions to invest. Continuation of the current efforts may be factored in, but they are not a determining factor. Now, a reasonable candidate Architecture Vision for each theme, and hence, a portfolio is created.

</details>

<a id="8.3"></a>
## 8.3 平衡机会与可行性

原文：8.3. Balance Opportunity and Viability。PDF 物理页：[97, 98]。状态：`source-compared-revised`。

到目前为止，分析与架构开发严重偏向“由内而外”的方法。现在该向企业外部寻求帮助了。对于正在推动的这类变革，市场上可能存在可以加速的解决方案——无论是否在同一行业垂直领域。技术发展与外部环境变化可能为满足工作包需求提供新选项。考虑供应商、合作伙伴与企业的业务周期，现在就开始识别实施者可能是明智的。实施者不是决策者，也不是利益相关者；把他们纳入利益相关者矩阵不是好做法。

把业务、信息系统与技术架构规格开发到足以在市场上寻找选项的程度。重点更多是识别解决方案背后的动机，而不是确定某个解决方案。如果目的是以数字方式传输信息，就要识别影像方式是否不可接受；这仍为在解决方案交付阶段按需创新留下空间。与此相关的问题是：数据传输是用于记录保存，还是用于交易管理？这种动机识别出构建块的属性，以及在企业中已使用解决方案的潜在复用。评估解决方案选项时，更多应从排除的角度出发，而不是收窄到“那个解决方案”。

在细化架构时，会出现新的风险与依赖，也应制定适当的控制。开发选项、风险与控制矩阵，以支持可行性分析并与利益相关者权衡。持续用这些细化产生的数据填充需求管理职能。识别可作为限制条件加以利用或施加于解决方案的标准与参考架构清单；识别这些标准与架构，会放大并提升来自上层架构的（约束性）架构规格的具体性，也可能提供加速通往解决方案的路径。捕获所有可能的属性，以支撑权衡分析。

此时应深入 EA 存储库，寻找视角、视图、适当的构建块与参考架构，以开发处理差距的方法。视角应为利益相关者与决策者提供与其相关的 EA 全景参照点。持续验证主题内所有工作包的规格都得到同等细化——在决定优先级与资源需求所必需且可能的范围内。

识别可能必须发明解决方案的局部领域。在这种情况下，创建新工作包，在规模化推广之前开展概念验证。要明白概念验证工作实际上是实施，不是架构；架构工作是识别所需的占位符，以分配适当资金并缓解未知因素。支持组合的架构的主要焦点，是最大化利用可用资源所获得的“里程”；第二目标是识别在何种条件下可实现的里程增益；第三是识别达成目标的障碍，并构建工作以减少这些障碍的影响；最终目标是保证投资回报比不被改变。填充实现这四个目标所需的项目清单。

收集所有工作包的工作量与资源估算；重新审视工作包之间的依赖；识别工作包的重要性与影响。验证持票人身份的能力会随语境而变化：企业可能对不止一个场景或组合有同样需求；又如登机，可能涉及多个机构。这类工作包具有高重要性与高影响，需要在整体改进周期中提前投资。

结合可行的解决方案选项开展机会分析。记住，焦点是形成基线估算，并保证目标的可实现性。组合与权衡的验证，聚焦于按主题分组、相关影响与重要性评估。这里做出的决策会影响有限资源在投资连续谱上的分配。


### 语义规则与适用边界

- 市场探索中被识别的实施者不能因参与而自动拥有利益相关者批准权。
  - 适用边界：同一主体若在其他角色有真实授权需单独记录；不是永远排除所有实施者的利益。
  - PDF 97：The analysis and architecture development so far has been heavily focused on an inside-out approach. It is time to seek help outside the Enterprise. For the kind of changes being driven, potentially accelerating solutions might be available in the market – within the same industry vertical or otherwise. Technological developments and environmental changes might present new options to meet the needs of the work packages. Considering business cycles of suppliers, partners, and the Enterprise, it may be prudent to initiate identification of implementers now. These implementers are not decisions-makers or stakeholders. It is not good practice to include them in the stakeholder matrix.
- 概念验证属于实施；架构负责识别所需工作包、资金位置与未知风险。
  - 适用边界：不把完成架构分析声称为验证已执行，SBB 识别在表中为可选。
  - PDF 98：Identify pockets where a solution may have to be invented. In such a case, create new work packages to perform proof-of-concept validations before scaling out. Understand that proof-of-concept work is actually implementation, not architecture. Architecture work is identifying the placeholder required to allocate appropriate funds and mitigate unknowns. The main focus of the Architecture to Support Portfolio is to maximize the mileage gained with available resources. The second objective is to identify conditions under with projected mileage gain is achievable. The third is to identify barriers to achieve the goal and build efforts to diminish the impact of such barriers. The final objective is to provide assurance of investment to reward ratio being unaltered. Populate the list of projects required to meet these four objectives.

**G186:p0097:c004 · PDF 97**

8.3. Balance Opportunity and Viability

**G186:p0097:c005 · PDF 97**

The analysis and architecture development so far has been heavily focused on an inside-out approach. It is time to seek help outside the Enterprise. For the kind of changes being driven, potentially accelerating solutions might be available in the market – within the same industry vertical or otherwise. Technological developments and environmental changes might present new options to meet the needs of the work packages. Considering business cycles of suppliers, partners, and the Enterprise, it may be prudent to initiate identification of implementers now. These implementers are not decisions-makers or stakeholders. It is not good practice to include them in the stakeholder matrix.

**G186:p0097:c006 · PDF 97**

Develop the Business, Information Systems, and Technology Architecture specifications to the extent needed to scout the market for options. The focus is more on identifying the motivations behind the solutions than identifying a solution. If the purpose is to transmit information digitally, identify whether imaging is not an acceptable option. This still leaves the option to innovate, if needed, the right fit at the solution delivery stage. A related question would be: is the transmission of data for record-keeping purposes or transaction management purposes? Such a motivation identifies attributes of the building blocks and potential reuse of solutions already employed in the Enterprise. Assess the solution options more from an exclusion point of view, rather than narrowing down to “the solution”.

**G186:p0097:c007 · PDF 97**

In elaborating the architecture, new risks and dependencies will arise, and so should appropriate controls. Develop a matrix of options, risks, and controls to enable viability analysis and trade-off with

**G186:p0098:c000 · PDF 98**

stakeholders. Keep populating the requirements management function with data from such elaborations. Identify the list of standards and reference architecture that can be leveraged or imposed as limiting conditions on the solution. Identification of such standards and architectures amplifies and drives specificity of the (constraints) architecture specification from the superior architecture. It may also provide an accelerated path to solution. Capture all possible attributes to inform trade-off analysis.

**G186:p0098:c001 · PDF 98**

It is time to reach into the EA Repository for viewpoints, views, appropriate building blocks, and reference architectures to develop an approach to address the gaps. The viewpoints should provide a point of reference to the EA Landscape that is relevant for the stakeholder and decision-maker. Continuously validate that specifications for all work packages in the theme are elaborated equally, to the extent possible and necessary to decide the priority and resource needs.

**G186:p0098:c002 · PDF 98**

Identify pockets where a solution may have to be invented. In such a case, create new work packages to perform proof-of-concept validations before scaling out. Understand that proof-of-concept work is actually implementation, not architecture. Architecture work is identifying the placeholder required to allocate appropriate funds and mitigate unknowns. The main focus of the Architecture to Support Portfolio is to maximize the mileage gained with available resources. The second objective is to identify conditions under with projected mileage gain is achievable. The third is to identify barriers to achieve the goal and build efforts to diminish the impact of such barriers. The final objective is to provide assurance of investment to reward ratio being unaltered. Populate the list of projects required to meet these four objectives.

**G186:p0098:c003 · PDF 98**

Gather effort and resource estimates for all work packages. Revisit the dependencies across work packages. Identify the importance and impact of the work packages. The ability to authenticate the identity of the person carrying a ticket will vary with context. An Enterprise may have the same need for more than one scenario or portfolio. Or, in the case of boarding an aircraft, multiple agencies may have to be involved. Such work packages have high importance and impact, requiring early investments in the overall improvement cycle.

**G186:p0098:c004 · PDF 98**

Perform an opportunity analysis factoring viable options to approach the solution. Remember, the focus is driving a baseline estimate and assurance of achievability of the target. The validation of the portfolio and the trade-off is focused on grouping by theme, related impact, and importance assessment. The decisions driven here impact the distribution of limited resources across the investment continuum.

</details>

<a id="8.4.1"></a>
## 8.4.1 内部参与

原文：8.4.1. Internal Engagement。PDF 物理页：[98, 99]。状态：`source-compared-revised`。

除业务线负责人之外，财务总监办公室与项目管理办公室（PMO）的人员是推动预算的关键。这两个团队的目标根本不同，但每年会汇聚一次——即预算编制之时；汇聚点是预算偏差的趋势。企业基于统计数据制定关于年度资金与预算趋势的指导，却不附带任何关于所交付价值的限定。交付或执行团队通常会申请超过所需，或在需求不足的情况下维持同样的申请额度，唯恐失去资金。

另一个可能出现的因素是：服务消费者（例如销售团队）与服务提供者（例如许可与定价团队）在敏捷性期望上的差距引发冲突。这种冲突会以不同目标或工作名义，造成能力与服务的重复。为预算做准备时，EA 团队利用差距与工作包，努力消除来自过去这些“意见”或“重复”的偏差。

上层架构也很可能建议组织变革。此时，人力资源（HR）团队在预算编制中的作用将比以往更关键。推动决策不是 EA 团队的职责或职能；EA 必须框定对话与方向，以识别领导和推动变革的合适资源。所有相关内部团队——主要是 HR、PMO 与财务——的参与，对成功交付支持组合的架构至关重要。


### 语义规则与适用边界

- 组织变化需要 HR、PMO 和财务参与，EA 负责构建决策讨论及方向。
  - 适用边界：架构团队不能以生成预算方案自行代替决策授权。
  - PDF 99：It is highly likely for the superior architecture to recommend organizational changes as well. In this case, the Human Resources (HR) team is going to play a more critical role in budget preparation than ever before. It is not the responsibility or the function of the EA team to drive decisions. EA has to frame the conversation and the directions to identify the right resources to lead and drive change. It is imperative that the engagement of all concerned internal teams – mainly HR, PMO, and finance – is key to the success of delivering the Architecture to Support Portfolio.

### 原文内容单元

<details><summary>展开英文原文及文本块定位</summary>

**G186:p0098:c006 · PDF 98**

8.4.1. Internal Engagement

**G186:p0098:c007 · PDF 98**

Other than line of business leaders, personnel from the office of the financial controller and Project Management Office (PMO) are key to driving the budget. The objectives of these two teams are fundamentally different, but converge once a year – the time of budget preparation. The convergence is around the trend on variance to budget. Enterprises develop guidance on year-on-year funding and budget trend based on statistical data, without any qualification for the value delivered. It is normal for the delivery or execution teams to ask for more than is needed or to keep the same level of ask,

**G186:p0099:c000 · PDF 99**

without sufficient demand, for fear of losing funding.

**G186:p0099:c001 · PDF 99**

Another factor that could arise is the conflict due to gaps in the agility expectation of the service consumer (say sales team) and that of the service provider (say licensing and pricing team). Such a conflict creates duplication of capabilities and service in the guise of a different objective or effort name. Preparing for the budget, the EA team works to eliminate variations from such “opinions” or “duplications” of the past using gaps and work packages.

**G186:p0099:c002 · PDF 99**

It is highly likely for the superior architecture to recommend organizational changes as well. In this case, the Human Resources (HR) team is going to play a more critical role in budget preparation than ever before. It is not the responsibility or the function of the EA team to drive decisions. EA has to frame the conversation and the directions to identify the right resources to lead and drive change. It is imperative that the engagement of all concerned internal teams – mainly HR, PMO, and finance – is key to the success of delivering the Architecture to Support Portfolio.

</details>

<a id="8.4.2"></a>
## 8.4.2 目标是否已经达成？

原文：8.4.2. Has the Target been Reached?。PDF 物理页：[99, 100]。状态：`source-compared-revised`。

在建立信心、减少预算的人为偏差来源之后，接下来要处理估算的准确性。当变革需要合理数量的概念验证工作，或需要使用专门服务时，估算的准确性会受到质疑。为提高置信度，似乎需要更多时间、更多分析或更多迭代。除时间之外，下面这份简短检查清单可以表明何时应停止迭代：

1. 对每个“主题”，工作包是否已按能力连续谱分类（一个工作包不能同时处理“保持”与“改进”）？
2. 工作包的依赖与级联影响是否得到决策者与实施者的认可？
3. 对 EA 全景的细化与探索是否连续？
4. 风险（未知事件）的缓解与控制是否已加入组合？
5. 每个主题内是否兼顾运营卓越与适用性？
6. 是否存在时效性方面的担忧？
7. 是否已有粗略估算与应急系数（用于应对市场与外部趋势的缓冲百分比）？
8. 架构规格覆盖广度的增长与覆盖深度的增长之比，在迭代之间是否递减？
9. 当前迭代与上一迭代之间的估算差异是否小于应急系数？
10. 这些工作中有多少是支持转型的一次性执行？

当上述第 8 或第 9 项得到肯定回答时，就达到了收益递减点。多数情况下，在启动架构驱动规划周期后的头两三年，EA 团队会在满足第 8 或第 9 项之前用完时间；应计划建议设立一个可自由支配的支出池。

为完成架构工作，按所需与必要的程度更新架构路线图、风险矩阵、架构定义与规格。按需咨询主题专家与利益相关者并开展评审，以验证方向。对每个主题，定义利益相关者与决策者可接受的治理计划与模型。


### 语义规则与适用边界

- 第 8 或第 9 检查得到肯定答复即表明收益递减；早期能力可能先受时间限制。
  - 适用边界：是“或”，不是二者同时满足，也不是十项全 YES；第 6、10 项本来不是相同方向的通过条件。
  - PDF 99：The point of diminishing returns is met when positive responses are given to either (8) or (9) above. Mostly during the first two to three years after initiating an architecture-driven planning cycle, the EA team will run out of time before (8) or (9) could be met. Plan for recommending a discretionary

**G186:p0099:c003 · PDF 99**

8.4.2. Has the Target been Reached?

**G186:p0099:c004 · PDF 99**

Having driven confidence in reducing sources of artificial variance to budget, next to tackle is accuracy of the estimates. When the changes require a reasonable number of proof-of-concept efforts to be done or require employment of specialized services, veracity of the estimates would be questioned. In order to drive the level of confidence, it would appear that more time, more analysis, or more iterations are needed. Other than time, here is a short checklist that will indicate that it is time to stop iterating:

**G186:p0099:c005 · PDF 99**

1. For each “theme”, have the work packages been classified into a capability continuum (a work package cannot address both Sustain and Improve)?

**G186:p0099:c006 · PDF 99**

2. Are the dependencies and cascading impact of work packages acknowledged by decision-makers and implementers?

**G186:p0099:c007 · PDF 99**

3. Is there a contiguous elaboration and exploration of EA Landscape?

**G186:p0099:c008 · PDF 99**

4. Have the mitigations and controls for risks (unknown events) been added to the portfolio?

**G186:p0099:c009 · PDF 99**

5. Is there a blend of operational excellence and fitness for purpose within each theme?

**G186:p0099:c010 · PDF 99**

6. Are there any recency concerns?

**G186:p0099:c011 · PDF 99**

7. Is a raw estimate and contingency factor available (% buffer to account for market and external trends)?

**G186:p0099:c012 · PDF 99**

8. Is the ratio of growth in breadth of coverage architecture specification to depth of coverage diminishing between iterations?

**G186:p0099:c013 · PDF 99**

9. Is the variation in estimates between current and previous iteration less than the contingency factor?

**G186:p0099:c014 · PDF 99**

10. How many of the efforts are one-time executions to support transformations?

**G186:p0099:c015 · PDF 99**

The point of diminishing returns is met when positive responses are given to either (8) or (9) above. Mostly during the first two to three years after initiating an architecture-driven planning cycle, the EA team will run out of time before (8) or (9) could be met. Plan for recommending a discretionary

**G186:p0100:c000 · PDF 100**

spending bucket.

**G186:p0100:c001 · PDF 100**

To complete the architecture work, update the architecture roadmap, risk matrix, architecture definitions, and specifications to the extent needed and necessary. As needed, consult and conduct reviews with SMEs and stakeholders to validate the direction. For each theme, define the governance plan and model that is acceptable to stakeholders and decision-makers.

</details>

<a id="8.5"></a>
## 8.5 建立交付信心

原文：8.5. Drive Confidence of Delivery。PDF 物理页：[100]。状态：`source-compared-revised`。

有用的架构驱动变革并简化决策。预算编制的目标是建立对估算的信心、对按路线图交付的信心，并争取推动变革所需的资源。按主题分组、可追溯到目标的已排序工作包集合，建立对“为什么”和“做什么”的回答的信心；有偏差控制支撑的估算集合，建立对“如何做”和“做多少”的回答的信心；建立一套降低执行决策延误目标状态实现概率的项目治理，服务于该架构的最终目标——平衡创新与审慎的控制。

开发刚好足以支撑预算申请的视图、模型与规格。这些文档由一张矩阵支撑，列明对交付负责的各方，以及对验收、使用和签署负责的各方。成功度量以价值术语表述；控制以成本术语表述；风险与结果以价值术语表述。

启动完成架构工作的活动。这包括填充适当的项目愿景文档、项目架构定义、项目利益相关者清单、沟通计划以及支配权衡的条件。若项目获批执行，填充监控系统所需的各项目数据；按每个项目与“主题”的边界填充依赖矩阵。在支持项目的架构交付之前，架构项目不能算完成。在这一阶段启动工作，传达了战略层面的决策，而这些决策未来可以重新审视。最后一项验证，是确认运营模型（恢复驱动型，还是参与与连续性驱动型）与业务模型对齐。


### 语义规则与适用边界

- 预算支撑材料应包括交付问责方及验收、使用、签署问责方。
  - 适用边界：不得把所有 accountable 都归到同一架构师。
  - PDF 100：Develop just enough views, models, and specifications to support the budget request. These documents are supported by a matrix of accountable parties for delivery and accountable parties for acceptance, usage, and sign-off. Success measures are articulated in value terms – controls in cost measures, and risks and outcome in value measures.
- 组合完成工作需要启动项目层资料与治理，并交付支持项目的架构。
  - 适用边界：监控数据准备以项目获批执行为条件；准备不等于项目已经批准。
  - PDF 100：Initiate activities to complete the architecture work. This involves populating the appropriate project vision documents, project architecture definitions, project stakeholder list, communication plan, and conditions that govern trade-off. Populate the data required by monitoring the system for each project, should the project be approved for execution. Populate the dependency matrix in accordance with the boundaries set for each project and the “theme”. The Architecture Project cannot be completed until the Architecture to Support Project is delivered. Initiating the effort at this stage communicates the decisions at the strategy level that can be revisited in the future. The last validation is to define that the operating model (recovery-driven or engagement and continuity-driven) is aligned to the business model.

### 原文内容单元

<details><summary>展开英文原文及文本块定位</summary>

**G186:p0100:c002 · PDF 100**

8.5. Drive Confidence of Delivery

**G186:p0100:c003 · PDF 100**

Useful architecture drives change and simplifies decision-making. The objective of budget preparation is to drive confidence of estimates, confidence of delivery against the roadmap, and garner the resources required to drive change. The set of prioritized work packages grouped by themes that traces to objectives drives confidence in responses to the “why” and “what” questions. The set of estimates that is backed by variance control drives confidence to the “how” and “how much” questions. Creating a set of project governance that reduces the chances of execution decisions delaying the time to target state serves the final objective of this architecture – balancing innovation and considered controls.

**G186:p0100:c004 · PDF 100**

Develop just enough views, models, and specifications to support the budget request. These documents are supported by a matrix of accountable parties for delivery and accountable parties for acceptance, usage, and sign-off. Success measures are articulated in value terms – controls in cost measures, and risks and outcome in value measures.

**G186:p0100:c005 · PDF 100**

Initiate activities to complete the architecture work. This involves populating the appropriate project vision documents, project architecture definitions, project stakeholder list, communication plan, and conditions that govern trade-off. Populate the data required by monitoring the system for each project, should the project be approved for execution. Populate the dependency matrix in accordance with the boundaries set for each project and the “theme”. The Architecture Project cannot be completed until the Architecture to Support Project is delivered. Initiating the effort at this stage communicates the decisions at the strategy level that can be revisited in the future. The last validation is to define that the operating model (recovery-driven or engagement and continuity-driven) is aligned to the business model.

</details>

<a id="8.6"></a>
## 8.6 源自“野外”随机想法的架构工作请求

原文：8.6. Request for Architecture Work Originating from a Random Idea from the Wild。PDF 物理页：[100, 101]。状态：`source-compared-revised`。

在运行良好、富有创造力的组织中，许多好想法并非来自架构中识别出的差距。在这类组织中，架构工作请求来自某个对改进组织有好想法的人。

面对来自“野外”的请求，实践者通常会与一位强有力的倡导者合作，并识别 EA 全景中的空白。此时几乎不必担心与其他已识别的差距和工作包发生冲突。然而，倡导者对利益相关者偏好与关注点的看法往往有限，甚至狭隘。

实践者必须注意，要依靠企业的使命、愿景与战略，把开发保持在“野外”架构的语境之内。来自野外的请求理应挑战现状；其内在的创造力会受到优秀实践者的欢迎。由于缺少来自战略或组合的指导来约束架构开发，实践者必须确保完成正确利益相关者的识别，并确保关注点反映利益相关者的偏好与优先级——见 5.2.1 节。并非所有倡导者都是利益相关者，而且所有架构项目都受上层架构约束。

需要批判性地思考：为响应来自野外的架构工作请求而开发的架构，应如何准备，才能在企业路线图或实施计划的工作序列中插入最优时点。执行得当，组织就能在创造力与创新，同清晰理解依赖关系所带来的价值实现收益之间取得平衡。

虽然多数来自野外的架构工作请求针对的是支持项目的架构与支持解决方案交付的架构，但强有力的倡导者会推动一项组合举措。


### 语义规则与适用边界

- 创新倡导者未必是有批准权的利益相关者，所有创新架构项目仍受上层架构约束。
  - 适用边界：欢迎挑战现状不免除相关方识别、依赖分析和治理。
  - PDF 101：The Practitioner must take care to stay within the context of the wild architecture development relying on the mission, vision, and strategy of the Enterprise. Requests from the wild should be expected to challenge the status quo. The inherent creativity is welcomed by good Practitioners. Without much guidance from the strategy or portfolio to constrain the architecture development, Practitioners must ensure that identification of the correct stakeholders is completed and that the concerns reflect the stakeholder’s preferences and priorities – see Section 5.2.1. Not all champions are stakeholders, and all Architecture Projects are subject to superior architecture.

### 原文内容单元

<details><summary>展开英文原文及文本块定位</summary>

**G186:p0100:c006 · PDF 100**

8.6. Request for Architecture Work Originating from a

**G186:p0100:c007 · PDF 100**

Random Idea from the Wild

**G186:p0100:c008 · PDF 100**

In a well-run, creative organization many good ideas are not derived from gaps identified in the architecture. In these organizations, a Request for Architecture Work comes from someone with a good idea for improving the organization.

**G186:p0100:c009 · PDF 100**

With a request from the wild, the Practitioner will typically engage with a strong champion and identify holes in the EA Landscape. There is little need to worry about bumping shoulders with other identified gaps and work packages. However, the champion often will have a limited, or myopic, view of the stakeholder’s preferences and concerns.

**G186:p0101:c000 · PDF 101**

The Practitioner must take care to stay within the context of the wild architecture development relying on the mission, vision, and strategy of the Enterprise. Requests from the wild should be expected to challenge the status quo. The inherent creativity is welcomed by good Practitioners. Without much guidance from the strategy or portfolio to constrain the architecture development, Practitioners must ensure that identification of the correct stakeholders is completed and that the concerns reflect the stakeholder’s preferences and priorities – see Section 5.2.1. Not all champions are stakeholders, and all Architecture Projects are subject to superior architecture.

**G186:p0101:c001 · PDF 101**

There is a need for critical thinking around the preparation required to insert the architecture developed in response to a receipt of a Request for Architecture Work from the wild at the optimal point in the sequence of work within the Enterprise’s roadmap, or implementation plan. Well executed, the organization is able to balance creativity and innovation with the benefit derived from clear understanding of dependency to value realization.

**G186:p0101:c002 · PDF 101**

While most Requests for Architecture work from the wild are for Architecture to Support Project and Architecture to Support Solution Delivery, strong champions will drive a portfolio initiative.

</details>

<a id="8.7"></a>
## 8.7 结论

原文：8.7. Conclusion。PDF 物理页：[101]。状态：`source-compared-revised`。

定期开展价值评估与报告，沟通经验教训，以及所创建组合是在交付有机变革、激进创新，还是维持现状。实施项目在项目关闭后数个季度才交付价值；跟踪并报告价值是组合管理者的责任。向组合中加入一个明确的待办事项，以监控和报告已实现的价值。

如果该架构正在支持并购、收购或剥离活动，应包含明确建议，处理技术对简化业务运营、资产与风险核算的影响。

成功的衡量标准是：决策者在多个并行工作流、规划期内所需的总资源以及权衡标准上达成一致。

项目管理协会（PMI）定义：“组合是作为一组管理的项目群、项目和/或运营。其组成部分未必相互依赖甚至未必相关，但作为一组共同管理以实现战略目标。”以及：“项目群是协调管理的一组相关项目，以取得单独管理无法得到的收益。”Robert G. Cooper 将组合描述为新建与既有产品或服务开发工作的动态集合，为回应动态机会、多重目标、战略考虑、项目依赖和多个决策者及地点，分配、降低优先级或重新组织资源。上述定义均未明确处理企业语境下工作的连续性与关联性。因此本指南使用“主题”，使工作包的分组能填充 EA 全景中的相邻部分；例如一个主题填充运营卓越能力全景，另一个填充财务控制能力。


### 语义规则与适用边界

- 组合管理者负责在项目关闭后持续跟踪和报告实现价值，并显式设置待办项。
  - 适用边界：项目完成不等于价值已实现；几个季度是本文经验语境。
  - PDF 101：Conduct periodic value assessment and reporting to communicate lessons learned and whether the portfolio created is delivering organic change, radical innovation, or maintains the status quo. Implementation Projects deliver value a few quarters after the project is closed. It is the responsibility of those managing the portfolio to track and report value. Add to the portfolio an explicit backlog item to monitor and report value realized.

**G186:p0101:c003 · PDF 101**

8.7. Conclusion

**G186:p0101:c004 · PDF 101**

Conduct periodic value assessment and reporting to communicate lessons learned and whether the portfolio created is delivering organic change, radical innovation, or maintains the status quo. Implementation Projects deliver value a few quarters after the project is closed. It is the responsibility of those managing the portfolio to track and report value. Add to the portfolio an explicit backlog item to monitor and report value realized.

**G186:p0101:c005 · PDF 101**

In the event this architecture is supporting a merger, acquisition, or divestiture activity, include explicit recommendations to tackle the impact of technology in easing the business operations, asset, and risk accounting.

**G186:p0101:c006 · PDF 101**

Success is measured by alignment by the decision-makers on a number of concurrent streams, total resources required over the planning horizon period, and trade-off criteria.

**G186:p0101:c007 · PDF 101**

As defined by the Project Management Institute: “A portfolio is a collection of programs, projects, and/or operations managed as a group. The components of a portfolio may not necessarily be interdependent or even related, but they are managed together as a group to achieve strategic objectives.” And: “A program is a group of related projects managed in a coordinated manner to obtain benefits not available from managing them individually.” According to Robert G. Cooper: “Portfolio is a dynamic collection of new and existing product or service development efforts, to allocate, de- prioritize, or regroup resources in response to dynamic opportunities, multiple goals, and strategic considerations, interdependence among projects, and multiple decision-makers and locations.” All of these definitions do not explicitly address the continuity and connectedness of the efforts in the context of an Enterprise. In order to stay away from such limitations, this document resorted to using “theme” to indicate that work packages should be grouped in such a way as to enable populating neighbors in the EA Landscape. One theme may populate the Operational Excellence capability landscape while another may populate the Financial Controls capability.

</details>

<a id="9"></a>
## 9. 走查：支持项目的架构

原文：9. Walk Through Architecture to Support Project。PDF 物理页：[102, 103, 104, 105]。状态：`source-compared-revised`。

在此语境中，架构用于澄清项目的目的与价值，识别处理协同与未来依赖的需求，确保符合架构治理，并支持项目之间的集成与对齐。

本章描述为组合中的一个项目开发架构。工作从识别语境、识别界定愿景、范围与项目应交付价值的上层架构开始。若不对项目在 EA 全景中的位置做初步探索，支持项目的架构就处于易变状态。架构项目中的实践者有责任收集项目未覆盖障碍的线索。项目位于路线图中某个工作包序列的线性位置上；仅凭路线图就有许多线索，可以指出前方危险所在，以及该向谁询问未知的警示信号。

本章的目的是突出 ADM 周期各阶段中开发 EA 的详细程度、时间与广度，聚焦于对项目架构与治理的支持。大部分工作发生在阶段 F 的语境中。

表 9-1 汇总了相关活动以及 ADM 阶段中适当步骤的使用；本章其余部分将详细讨论表中内容。

表 9-1：汇总表——ADM 阶段与支持项目的架构

| 主题 | 对应的 TOGAF ADM 阶段 |
|---|---|
| 确定依赖 | 部分能力层阶段 A。<br>项目群语境：验证时效性；验证利益相关者、成果、时间线；在 EA 全景中定义项目语境。<br>部分能力层阶段 B、C、D。<br>企业语境：评估企业吸收所提议解决方案的就绪度；评估解决方案提供商交付解决方案的能力；确定实施项目的变更范围。<br>项目群语境：细化架构定义；细化架构规格；定义最小化依赖的方法；定义风险控制。 |
| 平衡选项与供应商 | 部分能力层阶段 B、C、D。<br>对每项能力：定义解决方案边界与集成条件；验证 EA 全景中的连续性；创建领域特定工作包。<br>部分能力层阶段 E。<br>对组合中的每个项目：识别候选 ABB 与 SBB；收集估算；制定项目时间线；与上层架构权衡影响；更新路线图；更新风险矩阵；更新工作包与架构规格。 |
| 最终确定范围与预算 | 部分能力层阶段 F。<br>对组合中的每个项目：最终确定估算与时间线；更新企业路线图；填充治理与批准计划。<br>部分能力层阶段 G。<br>对组合中的每个项目：开展架构评审；确认利益相关者批准。<br>层级未注明的阶段 A。<br>对组合中的每个项目：候选概念验证工作包（按需）；候选工作说明书；最终确定利益相关者清单；草稿成功度量。 |
| 准备解决方案交付治理 | 部分项目群层阶段 F。<br>项目群语境：启动架构工作的完成；定义目标解决方案架构；最终确定工作量与资源估算；在项目特定治理模型中定义偏差度量；更新风险矩阵。 |

对支持项目的架构而言，关键聚焦点是：

- **范围界定：**
  - 收到架构工作请求的起因是什么？
  - 我会在哪里发生重叠？我的“邻居”是谁（EA 全景）？
  - 我该在哪里查找（EA 全景：深度、广度、详细程度）？
  - 我的利益相关者/组合指导是否仍然相关（时效性）？

- **领域特定的利益相关者关注点与架构细化：**
  - 视角/利益相关者地图
  - 我需要知道/解决什么？
  - 解决跨架构的影响

- **最终确定目标过渡架构及其价值：**
  - 创建需求与规格
  - 为解决方案交付架构争取架构工作请求

相关结构化资料：[source-tables](../tables/G186.json)。本节表格全文已对照；独立结构化表保留单元格与位置。

### 语义规则与适用边界

- 项目支持架构须先定位上层愿景、范围、价值和路线图中的依赖位置。
  - 适用边界：本章是组合内一个项目的情境；来自外部想法的请求另按 9.5 处理。
  - PDF 102：This chapter describes development of architecture for one project within a portfolio. The effort starts with identifying the context, the superior architecture that defines the visions, the scope, and the value the project should deliver. Without initial exploration about where the project sits inside of the EA Landscape, Architecture to Support Project is in a volatile state. It is the responsibility of the Practitioners working in the Architecture Project to gather hints of uncovered barriers to the project. The project lies inside the roadmap at some linear point in a sequence of work packages. There are many hints from the roadmap alone of where to see danger ahead and who to ask about any unknown warning signs.

9. Walk Through Architecture to Support Project

**G186:p0102:c001 · PDF 102**

In this context, the architecture is used to clarify the purpose and value of the project, identify requirements to address synergy and future dependency, assure compliance with architectural governance, and to support integration and alignment between projects.

This chapter describes development of architecture for one project within a portfolio. The effort starts with identifying the context, the superior architecture that defines the visions, the scope, and the value the project should deliver. Without initial exploration about where the project sits inside of the EA Landscape, Architecture to Support Project is in a volatile state. It is the responsibility of the Practitioners working in the Architecture Project to gather hints of uncovered barriers to the project. There are many hints from the roadmap alone of where to see danger ahead and who to ask about any unknown warning signs.

The purpose is to highlight the level of detail, time, and breadth during the ADM cycle phases for developing an EA as a focus of support to project architecture and governance. Most of the effort happens in the context of Phase F.

<a id="9.1"></a>
## 9.1 确定依赖

原文：9.1. Ascertain Dependencies。PDF 物理页：[105]。状态：`source-compared-revised`。

在整个 ADM 过程中，建议密切关注 EA 全景中的上层架构。上层架构的实践者可能已经指定了一系列内容，后续架构的实践者可以直接拉取下来，作为架构描述的新增片段。

当项目目的已在路线图中指定时，没有太大必要再去探索做架构的理由。实践者可能发现，从组合架构已完成的工作中已经获得了足够的架构愿景。然而，实践者必须采取负责任的行动，确认架构愿景以及若干组合层目标架构组件，以评估时效性的影响（见 3.2.1 节）。

评估时效性是架构项目的脉搏。这涉及从“自下而上”审视 EA 全景中的当前工作，评估时效性对先前 EA 的影响。查看支持组合的架构所产生的一组架构愿景。下列问题可指导评估时效性对先前 EA 工作在使用方面的影响：

- 哪些 EA 正在并行开发？
- 哪些目标正在实现过程中？
- 哪些目标已获批准？
- 时效性对先前 EA 有什么影响？

在先前的 EA 工作经过评审、重申或替换之后，时效性的影响被重置，架构项目的风险显著降低。


### 语义规则与适用边界

- 可复用组合架构愿景，但须复核时效性和相关目标组件。
  - 适用边界：复用不是不检查；评审、重申或替换后才降低陈旧信息风险。
  - PDF 105：There is not much need to explore a reason to do architecture when the purpose of the project has already been specified within a roadmap. The Practitioner may find that they already have a sufficient Architecture Vision from the work that has been done in the portfolio architecture. However, the Practitioner must take responsible action to confirm the Architecture Vision along with a number of portfolio-level Target Architecture components to assess the impact of recency (see Section 3.2.1).

### 原文内容单元

<details><summary>展开英文原文及文本块定位</summary>

**G186:p0105:c004 · PDF 105**

9.1. Ascertain Dependencies

**G186:p0105:c005 · PDF 105**

Throughout the entirety of the ADM, it is recommended to have a close look at the superior architecture in the EA Landscape. It is possible that Practitioners of superior architecture have already specified a list of things which the Practitioners of the preceding architectures are able to pull down to include as new inserts of an architecture description.

**G186:p0105:c006 · PDF 105**

There is not much need to explore a reason to do architecture when the purpose of the project has already been specified within a roadmap. The Practitioner may find that they already have a sufficient Architecture Vision from the work that has been done in the portfolio architecture. However, the Practitioner must take responsible action to confirm the Architecture Vision along with a number of portfolio-level Target Architecture components to assess the impact of recency (see Section 3.2.1).

**G186:p0105:c007 · PDF 105**

Assessing recency is the pulse of the Architecture Project. It will involve looking “bottom-up” at the current work in the EA Landscape to assess the impacts of recency to prior EA. Look at the set of Architecture Visions from the Architecture to Support Portfolio. The following set of questions will guide assessing the impacts of recency to prior EA work for the purpose of use:

**G186:p0105:c008 · PDF 105**

• What EA is parallel in development?

**G186:p0105:c009 · PDF 105**

• Which targets are in the process of being realized?

**G186:p0105:c010 · PDF 105**

• Which targets have been approved?

**G186:p0105:c011 · PDF 105**

• What is effect of recency on prior EA?

**G186:p0105:c012 · PDF 105**

After prior EA work has been reviewed, reaffirmed, or replaced, the effect of recency is reset and mitigates the risk to the Architecture Project significantly.

</details>

<a id="9.1.1"></a>
## 9.1.1 项目不是替换利益相关者的“魔法之地”

原文：9.1.1. Project is not a Magical Place to Swap Out Stakeholders。PDF 物理页：[105, 106]。状态：`source-compared-revised`。

跨架构的完整利益相关者集合是谁？组合层面的利益相关者需要得到重申。

常见的情况是：组织领导者在一个架构项目开始时，强烈希望把上层架构中识别的利益相关者，替换为那些有足够权力阻止或推进**项目**、但并不影响**架构**的利益相关者；参见《TOGAF 标准——ADM 技术》（利益相关者立场分类）。

这会向架构中引入新的项目特定关注点。必须强调：要牢牢把握“在架构项目中拥有高权力的利益相关者”与“仅在实施项目中拥有高权力的利益相关者”之间的区别。归根结底，实践者要处理的是那些掌握架构项目成功钥匙的授权利益相关者的关注点，因为他们有权塑造任何实施项目，使其符合已批准的目标。如果能同时解决两者并顺带获得一些额外收益，识别项目特定利益相关者的关注点可能是有用的；解决实施项目特定的关注点，只能算“锦上添花”。


### 语义规则与适用边界

- 不得以实施项目影响力大的人替换对架构目标有授权的利益相关者。
  - 适用边界：实施项目特定关注点可以在不损害既定目标时兼顾，不能挟持总体价值。
  - PDF 106：This will introduce new project-specific concerns into the architecture. It cannot be stressed enough, to hold on to the distinction between the stakeholders that have high power in the Architecture Project and those that have high power only in relation to the Implementation Project. At the end of the day, the Practitioner addresses the concerns of the empowered stakeholders holding the key to the success of the Architecture Project because they have the power to shape any Implementation Project in order to conform with the approved target. It may be useful to identify the project-specific stakeholders’ concerns if we can solve for both and get something for free. Solving for an Implementation Project- specific concern is what can be called a “nice-to-have”.

### 原文内容单元

<details><summary>展开英文原文及文本块定位</summary>

**G186:p0105:c013 · PDF 105**

9.1.1. Project is not a Magical Place to Swap Out Stakeholders

**G186:p0105:c014 · PDF 105**

Who are the complete set of stakeholders across the architecture? The stakeholders in the portfolio level will need to be reaffirmed.

**G186:p0105:c015 · PDF 105**

It is common to find organizational leaders who, at the start of an Architecture Project, feel a strong need to replace the stakeholders identified in their superior architecture with stakeholders which have a high enough power to block or advance a project but not the architecture; see the TOGAF Standard – ADM Techniques (Classify Stakeholder Positions).

**G186:p0106:c000 · PDF 106**

This will introduce new project-specific concerns into the architecture. It cannot be stressed enough, to hold on to the distinction between the stakeholders that have high power in the Architecture Project and those that have high power only in relation to the Implementation Project. At the end of the day, the Practitioner addresses the concerns of the empowered stakeholders holding the key to the success of the Architecture Project because they have the power to shape any Implementation Project in order to conform with the approved target. It may be useful to identify the project-specific stakeholders’ concerns if we can solve for both and get something for free. Solving for an Implementation Project- specific concern is what can be called a “nice-to-have”.

</details>

<a id="9.1.2"></a>
## 9.1.2 利益相关者与关键参与者

原文：9.1.2. Stakeholders versus Key Players。PDF 物理页：[106]。状态：`source-compared-revised`。

查看组合先前生成的利益相关者地图，评估时效性，把架构项目的完整利益相关者集合对照其已知关注点进行映射。

不要把实施项目特定的利益相关者集合（也就是架构项目的“关键参与者”）纳入利益相关者地图。如有需要，可把这些关键参与者与任何额外的实施项目特定关注点单独映射。拥有不止一套关键利益相关者，会完全阻碍开展权衡的能力。


### 语义规则与适用边界

- 架构利益相关者与实施项目关键参与者分别映射。
  - 适用边界：这是决策语境的区分，不是忽略实施者或否认其可兼任其他真实授权角色。
  - PDF 106：Do not include an Implementation Project-specific set of stakeholders (otherwise known as key players to the Architecture Project) in the Stakeholder Map. If desired, map the key players to any additional Implementation Project-specific concerns separately. Having more than one set of key stakeholders completely blocks the ability to perform trade-off.

<a id="9.1.3"></a>
## 9.1.3 视角与需求

原文：9.1.3. Viewpoints and Requirements。PDF 物理页：[106]。状态：`source-compared-revised`。

在做任何工作之前最重要的一步，是知道自己需要知道什么。一旦拥有描述利益相关者关注点的完整视图集合，你就确切知道自己需要做什么，或者至少知道该去哪里查找需要完成的工作。

从视角库中选择视角或开发新视角时，要问：该视角是否代表了架构项目的完整利益相关者关注点集合？所有利益相关者类别是否都代表了那些拥有目标架构批准权与实施项目决策权的类别？关注点是否被整合并限定为由企业战略派生的主题领域，从而在各架构项目之间保持一致？该视角是否为你需要知道的内容以及应在 EA 全景中查找的位置提供了参照点？

一旦实践者知道需要什么信息、在哪里能找到，就可以安全地继续工作，而不必担心踩到隐喻中的地雷。

查阅架构存储库中的资源，尤其是上层架构的架构规格、需求与工作包，以处理架构项目的利益相关者关注点。


### 语义规则与适用边界

- 视角覆盖对目标有批准权、对实施有相关决策权的主体及其完整关注点。
  - 适用边界：视角须指向所需信息和 EA 全景位置，不能只列图类型。
  - PDF 106：When selecting viewpoints from the viewpoint library or developing new viewpoints, ask if the viewpoint represents the complete set of stakeholder concerns to the Architecture Project. Are all the stakeholders’ classes representative of those which own the approval rights around the Target Architecture and decision rights around the Implementation Project?

9.1.3. Viewpoints and Requirements

The most important piece before doing any work; knowing what you need to know. Once you have a complete set of views which describe the stakeholders’ concerns, you know exactly what you need to do, or at the very least, where to go look to find out what needs to be done.

<a id="9.1.4"></a>
## 9.1.4 与“邻居”交谈

原文：9.1.4. Go Talk to the “Neighbors”。PDF 物理页：[106, 107]。状态：`source-compared-revised`。

在开发候选架构时，成功的关键是了解架构项目在 EA 全景中的邻居，并评估“邻里”的时效性。在可能发生重叠或与另一个架构项目碰撞之前，架构项目还有多少空间？何时必须与邻里对话，并评估其工作的时效性？

更复杂的是：相邻架构当前处于什么状态？相邻架构项目是已批准、处于过渡中，还是正在实现？在其中一个进入过渡状态之前，你可能不必担心与相邻架构项目发生摩擦。

定期与邻居进行必要的对话，使解决跨架构项目影响的过程更容易。与“邻居”的对话越晚，实践者越可能不得不做出本可轻易避免的艰难决策，例如缩小范围（de-scoping）。实践者必须检查候选架构的灵活性，以承受与其他处于多个过渡状态的架构项目共享的易变环境。


### 语义规则与适用边界

- 周期性沟通相邻架构，按状态与时效性分析交叠和影响。
  - 适用边界：不能等碰撞后才缩范围；候选应考虑过渡状态中的环境变化。
  - PDF 107：Have the necessary conversations with the neighbors periodically to make the process of resolving impacts across Architecture Projects easier. The later these conversations with the “neighbors” takes place, the more likely the Practitioner will incur harder decisions, which would have been easily avoided; such as de-scoping decisions. The Practitioner must check the candidate architecture’s flexibility to withstand the volatile environment shared with other Architecture Projects undergoing a number of transitional states.

**G186:p0106:c012 · PDF 106**

In developing a candidate architecture, the key to success is to be aware of the neighbors of the Architecture Project in the EA Landscape and to assess the “neighborhood” for recency. How much

**G186:p0107:c000 · PDF 107**

room is there for the Architecture Project until there would be an overlap or collide with another one?[1] When must you go and have a conversation with the neighborhood and assess their work for recency?

**G186:p0107:c001 · PDF 107**

To add complexity, what is the current status of the neighboring architectures? Are the neighboring Architecture Projects approved, in transition, or becoming realized? You may not have to worry about rubbing shoulders with a neighboring Architecture Project until one of them enters a transition state.

**G186:p0107:c002 · PDF 107**

Have the necessary conversations with the neighbors periodically to make the process of resolving impacts across Architecture Projects easier. The later these conversations with the “neighbors” takes place, the more likely the Practitioner will incur harder decisions, which would have been easily avoided; such as de-scoping decisions. The Practitioner must check the candidate architecture’s flexibility to withstand the volatile environment shared with other Architecture Projects undergoing a number of transitional states.

</details>

<a id="9.1.5"></a>
## 9.1.5 交付与验收能力评估

原文：9.1.5. Delivery and Acceptance Ability Assessment。PDF 物理页：[107]。状态：`source-compared-revised`。

此时是评估组织是否真正准备好开始执行和实现变革的好时机。这涉及识别工作包是否覆盖了业务流程、操作规程、培训等必要变更，以及解决方案交付后必须发生的一切。该评估范围很窄，用于检验邻居本应搭建好的“脚手架”。第二组评估是解决方案交付团队（内部或外部）按架构规格要求交付的能力。项目经理与产品负责人应充分了解保持价值的权衡标准，了解邻居对本项工作的依赖以及本项目对其他工作的依赖，并了解缓解这些风险的控制。


### 语义规则与适用边界

- 就绪评估同时检验组织接受变革的准备和交付团队交付架构规格的能力。
  - 适用边界：涵盖流程、规程、培训、跨项目依赖及风险控制，不限技术可部署。
  - PDF 107：This is an opportune time to assess the readiness of the organization to actually start to execute and realize the change. It involves identifying whether the work packages cover the necessary changes to business processes, operating procedure, training, and everything that has to happen once the solution is delivered. The assessment is narrowly focused to test the scaffolding the neighbors should have in place. A second set of assessment is the ability of the solution delivery team, internal or external, to deliver to the needs of the architecture specification. The project manager and the product owner are fully aware of the trade-off criteria to retain value; aware of dependencies from the neighbors to this effort and from this project to others; and the risks and controls to mitigate them.

### 原文内容单元

<details><summary>展开英文原文及文本块定位</summary>

9.1.5. Delivery and Acceptance Ability Assessment

**G186:p0107:c004 · PDF 107**

This is an opportune time to assess the readiness of the organization to actually start to execute and realize the change. It involves identifying whether the work packages cover the necessary changes to business processes, operating procedure, training, and everything that has to happen once the solution is delivered. The assessment is narrowly focused to test the scaffolding the neighbors should have in place. A second set of assessment is the ability of the solution delivery team, internal or external, to deliver to the needs of the architecture specification. The project manager and the product owner are fully aware of the trade-off criteria to retain value; aware of dependencies from the neighbors to this effort and from this project to others; and the risks and controls to mitigate them.

</details>

<a id="9.2"></a>
## 9.2 平衡选项与供应商

原文：9.2. Balance Options and Suppliers。PDF 物理页：[107]。状态：`source-compared-revised`。

支持项目的架构是在一个“盒子”里回答一组问题，答案应留在盒子之内。实践者必须把所有领域架构细化到刚好足以保证架构覆盖全部工作。在证明项目将成功之前，项目不能向前推进。收集交付项目所需全部资源的估算——要建整座桥，而不只是桥的一部分。记住，焦点是澄清并确认项目的目的与价值；桥的一部分既不服务于任何目的，也不增加任何价值。

有利之处在于：参与实施项目的实践者戴着眼罩，只能看到从脚下到地平线的距离。地平线就是实施开始前所需完成的工作。在实施项目的语境中，实践者的视线始终落在地平线上，包括到达那里的距离。站在地平线上时“成功”是什么样子，已经心知肚明；问题是：带我们到达那里需要做哪些工作？


### 语义规则与适用边界

- 项目范围应覆盖实现完整价值所需的全部工作与资源。
  - 适用边界：“证明成功”须结合 9.2.2 的充分审视与不可穷尽限制；不能承诺无风险必成功。
  - PDF 107：Architecture to Support Project is to answer a set of problems in a box; the answers are expected to stay within the box. The Practitioner must elaborate all domain architectures just enough to assure that the architecture is addressing all of the work. The project cannot move forward until it is proven that the project will be a success. Gather the estimates of all resources required to deliver the project. All of the bridge will be built, not just some of the bridge. Remember, the focus is to clarify and confirm the purpose and value of the project. Part of the bridge does not serve any purpose or add any value.

### 原文内容单元

<details><summary>展开英文原文及文本块定位</summary>

**G186:p0107:c005 · PDF 107**

9.2. Balance Options and Suppliers

**G186:p0107:c006 · PDF 107**

Architecture to Support Project is to answer a set of problems in a box; the answers are expected to stay within the box. The Practitioner must elaborate all domain architectures just enough to assure that the architecture is addressing all of the work. The project cannot move forward until it is proven that the project will be a success. Gather the estimates of all resources required to deliver the project. All of the bridge will be built, not just some of the bridge. Remember, the focus is to clarify and confirm the purpose and value of the project. Part of the bridge does not serve any purpose or add any value.

**G186:p0107:c007 · PDF 107**

The up-side is the Practitioners involved in the Implementation Project have blinders on that only allows them to see the distance from where they are standing to the horizon. The horizon is the work needed before implementation begins. In the context of the Implementation Project, the Practitioner’s line of sight is always the horizon, including the distance to get there. It is already understood what “success” will look like, standing on the horizon. What is the work that will take us there?

</details>

<a id="9.2.1"></a>
## 9.2.1 执行权衡

原文：9.2.1. Performing Trade-Off。PDF 物理页：[107, 108]。状态：`source-compared-revised`。

俗话说“人不能两次踏入同一条河流”，水总在变化、总在流动。如果在最终确定之前不先探明候选目标架构在 EA 全景中的位置，就很难引导项目避开瀑布与悬崖。

只有实践者“向下游”观察，才有条件执行权衡、解决跨目标架构的影响，并选择最平稳的航道。对 EA 全景做持续侦察，能让实践者看清项目在河流下游何处可以避开灾难。

为此，实践者要追查阻碍价值交付与实现的障碍。此时定义解决方案交付架构为时过早，这里绝对不是定义和设计解决方案的地方。实施不是架构；架构是保证对风险的韧性，并为实施者提供指导。任何为加速价值实现、提升符合性而推荐的 ABB 与 SBB，都应被识别并纳入架构规格。

如果发现实施项目的候选目标架构正在影响、或将会受到另一个在建项目已最终确定的目标架构影响，始终要评估时效性、加以确认，并做权衡分析。请记住，在做权衡分析、解决跨目标架构影响时，实施项目已受到严重约束，可能需要绕过那些已获批准、已在河岸扎根的其他项目，在河中开辟一条路径。随后，根据对实施项目的任何新发现，为实施项目创建架构规格，以确保避免重叠与冲突。


### 语义规则与适用边界

- 项目架构阶段提供韧性与实施指导，可推荐 ABB/SBB；不是直接设计实现。
  - 适用边界：与其他已批准项目冲突须复核、权衡并形成规格，不自行覆盖。
  - PDF 108：In order to perform, the Practitioner is chasing the barriers to deliver and realize value. This is too early to define the architecture for solution delivery. This is definitely not the place to define and design the solution. Implementation is not architecture. The architecture is assuring resilience to risks and guidance to implementers. Any recommendations of ABBs and SBBs to accelerate value realization and improve conformance are identified and included in an architecture specifications.

### 原文内容单元

<details><summary>展开英文原文及文本块定位</summary>

**G186:p0107:c008 · PDF 107**

9.2.1. Performing Trade-Off

**G186:p0107:c009 · PDF 107**

As the saying goes “you can’t step in the same river twice”; the water’s always changing, always flowing. Without discovery of where the candidate Target Architecture stands before finalization within the EA Landscape, it is harder to guide projects from running off waterfalls and large cliffs.

**G186:p0108:c000 · PDF 108**

Only until the Practitioner looks “downstream” are they in a position to perform a trade-off, resolve impacts across the Target Architecture, and choose the smoothest course. Doing a consistent reconnaissance of the EA Landscape will enlighten the Practitioner to where the project can avoid disaster further down the river.

**G186:p0108:c001 · PDF 108**

In order to perform, the Practitioner is chasing the barriers to deliver and realize value. This is too early to define the architecture for solution delivery. This is definitely not the place to define and design the solution. Implementation is not architecture. The architecture is assuring resilience to risks and guidance to implementers. Any recommendations of ABBs and SBBs to accelerate value realization and improve conformance are identified and included in an architecture specifications.

**G186:p0108:c002 · PDF 108**

If it is discovered that the Implementation Project’s candidate Target Architecture is impacting or will be impacted by a finalized Target Architecture of another project in-flight, always assess recency, confirm, and do a trade-off analysis. Keep in mind that when doing a trade-off analysis and resolving impacts across the Target Architecture that the Implementation Project is already heavily constrained and may need to mold a path down the river around the other projects that have been approved and have taken root along the river bank. Then, given any new discoveries to the Implementation Project, if any, create the architecture specifications for the Implementation Project to assure avoidance of overlap and conflict.

</details>

<a id="9.2.2"></a>
## 9.2.2 管理当前实施变革的方法

原文：9.2.2. Managing the Current Approach towards Implementing the Change。PDF 物理页：[108]。状态：`source-compared-revised`。

影响解决之后，创建必要的视图，向利益相关者传达：他们的关注点已通过解决方案交付启动之前制定的必要约束与指导得到处理，从而使交付能够成功。

实践者对目标架构的分析不可能评估所有情形或所有可能的变更选项。关于架构项目，总有无限多的事情可以发现。实践者的工作是表明：经过充分程度的审视，产生了架构项目的交付物，使解决方案交付架构能够成功。实践者只应评估到避开重大悬崖的程度；一旦开始为架构项目评估所有细微的颠簸，就已经超出了充分审视的程度，在浪费宝贵资源。

向利益相关者证明：当架构项目被解决方案交付架构消费时，他们的需求已得到满足，企业的变更将得到有效指导与约束。识别并争取批准必要资源，以便开始为解决方案交付架构分配预算。

当收到解决方案交付的架构工作请求时，实践者就知道架构项目成功了。


### 语义规则与适用边界

- 分析达到足以避免重大失败的审视程度即可，不穷举所有实现细节。
  - 适用边界：仍需向利益相关者说明所需约束与指导，并取得必要资源批准。
  - PDF 108：The Practitioner’s analysis of the Target Architecture cannot have assessed every circumstance, or change option possible. There will always be an infinite number of things to discover about the Architecture Project. The Practitioner’s job is to show that a sufficient level of scrutiny led to the deliverables of the Architecture Project for the solution delivery architecture to succeed. The Practitioner should only assess to the extent of avoiding major cliffs. Once you start assessing the Architecture Project for all the subtle bumps, you have exceeded the sufficient level of scrutiny and are wasting valuable resources.

The Practitioner’s analysis of the Target Architecture cannot have assessed every circumstance, or change option possible. The Practitioner’s job is to show that a sufficient level of scrutiny led to the deliverables of the Architecture Project for the solution delivery architecture to succeed. The Practitioner should only assess to the extent of avoiding major cliffs. Once you start assessing the Architecture Project for all the subtle bumps, you have exceeded the sufficient level of scrutiny and are wasting valuable resources.

<a id="9.3"></a>
## 9.3 最终确定范围与预算

原文：9.3. Finalize Scope and Budget。PDF 物理页：[109]。状态：`source-compared-revised`。

实施规划（阶段 F）是为实施项目走查 ADM 时最关键的一环。实践者必须为其架构项目合理说明需要哪些资源。

把项目的架构规格打包，其中包含缓解已识别实施项目风险的后续控制。该包随后移交给实施实践者。为实施实践者配备成功实施项目所需的一切，是架构项目实践者的责任。

如果有一个或多个工作包尚未分配给实施项目，就进行分配并寻求批准。要熟悉这些工作包填补哪些差距，以及它们在路线图中排序的目的；也可能需要熟悉项目不会填补的工作包。识别工作包内以及实施项目内的风险。

为实施项目的目的而“架构”这个包。创建架构规格，其程度应足以让架构项目不会脱轨；另一方面，铁路也不能被轻易磨平或约束到僵化的地步，以致无法适应 EA 全景的易变环境。让实施项目留在轨道上，同时维护架构项目的铁路。

实践者应在组合架构愿景的光照下、在利益相关者地图评审中、在 EA 全景的承诺中，把原则、需求与控制一并打包进架构规格。

在企业可接受的偏差范围内，细化项目的估算与时间线；把项目范围、权衡与时间线的更新级联到企业路线图。咨询必要的主题专家与利益相关者，完成架构评审。为解决方案交付工作填充治理与批准计划。


### 语义规则与适用边界

- 工作包分配给实施项目须寻求批准，并知晓负责与不负责的差距。
  - 适用边界：变更范围、时间和权衡须级联到企业路线图。
  - PDF 109：If one or more work packages have not already been assigned to the Implementation Project, do so and seek approval. Be familiar with which gaps the work package(s) are filling and the purpose of their sequence in the roadmap. It may also be necessary to be familiar with the work packages the project will not be filling. Identify the risks within the work packages and subsequently within the Implementation Project.
- 交付规格包包含原则、需求、控制，且置于组合愿景、利益相关者与全景语境中。
  - 适用边界：规格包不是没有理由的实施标准清单。
  - PDF 109：The Practitioner should package the architecture specifications including the principles, requirements, and controls within the context of the light shining down from the Architecture Vision of the portfolio, in the review of the Stakeholder Map, and the undertakings of the EA Landscape.

**G186:p0109:c000 · PDF 109**

9.3. Finalize Scope and Budget

**G186:p0109:c001 · PDF 109**

Implementation planning (Phase F) is the most critical piece in executing a walk through the ADM for the Implementation Project. Practitioners must rationalize for their Architecture Project what resources are required.

**G186:p0109:c002 · PDF 109**

Package the project’s architecture specifications, which includes the subsequent controls that mitigate the identified Implementation Project’s risks. The package is then handed off to the Implementation Practitioner. It is the responsibility of the Architecture Project Practitioner to set up the Implementation Practitioner with everything they need to implement the project successfully.

**G186:p0109:c003 · PDF 109**

If one or more work packages have not already been assigned to the Implementation Project, do so and seek approval. Be familiar with which gaps the work package(s) are filling and the purpose of their sequence in the roadmap. It may also be necessary to be familiar with the work packages the project will not be filling. Identify the risks within the work packages and subsequently within the Implementation Project.

**G186:p0109:c004 · PDF 109**

Architect the “package” for the purpose of the Implementation Project. Create architecture specifications to the extent that an Architecture Project will not go off the rails on a crazy train. On the other hand, the railroads must not be easily scoured or constrained to the point of inflexibility of the volatile environment of the EA Landscape. Keep the Implementation Project on the tracks while maintaining the railways of the Architecture Project.

**G186:p0109:c005 · PDF 109**

The Practitioner should package the architecture specifications including the principles, requirements, and controls within the context of the light shining down from the Architecture Vision of the portfolio, in the review of the Stakeholder Map, and the undertakings of the EA Landscape.

**G186:p0109:c006 · PDF 109**

Refine the estimates and timeline for the project within the acceptable variance limits of the Enterprise. Cascade the update to project scope, trade-offs, and timelines to the Enterprise roadmap. Consult the requisite SMEs and stakeholders, and complete the architecture review. Populate the governance and approval plan for the solution delivery effort.

</details>

<a id="9.4"></a>
## 9.4 准备解决方案交付治理

原文：9.4. Prepare for Solution Delivery Governance。PDF 物理页：[109, 110]。状态：`source-compared-revised`。

在这一步，架构实践者向企业交付最大价值。范围与预算最终确定之后，要确保项目的待办信息完整；面向产品负责人、产品经理、Scrum Master 或项目经理（无论角色与头衔如何）以及实施实践者的权衡与决策标准已充分定义并被理解；决策者与组织领导者充分了解他们必须努力消除的障碍。

此时任何尚未完成的概念验证工作，都应限于理解解决方案的方法，而不是架构。提供充分的度量标准、偏差预警指标、升级机制、主题专家部署以及实施治理。

启动关闭架构项目的步骤。架构项目的范围限于变更管理与治理；从这个意义上说，项目尚未完成。这也是架构团队与多数实践者退居幕后、把接力棒交给实施实践者的时刻。为实施实践者提供所需支持，以便在预算分配期间为项目辩护。在预算分配完成、实施项目章程签署之前，工作不算完成。


### 语义规则与适用边界

- 工作完成以预算已分配、实施项目章程已签署为条件，治理支持可继续。
  - 适用边界：架构人员退到支持位置不意味着所有后续责任已关闭。
  - PDF 110：Initiate steps to close the Architecture Project. The Architecture Project’s scope is limited to change management and governance. From that aspect, the project is not completed. This is also the time the architecture team and most of the Practitioners withdraw themselves from the limelight and pass the baton to Implementation Practitioners. Provide any required support for the Implementation Practitioners to defend the project during budget allocation. The work is not complete until the budget is allocated and the Implementation Project charter is signed.

**G186:p0109:c007 · PDF 109**

9.4. Prepare for Solution Delivery Governance

**G186:p0109:c008 · PDF 109**

The maximum value is to be delivered by the Architecture Practitioner to the Enterprise in this step. Having finalized the scope and budget, make sure that the backlog information is complete for the project; trade-off, and decision criteria for the product owner, product manager, scrum master, or the project manager (whatever the role and title is) and the Implementation Practitioner is fully defined and understood; decision-makers and organizational leaders are fully aware of the barriers they must work to remove.

**G186:p0109:c009 · PDF 109**

Any outstanding proof-of-concept work at this time should be limited to understanding an approach to the solution, not the architecture. Provide sufficient measurement criteria, indicators to warn of any variances, escalation, and deployment of SMEs, and implementation governance.

Initiate steps to close the Architecture Project. The Architecture Project’s scope is limited to change management and governance. From that aspect, the project is not completed. This is also the time the architecture team and most of the Practitioners withdraw themselves from the limelight and pass the baton to Implementation Practitioners. Provide any required support for the Implementation Practitioners to defend the project during budget allocation. The work is not complete until the budget is allocated and the Implementation Project charter is signed.

## 9.5 源自“野外”的项目架构工作请求

原文：9.5. Project Request for Architecture Work Originating from the Wild。PDF 物理页：[110]。状态：`source-compared-revised`。

最常见的来自野外的架构工作请求，是针对支持项目的架构。实践者的核心问题是识别所提议项目与预期价值的一致性，以及组织的机会成本。讨论见 8.6 节。

尾注

[1] 该架构项目是位于 EA 全景的莫哈韦沙漠中，还是位于阿布扎比？


### 语义规则与适用边界

- 外来项目请求要评估与预期价值的一致性及组织机会成本。
  - 适用边界：倡导力度或已有方案完整度不能替代该分析。
  - PDF 110：The most common Requests for Architecture Work from the wild are for Architecture to Support Project. The central question for the Practitioner is to identify the proposed project’s alignment to expected value and the opportunity cost for the organization. See Section 8.6 for a discussion.

<a id="10.1"></a>
## 10.1 引言

原文：10.1. Introduction。PDF 物理页：[111, 112, 113]。状态：`source-compared-revised`。

该架构及其成果能否成功，取决于架构实践者与实施实践者之间的协调程度。架构实践者把一个受到良好约束、但仍留有充分创造与创新空间的“盒子”交给实施实践者。实施实践者的职责是不破坏这个盒子，也不改变其形状或外观；架构实践者的职责是在 EA 全景中界定这个盒子的语境，定义所有推拉力量。此时，候选架构项目就是目标架构。

请注意，表 10-1 对阶段 G 的讨论很少；所有这些活动都发生在阶段 G 的语境中。该表说明其他阶段的活动如何促成解决方案交付，并推动架构项目收尾。实际收尾由阶段 H 触发——要么识别新的工作，要么宣告目标状态已达成。

表 10-1 汇总了相关活动以及 ADM 阶段中适当步骤的使用；本章其余部分将详细讨论表中内容。

表 10-1：汇总表——ADM 阶段与支持解决方案交付的架构

| 主题 | 对应的 TOGAF ADM 阶段 |
|---|---|
| 对齐实施者 | 部分能力层阶段 A。<br>项目语境：验证时效性；重申利益相关者、成果、时间线；沟通价值主张。<br>部分能力层阶段 B、C、D。<br>项目群语境：细化架构规格；重申风险控制；沟通 SBB。<br>部分项目层阶段 G。<br>项目群语境：启动项目治理。 |
| 指导交付 | 部分项目层阶段 B、C、D。<br>项目语境：持续更新 EA 全景；细化 SBB 与解决方案边界；监控控制。<br>EA 能力特定语境：更新 EA 存储库（内容与模型）；更新标准与参考架构；分配资源。<br>部分能力层阶段 E。<br>项目语境：分析与上层架构权衡的影响；更新风险矩阵。<br>部分能力层阶段 G。<br>项目语境：开展利益相关者评审；获得架构批准；验证解决方案与愿景的一致性。 |
| 实现解决方案 | 部分项目层阶段 H。<br>项目群语境：评估解决方案的差距；评估风险关闭；更新企业路线图。<br>部分项目层阶段 F。<br>项目语境：基线化过渡状态架构；完成经验教训；关闭架构工作。<br>部分企业层阶段 H。<br>项目群语境：评估对企业路线图的变更；按需为架构工作创建待办。<br>EA 能力特定语境：参与利益相关者；更新 EA 路线图。 |

给实施实践者的简单指导是：盯住上层架构的目标。要绝对清楚架构试图优化什么、被要求交付什么。人们很容易想消除当前交付周期中的所有次优化选项，请克制这种冲动。要验证次优化是有意为之，未来的工作会处理这些关注点。一个糟糕的司机就足以扰乱数英里的交通。要明白，解决方案架构只是企业中众多同时运动的部件之一。

开发和交付该架构时最主要的关注点，见以下各节。

相关结构化资料：[source-tables](../tables/G186.json)。本节表格全文已对照；独立结构化表保留单元格与位置。

### 语义规则与适用边界

- 交付活动整体在阶段 G 的实施治理语境中，其他阶段活动支持交付，实际关闭由 H 触发。
  - 适用边界：表中 G 出现少不代表不做治理；候选转目标仍须批准。
  - PDF 111：Note that there will be minimal discussion on Phase G in Table 10-1. All of these activities occur in the context of Phase G. The table informs how activities in other phases enable delivery of the solution and drive closure to an Architecture Project. Actual closure is triggered from Phase H, either identifying a new effort or signaling achievement of target state.
- 应核实次优选择是否有意、是否由未来工作处理。
  - 适用边界：不得为当前局部最优破坏上层路线图或并行项目。
  - PDF 113：Simple guidance for the Implementation Practitioner is to keep an eye on the target of the superior architecture. Be absolutely clear what the architecture is trying to optimize and what it is being asked to deliver. It may be tempting to remove all sub-optimization choices in the current delivery cycle. Refrain. Validate that sub-optimization is intentional and future work will address such concerns. All it takes is one bad driver to upset miles of traffic. Understand that the Solution Architecture is one of the many concurrently moving parts in the Enterprise.

### 原文内容单元

<details><summary>展开英文原文及文本块定位</summary>

**G186:p0111:c002 · PDF 111**

10.1. Introduction

**G186:p0111:c003 · PDF 111**

The success of this architecture and its outcome are driven by the degree of coordination between Architecture Practitioner and the Implementation Practitioner. The Architecture Practitioner hands over a well constrained, yet with sufficient room for creativity and innovation, box to the Implementation Practitioner. It is the duty of the Implementation Practitioner to not break the box or to morph its shape or appearance. It is the duty and responsibility of the Architecture Practitioner to define the context of this box within the EA Landscape, defining all of the push and pull forces. The candidate Architecture Project is now the Target Architecture.

**G186:p0111:c004 · PDF 111**

Note that there will be minimal discussion on Phase G in Table 10-1. All of these activities occur in the context of Phase G. The table informs how activities in other phases enable delivery of the solution and drive closure to an Architecture Project. Actual closure is triggered from Phase H, either identifying a new effort or signaling achievement of target state.

**G186:p0111:c005 · PDF 111**

Table 10-1 summarizes the activities and use of appropriate steps from the ADM phases. The content of the table is discussed in detail in the rest of this chapter.

| --- | --- |
| Guide Delivery | Partial Project Level Phases B, C, and D<br>Project context:<br>• Continuously update EA Landscape<br>• Refine SBBs and solution boundaries<br>• Monitor controls<br>EA Capability specific context:<br>• Update EA Repository (contents and models)<br>• Update standards and reference architectures<br>• Distribute resources<br>Partial Capability Level Phase E<br>Project context:<br>• Analyze impact of trade-off with superior architecture<br>• Update risk matrix<br>Partial Capability Level Phase G<br>Project context:<br>• Conduct stakeholder review<br>• Obtain architecture approval<br>• Validate alignment of solution to vision |

| Topic | Mapping to TOGAF ADM Phase |
| --- | --- |
| Realizing the Solution | Partial Project Level Phase H<br>Program context:<br>• Assess solution for gaps<br>• Assess risk closure<br>• Update Enterprise roadmap<br>Partial Project Level Phase F<br>Project context:<br>• Baseline transition state architecture<br>• Complete lessons learned<br>• Close architecture work<br>Partial Enterprise Level Phase H<br>Program context:<br>• Assess changes to Enterprise roadmap<br>• As required, create backlog for architecture work<br>EA Capability specific context:<br>• Engage stakeholders<br>• Update EA roadmap |

**G186:p0113:c001 · PDF 113**

Simple guidance for the Implementation Practitioner is to keep an eye on the target of the superior architecture. Be absolutely clear what the architecture is trying to optimize and what it is being asked to deliver. It may be tempting to remove all sub-optimization choices in the current delivery cycle. Refrain. Validate that sub-optimization is intentional and future work will address such concerns. All it takes is one bad driver to upset miles of traffic. Understand that the Solution Architecture is one of the many concurrently moving parts in the Enterprise.

**G186:p0113:c002 · PDF 113**

Top concerns to be addressed in developing and delivering this architecture are covered in the following sections.

</details>
