<a id="10.2"></a>
## 10.2 对齐实施者

原文：10.2. Aligning Implementers。PDF 物理页：[114, 115]。状态：`source-compared-revised`。

架构实践者与实施实践者必须验证：架构的自下而上视图在“时效性”度量上良好对齐。下一步是验证横向架构集合的时效性度量。架构项目界定边界条件，以限制对整体架构的影响，并考虑实施架构师会做出的所有权衡选择。这并不意味着每个解决方案与其他解决方案的交互方式不能改变；这种影响并不需要重印所有培训手册、重排解决方案用户的培训计划。

多数情况下会有不止一个参与者：解决方案提供者与解决方案消费者。业务的动态性质可能要求在推进中途变更所提议的解决方案。架构项目以及解决方案架构要清楚定义可能触发变更、利益相关者评审与架构批准的条件。相当一部分项目会涉及不止一个解决方案实施者。开发架构，以识别、澄清、约束每个解决方案实施者，并使其不受其他实施者的束缚。解决方案架构阐明整体解决方案的集成与验收条件。

内部或第三方解决方案实施者依据该架构交付。由第三方提供时，验证、集成与验收解决方案的责任仍在内部团队。归根结底，消费者与最终用户并不关心是谁提供了解决方案；他们的问题是：“这是否满足我的期望，是否说到做到，是否如声明和定义的那样可用？”确保架构、治理计划与实施者在价值主张、权衡条件与利益相关者矩阵上完全对齐。

如果解决方案交付项目是在验证某个概念，其主要成果是挖掘所有失败点，次要成果是该想法的可行性，第三成果是该想法满足使用需求的可扩展性。如果解决方案交付项目是在建一座桥，其主要目标是使交通在多数环境条件下得以通行，次要目标是设定使用条款。解决方案交付项目之间的差异如此之大，本文档无法提供足够的示例来强调与邻居对齐以及完成自下而上视图的重要性。

阶段 A 中完成的工作最少，它只关乎确认范围、利益相关者、时效性与价值主张。


### 语义规则与适用边界

- 第三方交付后，内部团队仍负责验证、集成和验收。
  - 适用边界：外包供给不转移企业整体接受责任；具体供应商质量责任另由合同规定。
  - PDF 114：In-house or third-party solution implementers deliver against this architecture. When supplied by a third party, the onus is still on the in-house team to validate, integrate, and accept the solutions. At the end of the day, the consumers and end-users do not care who supplied the solution. Their question is: “Does this meet my expectations, does what it says, available as stated and defined?” Make sure that architecture, the governance plan and implementer are totally aligned on value proposition, conditions for trade-off, and the stakeholder matrix.
- 架构须明确变更触发、评审、批准及总体集成验收条件。
  - 适用边界：多供应商独立空间与共同边界同时存在。
  - PDF 114：In most cases, there would be more than one player; a solution provider and a solution consumer. The dynamic nature of business could ask for changes to the solution proposed mid-stream. The Architecture Project and hence the Solution Architecture clearly define the conditions that could trigger a change, stakeholder review, and architecture approval. A sizeable fraction of the projects will involve more than one solution implementer. Develop the architecture to identify, clarify, constrain, and liberate each of the solution implementers from the other. The Solution Architecture articulates conditions for integration and acceptance of the total solution.

### 原文内容单元

<details><summary>展开英文原文及文本块定位</summary>

**G186:p0114:c010 · PDF 114**

10.2. Aligning Implementers

**G186:p0114:c011 · PDF 114**

It is imperative that the Architecture Practitioner and Implementation Practitioner verify that the bottom-up view of the architecture aligns well on the “recency” measure. The next step is to validate the recency measure of the lateral set of architectures. The Architecture Project defines the boundary conditions to limit the impact to the overall architecture, accounting for all trade-off choices that would be made by the implementation architect. This doesn’t mean that there cannot be changes to how each solution interacts with another. The impact does not require reprinting all of the training manuals and redoing the training schedule for the users of the solution.

**G186:p0114:c012 · PDF 114**

In most cases, there would be more than one player; a solution provider and a solution consumer. The dynamic nature of business could ask for changes to the solution proposed mid-stream. The Architecture Project and hence the Solution Architecture clearly define the conditions that could trigger a change, stakeholder review, and architecture approval. A sizeable fraction of the projects will involve more than one solution implementer. Develop the architecture to identify, clarify, constrain, and liberate each of the solution implementers from the other. The Solution Architecture articulates conditions for integration and acceptance of the total solution.

**G186:p0114:c013 · PDF 114**

In-house or third-party solution implementers deliver against this architecture. When supplied by a third party, the onus is still on the in-house team to validate, integrate, and accept the solutions. At the end of the day, the consumers and end-users do not care who supplied the solution. Their question is: “Does this meet my expectations, does what it says, available as stated and defined?” Make sure that architecture, the governance plan and implementer are totally aligned on value proposition, conditions for trade-off, and the stakeholder matrix.

**G186:p0114:c014 · PDF 114**

If the solution delivery project is validating a concept, the primary outcome is unearthing all points of failure; the secondary outcome is feasibility of the idea; and the tertiary outcome is scalability of the

**G186:p0115:c000 · PDF 115**

idea to meet usage demands. If the solution delivery project is building a bridge, its primary objective is enabling transportation under most environmental conditions; its secondary objective is to set terms of use. The variances across the solution delivery project are so vast that this document cannot provide a sufficient set of examples to emphasize alignment with neighbors and completing the bottom-up view.

**G186:p0115:c001 · PDF 115**

There is the least amount of work done in Phase A. It is all about affirming scope, stakeholders, currency, and value proposition.

</details>

<a id="10.3"></a>
## 10.3 指导交付

原文：10.3. Guiding Delivery。PDF 物理页：[115, 116]。状态：`source-compared-revised`。

解决方案供应商交付的任何 SBB，都必须与企业生态系统的其余部分集成。在解决方案交付并对照未来工作（过渡架构 n+1）评估之前，无法确定当前某些工作是否会成为 SBB。不要为了创建构建块而工作；等解决方案交付并投入使用后再评估和细化。

就撰写时可用的架构风格与模式而言，可以把每个微服务，或微服务的聚合（SOA 服务），视为一个 SBB。

当上层架构表明存在可用的 ABB 与 SBB 时，深入企业存储库，以复用并符合架构。当 ABB 指向企业外部的实现时，引导行业协作与语境特定的权衡，以指导企业特定 SBB 的开发与交付。

架构成功的关键，是保留企业内部对解决方案块集成的所有权。把责任委托给任何其他方，都会导致项目管理与治理问题，最终使架构失败。

支持解决方案交付的架构，是所有实现与监管合规需求得到满足的地方。因此，企业下一个关键的长期成功因素，是识别应保留在内部的核心信息与数据。上层架构应定义企业的“核心”；所有其他数据集不必由企业保留、做主数据管理或控制。这一选择会驱动运营模型中的其他决策点：解决方案应作为企业的黑盒（托管服务）处理，还是内部专门化，或雇用专家团队？上层架构不必解决这一选择；解决方案提供商的选择是在开发和交付解决方案时做出的。某些解决方案提供商的选择可能受企业限制供应商数量的偏好约束；实践者不应仅因存在约束就觉得必须使用某个解决方案提供商。优先考虑的是交付适配性与加速上市时间。

集成方式的选择、“核心”信息的定义，以及托管服务与自建的决策，决定描述架构所需的粒度。

持续填充 EA 全景：随着每项决策做出，架构的粒度得以确定，跨解决方案块的交互得以定义。量化和记录每个解决方案块所需资源，可能不是实施实践者或架构实践者的直接关注点；采购成本、交付成本、运营成本等属性是企业规划组织所需的。把这些属性捕获在 EA 工具中是合理的选择；每个解决方案交付项目的财务投资数据，有助于减少完成权衡分析、预算汇总与分解等工作的时间。

本文档并不建议由 EA 工具主数据管理解决方案交付项目的资源分配数据，也不建议由 EA 团队承担这一责任。本文档指出的是能让实践者高效且有目的工作的一组数据。资源分配的事实来源应由实践者依据企业设定的指导来确定。良好的内容模型与 EA 工具通常能够在最低粒度上捕获这一数据点，并支持汇总与权衡分析。本文档的立场是：使用 EA 工具完成那些为权衡分析提供信息并影响权衡分析的计算，而不是用其他方法来加快为权衡提供信息的速度。

影响该架构的另一组权衡与约束，是企业中解决方案族的存在。数据托管服务或 ERP 包的供应商或技术选择，会约束项目中、有时是整个企业中可以使用的其他构建块。从上层架构评估这些解决方案族；若上层架构没有，实施实践者与架构实践者应花时间识别、分析并上报这些选择对大型职能领域（如企业资源管理与规划）的影响。

尽管架构项目定义了边界与接口，变更仍然必然发生。需要架构实践者与实施实践者持续互动，以主动缓解障碍。

目标是开发架构到治理所交付解决方案所需的程度，不要觉得必须连解决方案也一并定义。定义并运用必要的视角来沟通、指导和治理解决方案架构。监控实施风险以及为应对企业风险所实施的控制。每项权衡与实施选择都会影响、并可能修改目标架构。治理这些选择会影响目标架构中的差距、路线图，进而影响下一财年的支持组合的架构。

交付解决方案所做的工作主要跨越阶段 B、C、D、E。所考虑和采用的创新、研究与备选方案遵循阶段 E 的步骤，只是它们不经过严格的架构控制；备选方案受架构规格约束。因此，问题在于在约束内运作的能力，而不在于控制选择。按阶段 B、C、D 的步骤创建的规格，保证适当的选择。


### 语义规则与适用边界

- 是否可形成 SBB 要在交付、使用并对照下一过渡工作后评估。
  - 适用边界：不为制造构建块而制造；微服务作为 SBB 只是写作时可考虑的示例。
  - PDF 115：Any SBB delivered by solution suppliers will have to be integrated with the rest of the ecosystem of the Enterprise. Until the solution is delivered and evaluated against future work (transition architecture n+1), it will not be clear that some of the current work could become an SBB. Do not work to create a building block. Assess and refine once the solution is delivered and put to work.
- EA 工具可保留用于权衡的资源数据，但不应自动成为资源分配事实主库或令 EA 团队承担主责。
  - 适用边界：事实来源遵循企业指导确定。
  - PDF 116：It is not the recommendation of this document that resource allocation data for solution delivery projects be mastered in the EA tool or the EA team to take responsibility. This document is calling out a dataset that enables the Practitioner to be productive and purposeful. The source of truth for resource allocation should be determined by the Practitioner, following the guidance set by the Enterprise. A good content model and EA tool are normally capable of capturing this data point at the lowest level of granularity, and enable roll-up and trade-off analysis. It is the position of this document to use an EA tool to do the computations that inform and impact trade-off analysis, instead of using other methods to speed up the time to inform trade-off.
- 交付架构细化到足以治理为止，持续监控风险与控制并回写全景影响。
  - 适用边界：每个选择可能影响目标、路线图与下一年度组合，不能只记录代码实现。
  - PDF 116：The objective is to develop the architecture to the extent needed to govern the solution being delivered. Do not feel compelled to define the solution as well. Define and employ viewpoints necessary to communicate, guide, and govern the Solution Architecture. Monitor implementation risks and the controls being implemented for Enterprise risks. Every trade-off and implementation choice made impacts and potentially modifies the Target Architecture. Governing the selections impacts the gap in the Target Architecture, the roadmap, and therefore the Architecture to Support Portfolio of the following fiscal year.

### 原文内容单元

<details><summary>展开英文原文及文本块定位</summary>

**G186:p0115:c002 · PDF 115**

10.3. Guiding Delivery

**G186:p0115:c003 · PDF 115**

Any SBB delivered by solution suppliers will have to be integrated with the rest of the ecosystem of the Enterprise. Until the solution is delivered and evaluated against future work (transition architecture n+1), it will not be clear that some of the current work could become an SBB. Do not work to create a building block. Assess and refine once the solution is delivered and put to work.

**G186:p0115:c004 · PDF 115**

In terms of architecture styles and patterns available at the time of writing, you may consider each Microservice or an aggregation of Microservices (SOA service) as an SBB.

**G186:p0115:c005 · PDF 115**

When the superior architecture indicates availability of ABBs and SBBs, reach into the Enterprise Repository to reuse and conform to the architecture. When the ABBs point to implementations outside the Enterprise, guide industry collaboration and context-specific trade-off to guide development and delivery of Enterprise-specific SBBs.

**G186:p0115:c006 · PDF 115**

Critical to success of architectures is retaining the ownership of integrating solution blocks within the Enterprise. Delegating the responsibility to any other party will lead to project management and governance issues, resulting in failed architecture.

**G186:p0115:c007 · PDF 115**

Architecture to Support Solution Delivery is where all realizations and regulatory compliance needs are met. Naturally, the next critical long-term success factor for the Enterprise is identification of core information and data that should be retained in-house. The superior architecture should define the “core” for the Enterprise. All other datasets need not be retained, mastered, or controlled by the Enterprise. This choice drives other decision points in the operating model. Should the solution be treated as a black box for the Enterprise (a managed service) or specialized in-house or an expert team employed? Superior architectures need not resolve this choice. The choice and selection of solution provider is made at the time of developing and delivering the solution. Some of the solution provider choices may be constrained by the Enterprise’s preference to restrict the number of suppliers. The Practitioner should not feel compelled to use a solution provider just because a constraint exists. Priority is fitness to deliver and accelerate time-to-market.

**G186:p0115:c008 · PDF 115**

Choice of integration, definition of “core” information, and managed service versus in-house decisions guide the level of granularity needed to describe the architecture.

**G186:p0115:c009 · PDF 115**

Populate the EA Landscape continuously; as each decision is made, the level of granularity of the architecture is arrived at, and interactions across solution blocks are defined. Quantifying and documenting the resource required by each solution block may not be the direct concern of the

**G186:p0116:c000 · PDF 116**

Implementation Practitioner or the Architecture Practitioner. Attributes like cost to procure, cost to deliver, and cost to operate are required by the Enterprise planning organization. It is a sensible option to capture these attributes within the EA tool. Financial investment data for each solution delivery project aids and reduces time to complete the trade-off analysis, roll-up and roll-down of budget, among other benefits.

**G186:p0116:c001 · PDF 116**

It is not the recommendation of this document that resource allocation data for solution delivery projects be mastered in the EA tool or the EA team to take responsibility. This document is calling out a dataset that enables the Practitioner to be productive and purposeful. The source of truth for resource allocation should be determined by the Practitioner, following the guidance set by the Enterprise. A good content model and EA tool are normally capable of capturing this data point at the lowest level of granularity, and enable roll-up and trade-off analysis. It is the position of this document to use an EA tool to do the computations that inform and impact trade-off analysis, instead of using other methods to speed up the time to inform trade-off.

**G186:p0116:c002 · PDF 116**

Another set of trade-offs and constraints that impact this architecture is the existence of solution families in the Enterprise. The choice of a supplier or technology for data hosting services or ERP package constrains other building blocks that can be employed in the project and sometimes across the Enterprise. Take an assessment of such solution families from the superior architecture. When not available, the Implementation Practitioner and the Architecture Practitioner should spend time identifying, analyzing, and escalating impact of choices on large functional areas like Enterprise resource management and planning.

**G186:p0116:c003 · PDF 116**

Even though the Architecture Project defines the boundary and the interface, change is bound to happen. Continuous interaction with the Architecture Practitioner and Implementation Practitioner is required to proactively mitigate barriers.

**G186:p0116:c004 · PDF 116**

The objective is to develop the architecture to the extent needed to govern the solution being delivered. Do not feel compelled to define the solution as well. Define and employ viewpoints necessary to communicate, guide, and govern the Solution Architecture. Monitor implementation risks and the controls being implemented for Enterprise risks. Every trade-off and implementation choice made impacts and potentially modifies the Target Architecture. Governing the selections impacts the gap in the Target Architecture, the roadmap, and therefore the Architecture to Support Portfolio of the following fiscal year.

**G186:p0116:c005 · PDF 116**

Work performed to deliver the solution mainly spans Phases B, C, D, and E. Innovations, research, and alternatives considered and employed follow the steps in Phase E. It is just that they do not go through rigorous architecture control. The alternatives are constrained by the architecture specification. Hence, it is a question of the ability to operate within constraints and not about controlling the selection. Specification created by following the steps in Phases B, C, and D assures appropriate selection.

</details>

<a id="10.4"></a>
## 10.4 实现解决方案

原文：10.4. Realizing the Solution。PDF 物理页：[117]。状态：`source-compared-revised`。

从合同角度看，这是上线后的保修期。取决于企业采用的解决方案交付方法，它可能与“指导交付”并行。这是把解决方案交到受益者（客户、最终用户、支持人员、合作伙伴等）手中的时期。架构实践者的参与只有在解决方案投入使用后才结束或换挡。取决于企业的接受意愿，成功使用可定义为最初的 30、60 或 90 天。

在这一时期结束时，架构实践者启动已实现架构与用于解决方案交付的基线架构之间的差距分析。只有在这项分析结束时，才能决定是否释放关键资源——项目经理、实施架构师、供应商代表、为开发解决方案保留的技术资源等。实施实践者接受上层架构后，架构项目即告关闭；但架构实践者提供的监督会保留到解决方案交付完成标准得到满足。

利用架构项目提供的基础，不时报告已实现的价值。记录经验教训，主要是交付解决方案架构期间所填补的上层架构描述中的差距；记录加速解决方案整体交付的控制与约束。更新项目对 EA 全景与路线图的级联影响。按需验证、关闭并更新企业待办。


### 语义规则与适用边界

- 架构参与在解决方案实际使用后才结束或转变，成功使用期由企业定义。
  - 适用边界：30/60/90 天是可选示例而非固定验收时长。
  - PDF 117：Contractually, this is the post-rollout, warranty period. Depending on the solution delivery method used in the Enterprise, this may be a parallel path to Guiding Delivery. It is the period of putting the solution in the hands of the beneficiaries (customers, end-users, support personnel, partners, etc.). The engagement of the Architecture Practitioner comes to a conclusion or shifts gear only when the solution is put to use. Depending on the appetite of the Enterprise, successful usage may be defined as the first 30, 60, or 90 days.
- 释放关键资源须基于实现与基线差距分析；架构项目关闭与监督停止分开。
  - 适用边界：原文关闭条件之间存在张力，应与 10.6 的评估签署结合，不能无条件自动关单。
  - PDF 117：At the end of this period, the Architecture Practitioner initiates a gap analysis between the realized architecture and the Baseline Architecture to be used for solution delivery. It is only at the end of this analysis that a determination can be made about releasing key resources – the project manager, the implementation architect, supplier representative, technology resources reserved for developing the solution, etc. Closure of the Architecture Project is achieved as soon as the Implementation Practitioner accepts the superior architecture. However, the oversight provided by the Architecture Practitioner is retained until the solution delivery completion criteria are met.

**G186:p0117:c000 · PDF 117**

10.4. Realizing the Solution

**G186:p0117:c001 · PDF 117**

Contractually, this is the post-rollout, warranty period. Depending on the solution delivery method used in the Enterprise, this may be a parallel path to Guiding Delivery. It is the period of putting the solution in the hands of the beneficiaries (customers, end-users, support personnel, partners, etc.). The engagement of the Architecture Practitioner comes to a conclusion or shifts gear only when the solution is put to use. Depending on the appetite of the Enterprise, successful usage may be defined as the first 30, 60, or 90 days.

**G186:p0117:c002 · PDF 117**

At the end of this period, the Architecture Practitioner initiates a gap analysis between the realized architecture and the Baseline Architecture to be used for solution delivery. It is only at the end of this analysis that a determination can be made about releasing key resources – the project manager, the implementation architect, supplier representative, technology resources reserved for developing the solution, etc. Closure of the Architecture Project is achieved as soon as the Implementation Practitioner accepts the superior architecture. However, the oversight provided by the Architecture Practitioner is retained until the solution delivery completion criteria are met.

**G186:p0117:c003 · PDF 117**

Use the basis provided by the Architecture Project to report the value realized from time to time. Document the lessons learned, mainly the gaps in the description of the superior architecture that were filled while delivering the Solution Architecture. Document controls and constraints that accelerated overall delivery of the solution. Update the cascading impact of the project to the EA Landscape and roadmap. As needed, validate, close, and update the Enterprise backlog.

<a id="10.5"></a>
## 10.5 源自“野外”的项目架构工作请求

原文：10.5. Project Request for Architecture Work Originating from the Wild。PDF 物理页：[117]。状态：`source-compared-revised`。

针对支持解决方案交付的架构，来自野外的架构工作请求通常不会发生。相反，会出现一个已完全成型、附带拟议解决方案的实施项目。此时实践者必须对照上层架构评估这个完全成型的解决方案；这更像适配性分析，并带有自己的政治含义。更广泛的讨论与评估报告示例见 8.6 节与表 15-3。


### 语义规则与适用边界

- 完全成型的外来实施方案需对照上层架构做适配分析。
  - 适用边界：政治压力及已完成设计不等同合规证据。
  - PDF 117：Requests for Architecture Work from the wild for Architecture to Support Solution Delivery are typically not done. Instead, there is a fully-baked Implementation Project with a proposed solution. In this case the Practitioner has to assess the fully-baked solution against the superior architecture. This becomes more of fitment analysis with its own political implication. See Section 8.6 and Table 15-3 for a broader discussion and assessment reporting example.

10.5. Project Request for Architecture Work Originating from

**G186:p0117:c005 · PDF 117**

the Wild

**G186:p0117:c006 · PDF 117**

Requests for Architecture Work from the wild for Architecture to Support Solution Delivery are typically not done. Instead, there is a fully-baked Implementation Project with a proposed solution. In this case the Practitioner has to assess the fully-baked solution against the superior architecture. This becomes more of fitment analysis with its own political implication. <a id="10.6"></a>
## 10.6 结论

原文：10.6. Conclusion。PDF 物理页：[117, 118]。状态：`source-compared-revised`。

许多架构实践者在支持解决方案交付时失职。把他们的角色与主题专家、审计员、利益相关者，以及企业利益相关者与决策者的代理相混淆，是很常见的。请回顾第 11 章与 15.2 节。已实现的解决方案就是新的基线，是演进和分析通往目标架构路线图的基础。企业中以及行业中所有被挡在解决方案交付影响之外的发展，都会被加入评估集。这项评估是架构实践者接下来要开展的关键活动；正是这项工作，为关闭当前架构项目、实施项目与资源提供了理由，也为实现目标过渡状态（n+1）的下一组举措的架构工作请求提供了理由。让所有利益相关者、决策者与实施者参与完成评估，并获得关闭这项工作的签署确认。


### 语义规则与适用边界

- 已实现方案形成新基线，评估遗留变化和下一过渡，并由相关各方签署关闭。
  - 适用边界：同时记录下一阶段架构请求；不把角色混用视为省略审批的理由。
  - PDF 117：Many Architecture Practitioners fail in their role when supporting solution delivery. It is quite normal to confuse their role with SME, auditor, stakeholder, and proxy for the Enterprise stakeholder and decision-maker. Review Chapter 11 and Section 15.2. The realized solution is the new baseline. It is the basis for evolving and analyzing the roadmap to the Target Architecture. All the development that happened in the Enterprise, and the industry, that were kept away from impacting solution delivery is added to the assessment set. This assessment is the next critical activity the Architecture Practitioner performs. It is this work that justifies closure of the current Architecture Project, Implementation Project, and resources. It also justifies the Request for Architecture Work for the next set of initiatives to achieve the target transition state (n+1). Involve all stakeholders, decision-makers, and implementers to complete the assessment, and gain the sign-off to close the effort.

Many Architecture Practitioners fail in their role when supporting solution delivery. It is quite normal to confuse their role with SME, auditor, stakeholder, and proxy for the Enterprise stakeholder and decision-maker. Review Chapter 11 and Section 15.2. <a id="11"></a>
## 11. 跳到阶段 G

原文：11. Jumping to Phase G。PDF 物理页：[121]。状态：`source-compared-revised`。

许多实践者会经常面对组织“跳到 G”的情况。许多组织依据“能把事情办成”的能力来选择领导者，这造成了一种行动偏好。使能有效变革，需要在可预测的计划性变革与创新、创造力之间取得平衡。

跳到阶段 G 的组织，要么是出于组织对可见行动的偏好，要么是因为 EA 团队执行失败。两种情况下，优秀的实践者都会对组织文化或自身失败做出回应。讨论有效参与和企业流程超出了本指南的范围；参见《TOGAF® 系列指南：TOGAF 领导者指南——建立并演进 EA 能力》[G184]。

本章将讨论经典的失败模式：

- 错过目的
- 错过业务周期
- 不做架构

本章还将说明实践者如何应对由创新、创造力与情境带来的不可预测变革。

开发 EA 的原因非常简单：**引导有效变革**。引导有效变革意味着服务决策者与实施者。支持战略、组合与项目的架构聚焦于支持决策者，并直接与业务周期的规划阶段挂钩；支持解决方案交付的架构主要面向实施者。当实践者未能及时为战略、组合与项目提供支持时，组织就会用做决策当天手头的信息继续做决策。

如果没有良好的支持战略、组合与项目的目标架构，组织就已经跳到了阶段 G。通常有两个原因：错位，以及错过目的。

真正的错位不在本文档范围之内。关于 EA 对齐的建议，参见《TOGAF® 系列指南：TOGAF 领导者指南——建立并演进 EA 能力》[G184]。

业界多数所谓的错位案例，实际上是实践者错过了解决方案部署之外的其他目的。


### 语义规则与适用边界

- 未及时交付规划支持时，组织仍会按当时信息决策。
  - 适用边界：跳到 G 可来自行动偏好或 EA 失败，不能假设所有组织都必须等待架构流程。
  - PDF 121：An EA is developed for one very simple reason: to guide effective change. Guiding effective change involves serving decision-makers and implementers. Architecture to Support Strategy, Portfolio, and Project are focused on supporting decision-makers and are directly tied to planning stages in the business cycle. Architecture to Support Solution Delivery is primarily aimed at implementers. When the Practitioner does not provide timely support for strategy, portfolio, and project, the organization will continue to make decisions using the information at hand on the day the decision must be made.

### 原文内容单元

<details><summary>展开英文原文及文本块定位</summary>

**G186:p0121:c000 · PDF 121**

11. Jumping to Phase G

**G186:p0121:c001 · PDF 121**

Many Practitioners will be regularly faced with their organization “Jumping to G”. Many organizations select leadership on their ability to get things done. This creates a bias to action. Enabling effective change requires balancing predictable planned change with innovation and creativity.

**G186:p0121:c002 · PDF 121**

Organizations that jump to Phase G will jump either because of organizational preference for visible action or execution failure by the EA team. In both cases, good Practitioners will respond to their organizational culture or to their failures. It is outside the scope of this Guide for Practitioners to discuss effective engagement and Enterprise processes; see the TOGAF® Series Guide: The TOGAF Leader’s Guide to Establishing and Evolving an EA Capability [G184].

**G186:p0121:c003 · PDF 121**

The chapter will address classic failure patterns:

**G186:p0121:c004 · PDF 121**

• Missing the purpose

**G186:p0121:c005 · PDF 121**

• Missing the business cycle

**G186:p0121:c006 · PDF 121**

• Not doing architecture

**G186:p0121:c007 · PDF 121**

This chapter will also identify how the Practitioner addresses unpredictable change resulting from innovation, creativity, and circumstance.

**G186:p0121:c008 · PDF 121**

An EA is developed for one very simple reason: to guide effective change. Guiding effective change involves serving decision-makers and implementers. Architecture to Support Strategy, Portfolio, and Project are focused on supporting decision-makers and are directly tied to planning stages in the business cycle. Architecture to Support Solution Delivery is primarily aimed at implementers. When the Practitioner does not provide timely support for strategy, portfolio, and project, the organization will continue to make decisions using the information at hand on the day the decision must be made.

**G186:p0121:c009 · PDF 121**

Without a good Target Architecture to Support Strategy, Portfolio, and Project, the organization has jumped to Phase G. Typically this happens for two reasons: misalignment and missing the purpose.

**G186:p0121:c010 · PDF 121**

Actual misalignment is outside the scope of this document. For advice on the alignment of the EA, see the TOGAF® Series Guide: The TOGAF Leader’s Guide to Establishing and Evolving an EA Capability [G184].

**G186:p0121:c011 · PDF 121**

Most examples of misalignment in the industry are actually Practitioners missing purposes other than solution deployment.

</details>

<a id="11.1"></a>
## 11.1 失败模式：错过目的

原文：11.1. Failure Pattern: Missing the Purpose。PDF 物理页：[121, 122]。状态：`source-compared-revised`。

如本文档前文所明确，不同目的需要不同的架构。为了向变革领导者展示候选架构如何回应敏捷性，支持战略目的所需的工作产品与分析项目，与支持解决方案部署目的所需的工作产品截然不同。实践者必须让基本结构与概念适配不同目的。过多的建议会用“高层”“愿景式”“概念式”或“逻辑式”等术语掩盖本质差异。优秀的实践者知道如何区分支持战略的高层工作与支持解决方案交付的高层工作。

每个利益相关者、每个关注点，都要在每个目的中得到处理。

实践者在对广度、深度与时间跨度讲故事时，就错过了目的。如 3.2.1 节所述，关于广度、详细程度与规划期有一组粗略指导。此外，无论某个架构开发项目必须处理 EA 全景的哪些具体部分，实践者都会发现自己面对的边缘并不干净。

支持某一目的的架构通常对齐业务周期的不同时点，并被要求为不同决策提供信息，因为所有工作都必须与当前目的对齐。这可能改变关键工作产品的本质目的，但不太可能实质性改变架构中必须分析哪些组件。


### 语义规则与适用边界

- 架构目的由其支撑的业务决策确定，不能仅靠高层/逻辑或细节等级标签。
  - 适用边界：相同组件可能为不同目的分析，但产品的用途和表达要适配。
  - PDF 122：Architecture to support a purpose is typically aligned to support different points in the business cycle, and required to inform different decisions, as all work must be aligned to the purpose at hand. This may change the key work product’s essential purpose, but is unlikely to substantially change which components in the architecture must be analyzed.

### 原文内容单元

<details><summary>展开英文原文及文本块定位</summary>

**G186:p0121:c012 · PDF 121**

11.1. Failure Pattern: Missing the Purpose

**G186:p0121:c013 · PDF 121**

As clearly articulated earlier in this document, different purposes require different architecture. The actual work product and analysis project to produce a view demonstrating to a change leader how a candidate architecture addresses agility for the purpose of strategy is radically different than for the purpose of solution deployment. Practitioners must adapt the basic structure and concepts to different purposes. Too much advice masks the essential differences by using terms such as high-level or

**G186:p0122:c000 · PDF 122**

aspirational or conceptual or logical. A good Practitioner will know how to distinguish high-level work for the purpose of strategy from high-level work for the purpose of solution delivery.

**G186:p0122:c001 · PDF 122**

Every stakeholder and every concern are addressed in every purpose.

**G186:p0122:c002 · PDF 122**

Practitioners miss the purpose when they tell themselves stories about breadth, depth, and timeframe. As discussed in Section 3.2.1, there is a set of rough guidelines regarding breadth, level of detail, and planning horizon. Further, regardless of the exact parts of the EA Landscape that must be addressed by any particular architecture development project, a Practitioner will find themselves without clean edges.

**G186:p0122:c003 · PDF 122**

Architecture to support a purpose is typically aligned to support different points in the business cycle, and required to inform different decisions, as all work must be aligned to the purpose at hand. This may change the key work product’s essential purpose, but is unlikely to substantially change which components in the architecture must be analyzed.

</details>

<a id="11.2"></a>
## 11.2 失败模式：错过业务周期

原文：11.2. Failure Pattern: Missing the Business Cycle。PDF 物理页：[122]。状态：`source-compared-revised`。

多数领导者有兴趣获得关于复杂决策的有效建议。通常，实践者却在等待一份永远不会到来的规划流程邀请。领导者身边可能围满了只想推销自己钟爱项目的局部利益倡导者；作为回应，他们会主动减少让参与规划流程的人，只留下那些提供有用、平衡建议的人，以及那些他们希望为变革负责的人。

交付支持业务周期的架构，要求走在决策之前。实践者在规划周期之前工作（见图 4-1）。对许多实践者而言，走在规划周期之前是一种令人不适的处境：他们必须专注于准备别人都还没在想的活动。

例如，支持组合的架构为采用年度预算流程的组织促进预算过程。在这种周期下，预算定稿可能在第三季度末完成；这要求预算规划在第二季度末完成；这又要求候选目标架构与候选路线图的初稿在第二季度可用。利益相关者与决策者随后就能利用候选架构与候选路线图，规划和准备自己的预算申请，并在由此产生的预算谈判中为自己的申请辩护。实践者随后需要理解：自己的候选材料会在整个预算编制与谈判过程中被使用、拉伸和修改。简言之，实践者在第二、三季度持续迭代阶段 E 与阶段 F。

不熟悉多数组织规划流程中“有来有往”的实践者，会等待清晰性或决策；而这两者只在规划流程结束时才出现，不在中途。结果，实践者错过了自己在业务周期中的位置。


### 语义规则与适用边界

- 组合架构候选材料需要在预算形成过程中迭代 E/F。
  - 适用边界：第二、三季度是示例年度周期；应按组织实际日历配置，不能自动设为事实。
  - PDF 122：For example, Architecture to Support Portfolio facilitates the budget process for an organization that operates an annual budget process. With such a cycle, the budget finalization is likely done near the end of the third quarter. This requires the budget planning to be done near the end of the second quarter, which requires the first draft of the candidate Target Architecture and candidate roadmap to be available for the second quarter. Stakeholders and decision-makers are then able to use the candidate architecture and candidate roadmap in planning and preparing their budget submission and defending their submission in any resulting budget negotiations. The Practitioner then needs to understand their candidate material is used, stretched, and changed through the entire budget preparation and negotiation. In short, the Practitioner is involved in iterating through Phase E and F through the second and third quarter.

### 原文内容单元

<details><summary>展开英文原文及文本块定位</summary>

**G186:p0122:c004 · PDF 122**

11.2. Failure Pattern: Missing the Business Cycle

**G186:p0122:c005 · PDF 122**

Most leaders are interested in receiving effective advice about complex decisions. Usually, the Practitioners are waiting for an invitation to a planning process that will never come. Leaders may be surrounded by parochial champions who wish to pitch their pet projects. In response, they actively seek to reduce involvement in planning processes to those who provide useful, balanced advice and those they wish to hold accountable for the change.

**G186:p0122:c006 · PDF 122**

Delivering architecture to support the business cycle requires being ahead of decisions. The Practitioner works ahead of the planning cycle (see Figure 4-1). For many Practitioners, working ahead of the planning cycle is an uncomfortable position. They must be focused on preparing for activities that no one else is thinking about.

**G186:p0122:c007 · PDF 122**

For example, Architecture to Support Portfolio facilitates the budget process for an organization that operates an annual budget process. With such a cycle, the budget finalization is likely done near the end of the third quarter. This requires the budget planning to be done near the end of the second quarter, which requires the first draft of the candidate Target Architecture and candidate roadmap to be available for the second quarter. Stakeholders and decision-makers are then able to use the candidate architecture and candidate roadmap in planning and preparing their budget submission and defending their submission in any resulting budget negotiations. The Practitioner then needs to understand their candidate material is used, stretched, and changed through the entire budget preparation and negotiation. In short, the Practitioner is involved in iterating through Phase E and F through the second and third quarter.

**G186:p0122:c008 · PDF 122**

Practitioners who are unfamiliar with the give-and-take typical in most organizations’ planning processes will wait for clarity or decision. Both are only available at the end of the planning process, not in the middle. As a result, the Practitioner has missed their place in the business cycle.

</details>

<a id="11.2.1"></a>
## 11.2.1 决策之后的架构

原文：11.2.1. Architecture after Decision。PDF 物理页：[123]。状态：`source-compared-revised`。

本文档旨在帮助实践者交付有用的架构。决策之后才产生的架构不仅迟到，还可能引发冲突。最好的情况是，架构验证了该决策；既然决策已由有权做决策的领导者做出，验证毫无意义。最坏的情况是，架构证明领导者做错了决策。获得这种知识并进行纠偏在技术上有用，但它对 EA 团队造成的损害，以及决策之后执行下一步所浪费的时间与精力，不太可能由一个更好的决策来补偿。

擅长确立价值的实践者会敏锐意识到时间对几乎所有价值计算的影响。最后，擅长估算变革成本的实践者会敏锐意识到，误射对组织执行有效变革的能力有多么昂贵。

实践者能做的活动中，很少有比“决策之后再做架构”更危险的了。


### 语义规则与适用边界

- 决策后架构可能引发冲突与昂贵纠偏，应将架构前置支持决策。
  - 适用边界：源文对事后验证价值语气绝对；不能据此禁止必要审计、事故复盘或已授权纠偏。
  - PDF 123：This document is designed to assist Practitioners to deliver useful architecture. Architecture produced after decisions is not only late but may cause conflict. At best, the architecture will validate the decision. Given the decision has already been made by leaders with the authority to make the decision, validation is pointless. At worst, the architecture will demonstrate the leaders made the wrong decision. It is technically useful to gain this knowledge and perform a course correction. The damage to the EA team and wasted time and effort executing the next steps following the decision are unlikely to be compensated by a better decision.

### 原文内容单元

<details><summary>展开英文原文及文本块定位</summary>

**G186:p0123:c000 · PDF 123**

11.2.1. Architecture after Decision

**G186:p0123:c001 · PDF 123**

This document is designed to assist Practitioners to deliver useful architecture. Architecture produced after decisions is not only late but may cause conflict. At best, the architecture will validate the decision. Given the decision has already been made by leaders with the authority to make the decision, validation is pointless. At worst, the architecture will demonstrate the leaders made the wrong decision. It is technically useful to gain this knowledge and perform a course correction. The damage to the EA team and wasted time and effort executing the next steps following the decision are unlikely to be compensated by a better decision.

**G186:p0123:c002 · PDF 123**

Practitioners adept at establishing value will be keenly aware of the impact time has on almost every value calculation. Lastly, Practitioners adept at estimating the cost of change will be keenly aware of how expensive misfires are on the ability of an organization to execute an effective change.

**G186:p0123:c003 · PDF 123**

Few activities a Practitioner can perform are as dangerous as architecting after decision.

</details>

<a id="11.3"></a>
## 11.3 失败模式：不做架构

原文：11.3. Failure Pattern: Not Doing Architecture。PDF 物理页：[123, 124]。状态：`source-compared-revised`。

实践者在架构开发与变革过程中常常承担多个角色。第 15 章把利益相关者、主题专家、架构师、实施者与审计员识别为架构开发中的基本角色。实践者通常充当利益相关者的代理，通过自己对利益相关者偏好集合的理解代为决策。许多实践者沿着成长路径，在特定领域拥有专家知识；他们往往会以主题专家的身份向利益相关者、其他架构师与实施者提供建议与指导。某些企业的结构可能要求实践者充当实施者。实施者通常关注产品选型、配置挑战、保证质量与可重复性等细节；这些任务往往耗时甚多，以致实践者没有时间做架构。

许多 EA 团队落入“隐式架构”的陷阱：实践者忙于充当利益相关者代理、主题专家与实施者，架构却从未被描述、也从未得到利益相关者批准。作为“架构项目”的最终成果，交付的其实是实施设计、实施规格与标准定义的工作产品。这些是最终成果——但它们不是架构。

第 15 章将讨论深入评审那些缺少架构描述、视图与架构规格支撑的实施工作产品的必要性。直白地说，实践者能提供什么证据，证明实施符合架构、提供了处理利益相关者偏好以及组织使命、愿景、价值主张与目标的最佳可用方法？唯一的选择是“以断言代替合规”。

“以断言代替合规”充斥着个人偏见与“游客式仪表盘决策”。

实践者交付价值，不是靠碰巧做对实施，而是靠促进完整利益相关者集合理解其偏好在企业使命、愿景、价值主张与目标语境中的影响。无论这是通过准备回应关注点的视图这条容易的路，还是通过促进相互竞争的决策之间权衡来完成，都无关紧要。缺少理解，意味着架构及其所使能的价值是脆弱的。实践者一旦不再参与全景，就不能指望运营团队和未来的实施团队维持价值——他们对偏好、优先级或价值可追溯性一无所知。

没有架构，企业别无选择，只能跳到阶段 G——完全无准备，没有能力实施治理。

不为决策者与实施者做架构，是实践者能实施的最有害的做法。


### 语义规则与适用边界

- 没有显式架构描述和利益相关者批准的设计、规格与标准，不足以证明架构已完成。
  - 适用边界：隐式个人知识不能支持持续治理，合规断言必须有证据。
  - PDF 123：Many EA teams fall into the trap of performing implicit architecture. The Practitioner is so busy acting as a stakeholder’s agent, SME, and implementer that the architecture is never described and approved by a stakeholder. A work product that is really implementation design, and implementation specification and standards definition is provided as the end result of the “Architecture Project”. These work products are the end result – they are not architecture.

### 原文内容单元

<details><summary>展开英文原文及文本块定位</summary>

**G186:p0123:c004 · PDF 123**

11.3. Failure Pattern: Not Doing Architecture

**G186:p0123:c005 · PDF 123**

Practitioners will often fulfill multiple roles in the architecture development and change process. Chapter 15 identifies stakeholder, SME, architect, implementer, and auditor as the essential roles in architecture development. Practitioners will typically act as an agent for the stakeholder, making decisions by proxy through their understanding of the set of stakeholders’ preferences. Many Practitioners, by way of their growth path, would have expert knowledge in specific domains; they will tend to provide advice and guidance as SMEs to stakeholders, other architects, and implementers. Some Enterprise’s structure may demand a Practitioners to act as implementer. An implementer normally pays attention to details like product selection, configuration challenges, assuring quality and repeatability, etc. These tasks are often sufficiently time-consuming that the Practitioner does not have time to perform architecture.

**G186:p0123:c006 · PDF 123**

Many EA teams fall into the trap of performing implicit architecture. The Practitioner is so busy acting as a stakeholder’s agent, SME, and implementer that the architecture is never described and approved by a stakeholder. A work product that is really implementation design, and implementation specification and standards definition is provided as the end result of the “Architecture Project”. These work products are the end result – they are not architecture.

**G186:p0123:c007 · PDF 123**

Chapter 15 will discuss the need to deeply review implementation work products that exist unsupported by architecture description, views, and architecture specification. Bluntly, what evidence can a Practitioner provide that the implementation is in conformance with the architecture, provides the best available approach to addressing the stakeholders’ preferences and the organization’s mission, vision, value proposition, and objectives? The only choice is compliance by assertion.

**G186:p0123:c008 · PDF 123**

Compliance by assertion is rife with personal bias and “tourist dashboard decisions”.

**G186:p0123:c009 · PDF 123**

Practitioners deliver value not by tripping over the correct implementation but by facilitating the complete set of stakeholders to understand the implications of their preferences in the context of the Enterprise’s mission, vision, value propositions, and objectives. Whether this is done on the easy path

**G186:p0124:c000 · PDF 124**

by preparing views addressing concerns or by facilitating trade-off between competing decisions is immaterial. The absence of understanding means the architecture, and the value it enables, is fragile. The moment the Practitioner is unengaged on landscape, there can be no expectation that the value will be sustained by operational teams and future implementation teams who are unaware of either preference, priority, or traceability to value.

**G186:p0124:c001 · PDF 124**

Without an architecture, the Enterprise has no choice but to jump to Phase G – completely unprepared, with no ability to exercise implementation governance.

**G186:p0124:c002 · PDF 124**

Not performing architecture to support decision-makers and implementers is the most pernicious practice a Practitioner can perform.

</details>

<a id="11.4"></a>
## 11.4 管理创新、创造力与情境

原文：11.4. Managing Innovation, Creativity, and Circumstance。PDF 物理页：[124, 125]。状态：`source-compared-revised`。

自上而下的方向与规划，为敏捷组织提供了部分答案：它提供做出战术决策所需的指南、约束与清晰度。有时，正确的决策就是开展计划外的变革。

无论实践者是因为失败、还是因为一个良好的审慎决策，而在变革实施时准备不足，实践者仍需要为变革活动提供有用的支持。只是利益相关者对项目以预期成本、在预期时间内交付预期价值的信心必然降低；未知因素的范围使高置信度无法成立。

这种信心缺失只意味着：架构在实现组织目标方面带有更多不确定性或风险。此时，实践者必须把所有精力集中在**风险缓解**上。

务实地看，实践者将不断履行风险管理职能。实践者不应深入实施细节，而应发现并揭示与目标相关的不确定性，以提供战术性治理支持。每个项目都有某种形式的收益陈述，每个组织都有某种形式的战略；实践者只需在没有中间踏脚石的情况下把点连起来。这里的重要区别是：实践者不应被指望纠正项目的收益陈述与实现计划，而应被指望缓解“实现项目所述收益”的不确定性。

TOGAF 阶段 G 为这项活动提供了一个步骤，实践者在此向实施项目提供指导。实践者必须在“指导”与“亲自实施”之间走一条细线；关于实践者可能扮演的不同角色，见 11.3 节与 15.2 节。实施者被期望在项目约束内行事，实践者被期望审视项目的语境。当组织跳到阶段 G 时，最有价值的行动与应对敏捷等快速实施方法时相同：实践者必须聚焦实施项目的范围，在**企业**收益实现（而非项目收益实现）的语境中促进良好决策，并确保利益相关者与实施者理解其选择对企业收益的影响——但不是驱使他们做出不同选择。这是一个非常细微的区别，也是对“不纠正项目，而是确保利益相关者与实施团队诚实理解价值与收益”的重申。

组织跳到阶段 G 时，创新与创造力处于前台。原本应在所需详细程度上提供指导与约束的深思熟虑的架构开发将会缺失。当实践者所在组织急于求成时，组织关注的是通过差异化与实验获得价值。通常，不澄清依赖关系，就无法实现持续的效率提升。实践者应预期，急于求成的组织通常充分意识到跨时间和规模化维持实验的困难。因此，实践者必须聚焦价值实现。直白地说，这与更深思熟虑的方法并无不同：利益相关者的偏好与优先级驱动架构开发。

就 TOGAF ADM 阶段而言，实践者将不断进行微观迭代，把离散的价值陈述一路探索到实施，目的是澄清预期价值，以及实施中哪些因素造成不确定性。为此，实践者必须把全部注意力集中在价值实现关键路径上的一组狭窄关注点上。

当组织跳到阶段 G 时，实践者通常需要充当利益相关者的代理。实践者必须敏锐意识到同时充当架构师与利益相关者代理的危险；必须防范隧道视野、个人偏见与“游客式仪表盘决策”。针对关键路径上狭窄关注点以及实施项目的专门报告，构成一种控制，用以缓解准备不足与职责未分离的问题。


### 语义规则与适用边界

- 直接进入实施时，实践者重点揭示达成已陈述收益的不确定性并提供战术治理。
  - 适用边界：不是擅自改写已授权项目的收益目标或实施计划。
  - PDF 124：Pragmatically the Practitioner is going to be constantly performing a risk management function. Rather than diving into the details of implementation the Practitioner needs to find and expose uncertainty associated with the objective to provide tactical governance support. Every project will have some form of benefits statement. Every organization has some form of strategy. The Practitioner simply has to connect the dots without the benefit of any intermediate stepping stones. The important distinction here is that the Practitioner is not expected to correct the project regarding benefits statement and realization plan. The Practitioner is expected to mitigate uncertainty regarding realizing the benefits stated in the project.
- 通过围绕价值关键路径的窄范围微迭代澄清预期价值与实施不确定性。
  - 适用边界：不确定性较大应降低置信度，不能声称完整规划已补齐。
  - PDF 125：In terms of the TOGAF ADM phases, the Practitioner will be running constant micro-iterations exploring discrete statements of value through to the implementation, with the purpose of clarifying the value expected and what in the implementation creates uncertainty. In order to perform this, the Practitioner will have to focus all attention on a narrow set of concerns on the critical path to value realization.
- 架构师兼任利益相关者代理时，要通过专门报告限制偏见与职责未分离风险。
  - 适用边界：代理意见不得伪装成利益相关者真实批准。
  - PDF 125：When the organization Jumps to Phase G, the Practitioner will routinely need to act as the stakeholders’ agent. Practitioners must be keenly aware of the danger acting as both the architect and the stakeholders’ agent. Care must be taken to guard against tunnel vision, personal bias, and “tourist dashboard decisions”. Specialized reporting against the narrow set of concerns on the critical path to value and the Implementation Project form the control that mitigates lack of preparation and failing to separate duties.

### 原文内容单元

<details><summary>展开英文原文及文本块定位</summary>

**G186:p0124:c003 · PDF 124**

11.4. Managing Innovation, Creativity, and Circumstance

**G186:p0124:c004 · PDF 124**

Top-down direction and planning provides part of the answer for a nimble organization. It provides the guidelines, constraints, and clarity required to make tactical decisions. Sometimes the correct decision is to embark on unplanned change.

**G186:p0124:c005 · PDF 124**

Whether the Practitioner has arrived at implementation of change unprepared because of a failure or because of a good deliberate decision, the Practitioner still needs to provide useful support of the change activity. Stakeholders simply have to have less confidence that the project will deliver the expected value with the expected cost and the projected time. The range of unknown ones precludes high confidence.

**G186:p0124:c006 · PDF 124**

This lack of confidence simply means the architecture has more uncertainty, or risk, associated with realizing the organization’s objectives. At this point, Practitioners have to focus all of their energy on risk mitigation.

**G186:p0124:c007 · PDF 124**

Pragmatically the Practitioner is going to be constantly performing a risk management function. Rather than diving into the details of implementation the Practitioner needs to find and expose uncertainty associated with the objective to provide tactical governance support. Every project will have some form of benefits statement. Every organization has some form of strategy. The Practitioner simply has to connect the dots without the benefit of any intermediate stepping stones. The important distinction here is that the Practitioner is not expected to correct the project regarding benefits statement and realization plan. The Practitioner is expected to mitigate uncertainty regarding realizing the benefits stated in the project.

**G186:p0124:c008 · PDF 124**

TOGAF Phase G provides a step for this activity where the Practitioner provides guidance to the Implementation Project. The Practitioner must walk a line between guiding and performing implementation; for a discussion of the different roles a Practitioner may play, see Section 11.3 and Section 15.2. Implementers are expected to live within the constraints of the project; Practitioners are expected to look at the context of the project. The most valuable actions when the organization jumps to Phase G are identical to addressing rapid implementation methods such as Agile. The Practitioner must focus on the scope of the Implementation Project, facilitating good decision-making in the context not of project benefits realization but of Enterprise benefits realization, and ensuring the stakeholders and implementers understand the implications of their choices regarding Enterprise benefits not

**G186:p0125:c000 · PDF 125**

driving them to make different choices. This is a very fine distinction and is it a reiteration of not fixing the project but ensuring stakeholders and implementation teams understand what can honestly be expected in terms of value and benefit.

**G186:p0125:c001 · PDF 125**

Innovation and creativity are at the fore when an organization jumps to Phase G. Thoughtful architecture development providing guidance and constraints at the required level of detail will be missing. When the Practitioner’s organization is in a hurry they are focused on receiving value through differentiation and experimentation. Typically, a sustained efficiency gain is not achieved without clarifying dependency. Practitioners should expect that organizations in a hurry are usually fully aware of the difficulty sustaining experiments across time and when scaled. Hence, the Practitioner must focus on value realization. Bluntly, this is not different than a more thoughtful approach: The stakeholders’ preference and priority drives the architecture development.

**G186:p0125:c002 · PDF 125**

In terms of the TOGAF ADM phases, the Practitioner will be running constant micro-iterations exploring discrete statements of value through to the implementation, with the purpose of clarifying the value expected and what in the implementation creates uncertainty. In order to perform this, the Practitioner will have to focus all attention on a narrow set of concerns on the critical path to value realization.

**G186:p0125:c003 · PDF 125**

When the organization Jumps to Phase G, the Practitioner will routinely need to act as the stakeholders’ agent. Practitioners must be keenly aware of the danger acting as both the architect and the stakeholders’ agent. Care must be taken to guard against tunnel vision, personal bias, and “tourist dashboard decisions”. Specialized reporting against the narrow set of concerns on the critical path to value and the Implementation Project form the control that mitigates lack of preparation and failing to separate duties.

</details>

<a id="12.1"></a>
## 12.1 敏捷企业中的架构

原文：12.1. Architecture in an Agile Enterprise。PDF 物理页：[126]。状态：`source-compared-revised`。

关于与敏捷实施方法对齐，已有大量讨论；为了把 ADM 各阶段与这些开发方法对齐，不知耗费了多少笔墨。所有这些讨论都模糊了实施与架构之间的界线。TOGAF 标准在阶段 G 与敏捷开发对齐。仅此而已。

良好的支持组合的架构或支持项目的架构，会识别企业需要哪些产品、产品的边界，以及产品负责人受到哪些约束。简言之，良好的架构定义企业的待办事项。

支持项目的架构与支持解决方案交付的架构会有一组约束，限制敏捷团队的选择。这些约束正是单个产品必须服从企业问题之处，产品负责人的局部偏好在此不成立。

然后是阶段 G，实施治理：实践者服务于守护使命、愿景、目标与投资路线图的利益相关者。简言之，守护企业价值。


### 语义规则与适用边界

- 本指南把敏捷实施与 ADM 的连接置于阶段 G，并由上层架构约束产品边界和选择。
  - 适用边界：这是敏捷实施关系，不是禁止敏捷架构方法或把 sprint 强映射每个 ADM 阶段。
  - PDF 126：There has been a great deal of conversation about aligning to agile implementation methods. Ink has been spilled trying to align the phases of the ADM to these development methods. All of this conversation has blurred the line between implementation and architecture. The TOGAF Standard aligns to agile development in Phase G. Full stop.

### 原文内容单元

<details><summary>展开英文原文及文本块定位</summary>

**G186:p0126:c001 · PDF 126**

12.1. Architecture in an Agile Enterprise

**G186:p0126:c002 · PDF 126**

There has been a great deal of conversation about aligning to agile implementation methods. Ink has been spilled trying to align the phases of the ADM to these development methods. All of this conversation has blurred the line between implementation and architecture. The TOGAF Standard aligns to agile development in Phase G. Full stop.

**G186:p0126:c003 · PDF 126**

A good Architecture to Support Portfolio, or Project, will identify what products the Enterprise needs, the boundary of the products, and what constraints a product owner has. In short, a good architecture defines the Enterprise’s backlog.

**G186:p0126:c004 · PDF 126**

Architecture to Support Project and Solution Delivery will have a set of constraints that limit the choices of the agile team. These constraints are where an individual product must bend to Enterprise issues and the parochial preference of a product owner is not valid.

**G186:p0126:c005 · PDF 126**

Then Phase G, Implementation Governance: the Practitioner serves the stakeholders guarding the mission, vision, goals, and investment roadmap. In short, guarding Enterprise value.

</details>

<a id="12.2"></a>
## 12.2 领域架构

原文：12.2. Architecture for a Domain。PDF 物理页：[126]。状态：`source-compared-revised`。

一条常见的失败路径是：领域架构师按不同的目的工作，或假装自己与团队其他成员在做不同的架构项目。领域必须融入 EA 的整体；同样，EA 的其余部分也必须与领域相契合。否则就是“游客式仪表盘决策”（见 6.2 节）。

安全是一个特殊的领域。安全架构只有在参照其他领域时才存在，最好把它视为一个关注点。实践者始终要处理利益相关者的安全与风险关注点。


### 语义规则与适用边界

- 领域架构与整体 EA 双向相容，须在共同目的和项目语境中工作。
  - 适用边界：安全是跨域关注点；不能孤立形成不受整体权衡的安全方案。
  - PDF 126：A common failure path is for domain architects to work to a different purpose, or pretend that they are working on a different Architecture Project than the rest of the team. A domain[1] must fit into the whole of the EA. Also, the rest of the EA must fit with a domain. Anything else is a tourist dashboard decision (see Section 6.2).

### 原文内容单元

A common failure path is for domain architects to work to a different purpose, or pretend that they are working on a different Architecture Project than the rest of the team. A domain[1] must fit into the whole of the EA. Also, the rest of the EA must fit with a domain. Anything else is a tourist dashboard decision (see Section 6.2).

**G186:p0126:c008 · PDF 126**

A distinct domain is security. A security architecture only exists in reference to other domains and is best considered a concern. Practitioners will always address their stakeholders’ security[2] and risk concerns.

<a id="12.3"></a>
## 12.3 应对事件时的架构

原文：12.3. Architecture in Response to an Incident。PDF 物理页：[126, 127, 128]。状态：`source-compared-revised`。

某件事发生了，组织的回应就是把它修好。

作为起点，实践者应把风险理解为不确定性对达成目标的影响，并理解风险偏好与风险容忍度。实现所有目标都具有不确定性，企业的回应由风险容忍度与风险偏好驱动。

风险偏好为“为实现预期成果而承担多少风险”提供平衡指导。风险偏好通常表达为风险/业务影响与可能性网格上的边界，或定性度量。例如：企业今年愿为 y 美元回报承担 x 美元风险，或对生命损失零容忍。清晰理解的风险偏好，既定义了组织愿意接受的风险水平，也定义了确定该水平的策略。

对于高于可接受水平的风险，风险偏好定义了所用的缓解策略；超出风险偏好的风险，其策略通常是转移或规避。

风险容忍度处理的是与预期之间的偏差。简言之，当企业的不确定性被突破时该怎么办。不确定性的最常见表现是未能达成期望；此时，企业已确定无法实现其目标。

事件会改变利益相关者对风险的偏好。这是一项需求变更，架构必须调整。实践者的核心作用，是就目标应做哪些变更提供可靠建议，以及为达成变更所需的相关工作，使实现利益相关者目标达到可接受的确定性。当几乎没有变更具有可接受成本、利益相关者面临“取消目标还是取消变更”的选择时，实践者不应感到意外。

尾注

[1] 参见附录 F。

[2] 详细讨论请阅读所引用的 Open Group 指南《将风险与安全融入 TOGAF® 企业架构》。


### 语义规则与适用边界

- 事件导致风险偏好改变时是需求变化，架构需调整目标及达成目标的工作建议。
  - 适用边界：如没有成本可接受的选项，取消目标或取消变更也可能进入利益相关者选择。
  - PDF 127：An incident changes the stakeholders’ preferences with regard to risk. This is a change in requirement, and the architecture must adjust. The central role of the Practitioner is to provide solid advice on what changes to the target, and the associated work to achieve the change will reach an acceptable certainty of reaching the stakeholders’ objective. Practitioners should not be surprised when there are few changes that have an acceptable cost, and the stakeholder is faced with the option of canceling the objective or canceling the change.
- 风险偏好表达为达成成果愿承担风险的界线，风险容忍度处理偏离预期。
  - 适用边界：这是本指南的表述；示例金额和零损失容忍并非当前企业授权。
  - PDF 126：The risk appetite provides guidance balancing the amount of risk taken to achieve an expected outcome. Risk appetite is typically expressed as a boundary on a risk/business impact and likelihood grid, or qualitative measures. For example, the Enterprise will risk $x for $y reward this year, or has zero tolerance for loss of life. A well understood risk appetite defines both the level of risk the organization is willing to accept as well as its strategy in defining this level.

### 原文内容单元

<details><summary>展开英文原文及文本块定位</summary>

**G186:p0126:c009 · PDF 126**

12.3. Architecture in Response to an Incident

**G186:p0126:c010 · PDF 126**

Something happened, and the organization’s response is to fix it.

**G186:p0126:c011 · PDF 126**

As a starting point the Practitioner should understand risk as the effect of uncertainty on reaching objectives, risk appetite, and risk tolerance. Achieving all objectives is uncertain, and an Enterprise’s response is driven by risk tolerance and risk appetite.

**G186:p0126:c012 · PDF 126**

The risk appetite provides guidance balancing the amount of risk taken to achieve an expected outcome. Risk appetite is typically expressed as a boundary on a risk/business impact and likelihood grid, or qualitative measures. For example, the Enterprise will risk $x for $y reward this year, or has zero tolerance for loss of life. A well understood risk appetite defines both the level of risk the organization is willing to accept as well as its strategy in defining this level.

**G186:p0127:c000 · PDF 127**

For risks above this acceptable level, it defines the strategy used for mitigation. Strategy for risk in excess risk appetite is typically transference or avoidance.

**G186:p0127:c001 · PDF 127**

Risk tolerance addresses deviations from what is expected. In short, what to do when the Enterprise’s uncertainty is exceeded. The most common expression of uncertainty is failure to achieve expectations. At this point, the Enterprise is certain it will not achieve its objectives.

**G186:p0127:c002 · PDF 127**

An incident changes the stakeholders’ preferences with regard to risk. This is a change in requirement, and the architecture must adjust. The central role of the Practitioner is to provide solid advice on what changes to the target, and the associated work to achieve the change will reach an acceptable certainty of reaching the stakeholders’ objective. Practitioners should not be surprised when there are few changes that have an acceptable cost, and the stakeholder is faced with the option of canceling the objective or canceling the change.

**G186:p0128:c000 · PDF 128**

Endnotes

**G186:p0128:c001 · PDF 128**

[1] See Appendix F. [2] For a detailed discussion, read the referenced Open Group Guide: Integrating Risk and Security within a TOGAF® Enterprise Architecture.

</details>

<a id="13"></a>
## 13. 过渡架构：管理复杂路线图

原文：13. Transition Architecture: Managing Complex Roadmaps。PDF 物理页：[131]。状态：`source-compared-revised`。

至此，本文档大多用线性时间尺度描述概念，使努力与过程显得简单。它给人的印象是：创建一组由业务周期与规划期导向的良好对齐工作包，就能得到潜在的过渡状态与近乎线性的路线图。回想第 5 章在 EA 存储库语境中的那句简单陈述：“基线为所有变更提供参照。目标状态是利益相关者已批准的状态。过渡状态是现状与目标状态之间部分实现的目标。把 EA 全景的四个特征混合起来：广度、深度、时间与时效性。再把可能在不同时间、以不同详细程度处理同一主题的不同架构项目混合起来。”这就是提示现实世界复杂性的唯一线索。

除这些特征之外，增加复杂性的其他组织因素还有：

- 企业外部的进步与变化
- 共享服务
- 与供应商和合作伙伴的协作，包括组合所有权模型
- 难以穿透的依赖
- 多重地缘政治边界（财年日历、监管、文化）
- 团队成熟度与成长速度的差异
- EA 团队模型（联邦式、集中式等）
- 多种解决方案的可用性，或在用产品宣布停止服务（end-of-life）

这就是现实。一份企业路线图会被分解为分部、组合或地域。企业会同时追求不止一个目标，例如效率与重构。每个业务周期，路线图都会被重新审视，以自下而上、有时自上而下的方式做调整。这是一个清晰的用例，说明为什么需要良好的 EA 存储库：它维护现状与目标状态的完整性，同时允许创建变体。


### 语义规则与适用边界

- 存储库必须保留共同现状与目标的完整性，同时允许按分段、组合或地域建立变体。
  - 适用边界：路线图是动态、多目标、多状态结构，不能只保存一条线性时间轴。
  - PDF 131：This is the reality. One Enterprise roadmap gets broken down into segment, portfolio, or geography. The Enterprise will be pursuing more than one concurrent goal, say efficiency and retooling. For each business cycle, the roadmap is revisited to make adjustments, bottom-up and at times top-down. This is a clear use-case that drives the need for a good EA Repository: a repository that maintains the integrity of the current state and target state, but allows creation of variants.

### 原文内容单元

<details><summary>展开英文原文及文本块定位</summary>

**G186:p0131:c000 · PDF 131**

13. Transition Architecture: Managing Complex

**G186:p0131:c001 · PDF 131**

Roadmaps

**G186:p0131:c002 · PDF 131**

Until now, this document made the effort and process simple by describing most of the concepts using a linear time scale. It gave an impression that creating a well aligned set of work packages vectored by business cycle and planning horizon gives you potential transition states and a near linear roadmap. Recall this simple statement made in Chapter 5 in the context of the EA Repository: “Baseline provides reference for all change. The target state is what stakeholders have approved. Transition states are partially realized targets between current state and target state. Mix the four characteristics of the EA Landscape: breadth, depth, time, and recency. Mix the different Architecture Projects that can work on the same subject at different times and at different levels of detail.” That’s the only hint to indicate real-world complexity.

**G186:p0131:c003 · PDF 131**

In addition to characteristics, other organizational factors that add to complexity are:

**G186:p0131:c004 · PDF 131**

• Advancements and changes outside the Enterprise

**G186:p0131:c005 · PDF 131**

• Shared services

**G186:p0131:c006 · PDF 131**

• Collaboration with suppliers and partners, including portfolio ownership model

**G186:p0131:c007 · PDF 131**

• Impenetrable dependencies

**G186:p0131:c008 · PDF 131**

• Multiple geopolitical boundaries (fiscal calendars, regulations, cultures)

**G186:p0131:c009 · PDF 131**

• Varying rate of maturity and growth of teams

**G186:p0131:c010 · PDF 131**

• EA team model (federated, centralized, etc.)

**G186:p0131:c011 · PDF 131**

• Availability of multiple solutions or announcement of end-of-life for products currently in use

**G186:p0131:c012 · PDF 131**

This is the reality. One Enterprise roadmap gets broken down into segment, portfolio, or geography. The Enterprise will be pursuing more than one concurrent goal, say efficiency and retooling. For each business cycle, the roadmap is revisited to make adjustments, bottom-up and at times top-down. This is a clear use-case that drives the need for a good EA Repository: a repository that maintains the integrity of the current state and target state, but allows creation of variants.

</details>

<a id="13.1"></a>
## 13.1 路线图分组

原文：13.1. Roadmap Grouping。PDF 物理页：[131, 132]。状态：`source-compared-revised`。

从支持初始战略的一个版本开始，把存储库从战略层填充到项目层。组合获得接受后，按需创建版本；候选版本获得接受后，为现状与目标架构建立基线。为当前过渡状态创建多个基线。为每个变量、关注点或一组相关变量创建架构副本。

使用相同的规划期来展示影响与结果。规划期一旦改变，分析就会变复杂，并使多数决策失去连续性。

每条不同的父路线图——例如欧洲联盟运营与澳大利亚运营各有路线图——都要如此命名和标识。对那些为 what-if 分析而创建的派生路线图，采用适当的命名与版本化概念，并让人能直观识别已被弃用的备选方案。


### 语义规则与适用边界

- 比较路线图用相同规划终点，并为父路线图、派生方案和废弃方案明确命名版本。
  - 适用边界：固定同一时长而开始时间不同会造成比较失真。
  - PDF 131：Use the same planning horizon to showcase the impact and outcome. The moment planning horizons change, analysis becomes complex and results in loss of continuity for most decisions.

### 原文内容单元

<details><summary>展开英文原文及文本块定位</summary>

**G186:p0131:c013 · PDF 131**

13.1. Roadmap Grouping

**G186:p0131:c014 · PDF 131**

Start with one version that supports the initial strategy. Flesh out the repository from strategy to project. Upon acceptance of the portfolio, create versions as necessary. Once the candidate versions are accepted, baseline both current and Target Architectures. Create multiple baselines of the current transitional state. Create copies of the architecture, one per variable, concern, or a related group of variables.

**G186:p0131:c015 · PDF 131**

Use the same planning horizon to showcase the impact and outcome. The moment planning horizons change, analysis becomes complex and results in loss of continuity for most decisions.

**G186:p0132:c000 · PDF 132**

Each distinct parent roadmap – say if there is a separate roadmap for European Union Operations and Australian Operations – name and identify them as such. Employ appropriate naming and versioning concepts for and derived roadmaps of those created for what-if analysis. Make it intuitive to identify discarded alternatives.

</details>

<a id="13.2"></a>
## 13.2 比较架构

原文：13.2. Comparing Architectures。PDF 物理页：[132, 133]。状态：`source-compared-revised`。

创建独立路线图的意义，在于对齐每个架构项目的范围。当企业具有本章前面提到的任一特征或组织因素时，创建独立的架构项目与路线图来处理这种复杂性就是合理的。

为流程、业务术语、应用等采用标准参考架构，可以支持跨项目、跨路线图的分析。使用标准模型提供了必要的灵活性，以便跨解决方案供应商的实施模型进行映射；它也有助于评估潜在供应商的投标与报价。这也是 ABB 派上用场的地方：跨项目对 ABB 的实施与使用可以轻松分析。

把所有架构建立在与实施无关的参考模型之上，就能轻松识别对某个特定架构的修改所带来的影响。如图 13-1 所示，EA 存储库工具可以支持识别变更，无论变更针对的是某个架构组件的属性，还是组件目录的修改。在与联邦式团队协作时，使用此类工具和公共参考模型，可以极大地协调和沟通架构变更的影响。在路线图内，最好保持分析模式一致。

〔图示：图 13-1：使用存储库管理路线图——识别变更；参阅本节结构化引用。〕



比较架构的同一概念，也可用于创建和分析架构的逐年修改。在图 13-2 中，所用的 EA 存储库工具允许实践者把变更追溯到基线或修订版本。

〔图示：图 13-2：架构的影响分析；参阅本节结构化引用。〕



创建路线图时，要注意变更的影响。任何变更在引入时都会损害效率与整体吞吐量，有时还会导致重复投资。这类短期负面影响会掩盖对路线图的偏离。注入适当的标记，以识别任何非预期的次优化或路线图偏离。价值与成果地图应呈现实现价值的时间，以及规划期末的收益/损失。

相关结构化资料：[figure-13-1](../figures/G186/fig13-1.json)。本节核对图注及正文语义；图内标签、拓扑和甘特任务的完整审校状态以独立图记录为准，本轮正文覆盖不会自动升级。
相关结构化资料：[figure-13-2](../figures/G186/fig13-2.json)。本节核对图注及正文语义；图内标签、拓扑和甘特任务的完整审校状态以独立图记录为准，本轮正文覆盖不会自动升级。

### 语义规则与适用边界

- 共同的实施中立参考模型支持跨项目/供应商/联邦团队比较及变更追溯。
  - 适用边界：标准化比较不表示强制同一实现；分析模式需一致。
  - PDF 132：Basing all of the architectures on an implementation-neutral reference model allows impact of modifications to a specific architecture to be identified easily. As shown in Figure 13-1, the EA Repository tool could provide support to identify the change, whether it is to one of the attributes of an architectural component or a modification to the catalog of components. While working with a federated team, uses of such a tool and use of common reference models can go a long way to coordinate and communicate the impact of architecture changes. Within the roadmap, it is better to keep the analysis patterns consistent.
- 路线图应标记短期效率损失、非预期次优和偏离，并报告规划期末得失与价值时间。
  - 适用边界：短期负面变化可掩盖偏离，不能仅报告最终收益。
  - PDF 133：When creating the roadmap, pay attention to impact of change. Any change, when introduced, will tarnish the efficiency, overall throughput, and sometimes call for duplicative investments. Such short- term negative impacts can mask deviations from the roadmap. Inject appropriate markers to identify any unintended sub-optimization or deviations from the roadmap. The value and outcome map should present the time to value and gain/loss at the end of the planning horizon.

### 原文内容单元

<details><summary>展开英文原文及文本块定位</summary>

**G186:p0132:c001 · PDF 132**

13.2. Comparing Architectures

**G186:p0132:c002 · PDF 132**

The point of creating separate roadmaps is to align the scope of each Architecture Project. When the Enterprise has any one of the characteristic or organizational factors identified earlier in this chapter, it would make sense to create a separate Architecture Project and roadmap to deal with this complexity.

**G186:p0132:c003 · PDF 132**

Employing a standard reference architecture for process, business terms, applications, etc., supports cross-project and cross-roadmap analysis. Using a standard model provides the flexibility required to map across implementation models of the solution suppliers. It also helps in evaluating bids and offers from potential suppliers. This is another place where use of ABBs would come in handy. Implementation and use of ABBs across projects can be analyzed with ease.

**G186:p0132:c004 · PDF 132**

Basing all of the architectures on an implementation-neutral reference model allows impact of modifications to a specific architecture to be identified easily. As shown in Figure 13-1, the EA Repository tool could provide support to identify the change, whether it is to one of the attributes of an architectural component or a modification to the catalog of components. While working with a federated team, uses of such a tool and use of common reference models can go a long way to coordinate and communicate the impact of architecture changes. Within the roadmap, it is better to keep the analysis patterns consistent.

**G186:p0132:c005 · PDF 132**

Figure 13-1: Using Repository for Managing Roadmaps – Identify Change

**G186:p0133:c000 · PDF 133**

This same concept of comparing architectures can be used to create and analyze year-over-year modifications to the architecture. In Figure 13-2, the EA Repository tool in use allows the Practitioner to trace a change to the baseline or the revised version.

**G186:p0133:c001 · PDF 133**

Figure 13-2: Impact Analysis of Architectures

**G186:p0133:c002 · PDF 133**

When creating the roadmap, pay attention to impact of change. Any change, when introduced, will tarnish the efficiency, overall throughput, and sometimes call for duplicative investments. Such short- term negative impacts can mask deviations from the roadmap. Inject appropriate markers to identify any unintended sub-optimization or deviations from the roadmap. The value and outcome map should present the time to value and gain/loss at the end of the planning horizon.

</details>

<a id="13.3"></a>
## 13.3 一般指导

原文：13.3. General Guidance。PDF 物理页：[133, 134]。状态：`source-compared-revised`。

与不止一个架构项目或变革工作相交的工作包或架构规格，也会引入复杂性。每个企业的环境都高度动态，迫使人们不时进行权衡并运用专家判断。实施项目总是与外部环境发展带来的所有影响相隔绝。复杂性之所以出现，是因为每个过渡状态对企业而言都是功能完整、可运营的状态。架构与路线图不断演进，以跟上或领先这些外部变化。

从头开始时，实践者可能受益于只掌握关于全景的有限信息。随着全景被持续的架构项目填充，要始终注意对细节的无情抽象。把自己的偏见与包袱放在一边，把利益相关者偏好放在一边；一切只关乎指导选择所需的最少、绝对必要的信息。保持数据集一致；在分析架构时消除噪音与失真。

创建路线图时的常见陷阱包括范围界定不当。架构项目可能把某些职能排除在范围之外。本文档前文明确警告，不要偏离架构项目的章程。细则在于：如果你识别出一项需求或差距，就把它指出来——不要去开发那份架构。实践者有责任指出依赖关系，并记录其存在以及该差距在路线图中的处置方式。这类推迟的事项将成为它自己的路线图。日后为该差距开发架构时，要确保你在固定的**时间区间**内工作（与相关路线图有相同的结束日期），而不是固定的**时长**（例如每份路线图都三年）。


### 语义规则与适用边界

- 识别范围外需求或差距时，记录依赖和处置并安排后续路线图。
  - 适用边界：指出问题不等于越权开展全部范围外架构；相关路线图保持同一结束日期。
  - PDF 134：Common traps while creating roadmaps include incorrect scoping. The Architecture Project may exclude certain functions from the scope. Earlier chapters of this document explicitly warned you not to stray away from the charter of the Architecture Project. The fine-print is that, if you identify a need, a gap, call it out – don’t work on developing the architecture. It is the responsibility of the Practitioner to call out the dependency and document its existence and the disposition of the gap in the roadmap. Such deferred items will become its own roadmap. When developing architecture for this gap at a later date, make sure that you operate in a fixed block of time (same end dates as related roadmaps), not a fixed block of duration (say three years for each roadmap).

### 原文内容单元

<details><summary>展开英文原文及文本块定位</summary>

**G186:p0133:c003 · PDF 133**

13.3. General Guidance

**G186:p0133:c004 · PDF 133**

A work package or an architecture specification that intersects more than one Architecture Project or change effort also introduces complexity. The environment for every Enterprise is highly dynamic, forcing a need for trade-off and expert judgment every so often. Implementation Projects are invariably insulated from all impact from developments in the external environment. Complexity happens because every transitional state is a fully functional and operational state for the Enterprise. The architecture and roadmap evolve to stay abreast or ahead of such external changes.

**G186:p0133:c005 · PDF 133**

When starting afresh, the Practitioner potentially has the benefit of working with the limited set of information about the landscape. As the landscape is populated from ongoing Architecture Projects, continually pay attention to ruthless abstraction of detail. Set your biases and baggage aside. Set the stakeholder preference aside. It is all about the least and absolute necessary information to guide a choice. Keep the dataset consistent. Eliminate noise and distortions when performing analysis of architectures.

**G186:p0134:c000 · PDF 134**

Common traps while creating roadmaps include incorrect scoping. The Architecture Project may exclude certain functions from the scope. Earlier chapters of this document explicitly warned you not to stray away from the charter of the Architecture Project. The fine-print is that, if you identify a need, a gap, call it out – don’t work on developing the architecture. It is the responsibility of the Practitioner to call out the dependency and document its existence and the disposition of the gap in the roadmap. Such deferred items will become its own roadmap. When developing architecture for this gap at a later date, make sure that you operate in a fixed block of time (same end dates as related roadmaps), not a fixed block of duration (say three years for each roadmap).

</details>

<a id="14"></a>
## 14. 阶段 H（协调与业务周期在行动）

原文：14. Phase H (Coordination and Business Cycle in Action)。PDF 物理页：[135, 136, 137]。状态：`source-compared-revised`。

开发 EA 的原因非常简单：**引导有效变革**。变革只有在获得充分资源支持时才能实现。每个企业都有规划并分配资源的业务周期，通常为一个财年。财年日期不可变通，决策将依据可获得的数据与合理判断做出。

如果企业请求建立 EA 能力，这就是在承认：驱动投资与变革的“隐式”架构及其判断，并未交付企业想要的结果。EA 工作很可能是在本业务周期预算分配之后才启动的，或者能影响本业务周期决策的时间非常有限。不要在本周期浪费时间；坦然接受这种“隐式承认”，把重点放在为下一周期构建数据上。尽管没有明说，赞助人是指望用 EA 来保护“未来”的决策。实践者一旦意识到自己已赶不上下一个周期，就应把时间投入转向更新整个团队的求职简历（见 11.2 节）。

阶段 H 要求实践者识别自下而上的变革驱动因素——因可用技术进步、或因控制企业运营或环境的条件变化而产生的变革——并为下一个目标过渡状态启动架构工作（自上而下的驱动因素）。这并不意味着实践者需要把 EA 能力章程或预算覆盖的一切都细化出来。

前几章反复强调“刚好足够的架构”以及 EA 全景的特征。要了解团队界定工作范围的能力与容量。记住，“企业”的定义是可伸缩的，用来控制分析范围。如果这是开发支持战略的架构的第一轮，就据此界定工作范围。按企业的能力与就绪度，按比例定义并分配工作包。所有这些都指向一件事——影响并争取下一个周期的资源。

把一切与预算周期挂钩，只是凸显了良好 EA 在指导和约束变革决策方面的重要性。当决策做出之前没有来自良好 EA 团队的实用输入时，决策仍然会做出；它甚至可能是个好选择，但那是一个信息更少的选择。

一旦意识到数据本可获得却姗姗来迟，无论所做决策质量如何，EA 团队都失去了相关性。这是一个双输场景，会导致人们质疑 EA 团队的价值与目的。

取决于企业规模（与 EA 工作范围无关），预算编制可能在财年开始前两到四个月启动。实践者、实施项目架构师与实施项目经理需要扮演主题专家，至少在预算编制开始日期前两到四周评估实施团队完成所有工作包的能力。

除 EA 团队运行的第一年之外，多数后续架构工作都从阶段 H 启动。阶段 H 提供对价值实现的持续评审与对变革的监控；变革以及未能实现价值，都为 ADM 提供入口。绝不要迟到——预算规划开始前四周就已经太晚。EA 团队需要与组织的规划、预算、运营与变革流程对齐。图 14-1 给出时间线视图，描绘业务周期中关键决策与各目的架构的对齐关系。

〔图示：图 14-1：业务周期与按目的划分的架构；参阅本节结构化引用。〕



实践者的沟通一旦为预算规划提供信息并产生影响，前进路径就确定了。此后，这一上层架构治理并约束其余活动。

第二重要的活动是支持预算控制。架构项目的架构师是实施团队眼中利益相关者的代理，同时也是组合管理者用于验证已获进展与价值对应关系的主题专家。常见的情形是，实践者在预算控制阶段被角色的双重性绊住，从而失去对预算规划活动的专注。永远不要忘记，实践者的唯一目的是影响和引导变革，而不是深入实施细节。

EA 团队对每项工作都保持有意识，无论它叫什么名字——流程改进、运营、保持运行（KTLO）、增长、转型等。每项工作与想法都对目标架构有贡献。即使上层架构约束着支持组合与支持项目的架构，在资源（预算）分配之前，没有任何事项会被承诺并接受为下一个过渡状态。来自野外的随机想法（见 8.6 节）会设法进入流程。实践者要像鹰一样盯着，识别这类有趣的工作包，并触发对“新”组合的评审、权衡与治理。除非对组织的“行为”模式有足够洞察，否则很难把伪装成“自下而上”工作的“钟爱项目”和“随机想法”，与真正填补差距的合法举措区分开来。做一个简单的嗅探测试——这份架构规格是否试图同时完成不止一件事？再做利益相关者权衡——这些关注点是真的对齐了，还是因为没时间分析而被接受？创建变更请求，并留下线索，以便在下一周期重新审视并稳定架构。

要了解企业如何使用可自由支配资金，并明智地使用它们。一种务实做法是：引导把这类可自由支配资金用于探索性工作包，直到其与路线图的一致性得以合理化并纳入组合。接受这类请求，就是对目标架构的显式变更；应尽可能避免。遵循变更管理流程，没有例外。实践者在这一阶段扮演的角色，更像是运用架构知识的中介者与谈判者。归根结底，实践者对架构的稳定性与完整性负有责任与问责。

在资金分配最终确定时，良好的架构本身就会说话，实践者不必在场指导决策。分配发生时，决策者是在验证项目经理、组合管理者与实施架构师充分理解并同意按架构交付成果。决策者已经确信项目的必要性及其成果。如果实践者进入一个需要变更架构的场景，那就已经太晚了——基础有缺陷，架构项目与实施项目都无法继续。回到架构规格与利益相关者关注点；准备好面对工作不完整的后果。

如果实践者已经遵循本文档直到第 12 章的全部内容，那么本章提到的一切都应当显得陌生。否则，请从本文档重新开始。

相关结构化资料：[figure-14-1](../figures/G186/fig14-1.json)。本节核对图注及正文语义；图内标签、拓扑和甘特任务的完整审校状态以独立图记录为准，本轮正文覆盖不会自动升级。

### 语义规则与适用边界

- 阶段 H 持续审查价值和变化，为后续架构项目提供入口。
  - 适用边界：H 触发工作不取消 A 的启动范围确认；规划与编制时点不同。
  - PDF 136：Other than the first year of operation of the EA team, most subsequent architecture work is initiated from Phase H. Phase H provides ongoing review of value realization and monitoring of change. Change and failure to realize value provide entry points to the ADM. Never be late – four weeks before the start of budget planning is too late. The EA team needs to be aligned with the organization’s planning, budgeting, operational, and change processes. Figure 14-1 shows a timeline view, depicting an alignment of key decisions made during a business cycle and the purpose architectures.
- 预算分配前，下一过渡状态尚未获资源承诺，新增工作须审查、权衡与变更登记。
  - 适用边界：已编写路线图不等于执行资金已获批。
  - PDF 136：The EA team is intentional about every effort, irrespective of the name used – process improvement, operations, Keep-The-Lights-On (KTLO), growth, transformation, etc. Every effort and idea contributes to the Target Architecture. Even through the superior architecture constrains the Architecture to Support Portfolio and Project, nothing is committed and accepted as the next transition state until resources (budget) are allocated. Random ideas from the wild (see Section 8.6) will find their way into the process. The Practitioner watches like a hawk to identify such interesting work packages and triggers a review, trade-off, and governance of the “new” portfolio. Unless sufficient insight is gained about the “behavioral” patterns of the organization, it is difficult to discern “pet projects” and “random ideas” disguised as “bottom-up” effort from a legitimate initiative to bridge a gap. Perform a simple sniff test – is the architecture specification trying to accomplish more than one thing; stakeholder trade-off – are the concerns aligned or being accepted for lack of time to analyze. Create a change request and leave a bread crumb to revisit and stabilize the architecture in the next cycle.
- 探索性自由资金可支持未知事项，但接受该请求是目标架构变更，仍遵循变更流程。
  - 适用边界：实践者承担稳定性与完整性责任，不自行豁免。
  - PDF 137：Understand how the Enterprise employs discretionary funds; use them wisely. A practical approach would be guiding allocation of such discretionary funds for exploratory work packages, until the alignment to roadmap could be rationalized and included in the portfolio. Acceptance of such requests is an explicit change to the Target Architecture. Avoid as much as possible. Follow the change management processes. No exceptions. The role being played by the Practitioner at this stage is more of a mediator and negotiator, applying the architectural knowledge. At the end of the day, the Practitioner is responsible and accountable for the stability and integrity of the architecture.
